-- INICIO src/games/MM2/Blatant/Shoot.lua
local Module={
Name="MM2 Shoot",
Events={}::{[string]:any},
Initialized=false,
Runtime=nil::any,
}
local activeCleanup:()->()=function():()end
function Module.init(runtime:any):any
if Module.Initialized then
return Module
end
local core:any=state.mm2Core
assert(type(core)=="table","MM2 Shoot requires the MM2 core module")
local prediction:any=state.mm2Prediction
assert(type(prediction)=="table","MM2 Shoot requires MM2 Prediction")
local trajectoryCalibration:any=core.trajectoryCalibration
Module.Runtime=runtime
local connectGunFiredSignal:any=core.connectGunFiredSignal
local disconnectGunFiredObserver:any=core.disconnectGunFiredObserver
local findMurderer:any=core.findMurderer
local getGunOriginCFrame:any=core.getGunOriginCFrame
local getPlayerRole:any=core.getPlayerRole
local getPlayerWeapon:any=core.getPlayerWeapon
local getWeaponServiceModule:any=core.getWeaponServiceModule
local isPlayerAlive:any=core.isPlayerAlive
local isProtectedTarget:any=core.isProtectedTarget
local isWeaponPathClear:any=core.isWeaponPathClear
local describeRayHit:any=core.describeRayHit
local mm2Settings:any=core.mm2Settings
local validateGunShot:any=core.validateGunShot
local shootMacro:boolean=false
local lastGunOccluder:any=nil
local function macroUnequip(gun:Tool?):()
if not shootMacro or not gun then
return
end
task.delay(0.01,function():()
pcall(function():()
local backpack:Instance?=LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
local character:Model?=LocalPlayer.Character
if backpack and character and gun.Parent==character then
gun.Parent=backpack
end
end)
end)
end
local function getSelectedShootTarget():Player?
local target
if mm2Settings.shootMode=="Custom"
and mm2Settings.shootTarget~=""then
target=findPlayerByText(mm2Settings.shootTarget)
else
target=findMurderer()
end
if not target
or target==LocalPlayer
or not isPlayerAlive(target)
or isProtectedTarget(target)then
return nil
end
if getPlayerRole(target)~="Murderer"then
return nil
end
return target
end
local SILENT_ORIGIN_STUDS:number=2.5
local SILENT_PIERCE_STUDS:number=1.0
local SILENT_LIFT_STUDS:number=1.6
local MAX_SILENT_SWEEP:number=1.5
local POINT_BLANK_STUDS:number=6
local function silentLift(info:any,predicted:Vector3):number
if type(info)=="table"
and typeof(info.rootPosition)=="Vector3"then
local aimOffsetY:number=predicted.Y-info.rootPosition.Y
if aimOffsetY>0.5 then
return math.clamp(aimOffsetY+0.35,1.2,2.4)
end
end
return SILENT_LIFT_STUDS
end
local function getSilentSweepDistance(speed:number?):number
local configured:number=tonumber(mm2Settings.silentSweep)or 0
local sweep:number=math.clamp(configured,0,MAX_SILENT_SWEEP)
local uncertainty:number=0
if type(prediction)=="table"
and type(prediction.getLeadUncertainty)=="function"then
local ok:boolean,seconds:any=pcall(
prediction.getLeadUncertainty,
"CFrame"
)
if ok and type(seconds)=="number"and seconds==seconds then
uncertainty=math.clamp(seconds,0,0.15)
end
end
if uncertainty<=0 then
return sweep
end
local travel:number=math.max(tonumber(speed)or 0,0)
local extra:number=uncertainty*travel
return math.clamp(sweep+extra,0,MAX_SILENT_SWEEP)
end
local function buildSilentShot(
_target:Player,
info:any,
muzzleOrigin:CFrame?,
variant:string
):(CFrame?,CFrame?,number,number,string)
local predicted:Vector3=info.endpoint
local liftStuds:number=silentLift(info,predicted)
local velocity:Vector3=info.velocity
local horizontal:Vector3=Vector3.new(velocity.X,0,velocity.Z)
local direction:Vector3?=nil
local sweep:number=0
if horizontal.Magnitude>1.5 then
direction=horizontal.Unit
sweep=getSilentSweepDistance(horizontal.Magnitude)
end
local sweepStart:Vector3=
direction and-direction*sweep or Vector3.zero
local sweepEnd:Vector3=
direction and direction*sweep or Vector3.zero
local approach:Vector3?=nil
local approachOffset:number=SILENT_ORIGIN_STUDS
if muzzleOrigin then
local toTarget:Vector3=predicted-muzzleOrigin.Position
local flat:Vector3=Vector3.new(toTarget.X,0,toTarget.Z)
if flat.Magnitude>0.05 then
approach=flat.Unit
approachOffset=math.min(
SILENT_ORIGIN_STUDS,
math.max(1.0,flat.Magnitude*0.6)
)
end
end
local startPoint:Vector3
local endPoint:Vector3
local resolvedVariant:string=variant
local pierceAxis:Vector3?=direction or approach
if(variant=="Through"or variant==nil or variant=="")and pierceAxis then
resolvedVariant="Through"
local captured:Vector3=info.capturedPosition or predicted
startPoint=captured-pierceAxis*1.0+sweepStart
endPoint=predicted+pierceAxis*SILENT_PIERCE_STUDS+sweepEnd
elseif variant=="Top"then
resolvedVariant="Top"
startPoint=predicted+Vector3.new(0,liftStuds,0)
+sweepStart
endPoint=predicted+sweepEnd
elseif variant=="Behind"and approach then
resolvedVariant="Behind"
startPoint=predicted+approach*approachOffset+sweepStart
endPoint=predicted+sweepEnd
elseif approach then
resolvedVariant="Front"
startPoint=predicted-approach*approachOffset+sweepStart
endPoint=predicted+sweepEnd
elseif direction then
resolvedVariant="Motion"
startPoint=predicted+sweepStart
endPoint=predicted+sweepEnd
else
resolvedVariant="Top"
startPoint=predicted+Vector3.new(0,liftStuds,0)
endPoint=predicted
end
if(endPoint-startPoint).Magnitude<=0.05 then
startPoint=predicted+Vector3.new(0,liftStuds,0)
endPoint=predicted
resolvedVariant="Top"
sweep=0
end
return CFrame.lookAt(startPoint,endPoint),CFrame.new(endPoint),
approachOffset,sweep,resolvedVariant
end
type FireOptions={
silent:boolean?,
quiet:boolean?,
whenReady:boolean?,
continuous:boolean?,
wallCheck:boolean?,
redirect:((CFrame,CFrame)->())?,
nativeRedirect:boolean?,
nativeOrigin:CFrame?
}
local function chooseExperimentalGunMode(target:Player?,origin:Vector3?):string
local character:Model?=target and target.Character
local root:BasePart?=character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")::BasePart?
if not root or not origin then return"Bullet"end
local speed:number=0
pcall(function():()
local velocity:Vector3=root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
speed=Vector3.new(velocity.X,0,velocity.Z).Magnitude
end)
if(root.Position-origin).Magnitude>=30 and speed>=8 then return"CFrame"end
return"Bullet"
end
local SHOT_CONFIRM_WINDOW:number=1.5
local ShootFeature:any=nil
local lastRejectReason:string=""
local AIM_BODY_PARTS:{string}={
"UpperTorso","Torso","HumanoidRootPart","Head",
"RightUpperArm","LeftUpperArm","RightUpperLeg","LeftUpperLeg",
"Right Arm","Left Arm","Right Leg","Left Leg",
}
local function ghostOnLine(
muzzle:Vector3,
endpoint:Vector3,
targetCharacter:Model?
):any
if type(describeRayHit)~="function"then
return nil
end
local ok:boolean,hit:any=pcall(
describeRayHit,
muzzle,
endpoint,
targetCharacter
)
if not ok or type(hit)~="table"or hit.onTarget then
return nil
end
if hit.transparency~=1 or hit.canCollide~=false then
return nil
end
local reach:number=(endpoint-muzzle).Magnitude
if type(hit.distance)~="number"or hit.distance>=reach-0.5 then
return nil
end
return hit
end
local GHOST_PROBE_INTERVAL:number=0.15
local GHOST_PROBE_CANDIDATES:number=4
local ghostProbe:any={
target=nil,
at=-math.huge,
partName=nil,
}
local function avoidGhostAim(
target:Player,
info:any,
muzzle:Vector3
):(Vector3?,string?)
local character:Model?=target.Character
if not character or not info or typeof(info.endpoint)~="Vector3"then
return nil,nil
end
local now:number=os.clock()
if target~=ghostProbe.target
or now-ghostProbe.at>=GHOST_PROBE_INTERVAL then
ghostProbe.target=target
ghostProbe.at=now
ghostProbe.partName=nil
if ghostOnLine(muzzle,info.endpoint,character)then
local captured:Vector3?=info.capturedPosition
local probeLead:Vector3=captured
and(info.endpoint-captured)
or Vector3.zero
local tried:number=0
for _,partName:string in ipairs(AIM_BODY_PARTS)do
if tried>=GHOST_PROBE_CANDIDATES then
break
end
local part:Instance?=character:FindFirstChild(partName)
if part and part:IsA("BasePart")then
tried+=1
local candidate:Vector3=(part::BasePart).Position
+probeLead
if not ghostOnLine(muzzle,candidate,character)then
ghostProbe.partName=partName
break
end
end
end
end
end
local partName:string?=ghostProbe.partName
if not partName then
return nil,nil
end
local part:Instance?=character:FindFirstChild(partName)
if not part or not part:IsA("BasePart")then
ghostProbe.partName=nil
return nil,nil
end
local captured:Vector3?=info.capturedPosition
local lead:Vector3=captured and(info.endpoint-captured)or Vector3.zero
return(part::BasePart).Position+lead,partName
end
local function fireGunAtTarget(
target:Player?,
options:FireOptions?
):boolean
local opts:FireOptions=options or{}
local silent:boolean=mm2Settings.silentAim
local experimental:boolean=opts.silent==nil and mm2Settings.experimentalAim==true
if experimental then
local localCharacter:Model?=LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character
local ownRoot:BasePart?=localCharacter and localCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")::BasePart?
silent=chooseExperimentalGunMode(target,ownRoot and ownRoot.Position)=="CFrame"
elseif opts.silent~=nil then
silent=opts.silent
end
local quiet:boolean=opts.quiet==true
local continuous:boolean=opts.continuous==true
local nativeRedirect:boolean=opts.nativeRedirect==true and type(opts.redirect)=="function"
local useRaw:boolean=mm2Settings.shootRaw
or(continuous and silent)
local useWallCheck:boolean=if opts.wallCheck~=nil
then opts.wallCheck==true
else mm2Settings.shootWallCheck
local function reject(message:string):boolean
lastRejectReason=message
if not quiet and ShootFeature then ShootFeature:SetStatus(message)end
return false
end
local character,humanoid=getCharacterParts()
if not target or not target.Character or target==LocalPlayer
or not isPlayerAlive(target)or isProtectedTarget(target)or getPlayerRole(target)~="Murderer"then
return reject("No selected shoot target was found.")
end
if not character or not humanoid then
return reject("Your character is not available.")
end
local targetCharacter=target.Character
local gun:Tool?=getPlayerWeapon(LocalPlayer,"Gun",true)
if not gun or gun.Parent~=character then
return reject("Equip the sheriff gun yourself to shoot.")
end
if not nativeRedirect and(gun.Enabled==false or gun:GetAttribute("Disabled")==true
or gun:GetAttribute("Reloading")==true or gun:GetAttribute("IsReloading")==true)then
return reject("The gun is disabled or reloading.")
end
local remote:Instance?=gun and gun:FindFirstChild("Shoot")
local okOrigin:boolean,foundOrigin:any=pcall(getGunOriginCFrame,character,gun)
local origin:CFrame?=okOrigin and foundOrigin or nil
if not origin and nativeRedirect and typeof(opts.nativeOrigin)=="CFrame"then
origin=opts.nativeOrigin
end
local pendingShot:any=state.mm2ShotFeedback.pending
if not continuous and not nativeRedirect
and type(pendingShot)=="table"
and type(pendingShot.queuedAt)=="number"
and os.clock()-pendingShot.queuedAt<SHOT_CONFIRM_WINDOW then
local remaining:number=SHOT_CONFIRM_WINDOW
-(os.clock()-pendingShot.queuedAt)
return reject(string.format(
"Shot gate: waiting for the previous shot (%.1f s left).",
remaining))
end
local trackFeedback:boolean=not continuous
and not(nativeRedirect and type(pendingShot)=="table")
if not remote or not remote:IsA("RemoteEvent")or not origin then
return reject(not origin and"The gun has no usable origin."
or"The murderer is unavailable or obstructed.")
end
local info:any=nil
local reason:string?=nil
if origin then
local aimMode:string=silent and"CFrame"or"Bullet"
local ok:boolean,endpoint:any,aimInfo:any,aimReason:any=
pcall(prediction.gunAim,target,origin.Position,aimMode)
if not ok then return reject("Aim or visibility check failed.")end
if endpoint==nil then
reason=aimReason or"no samples"
else
info=aimInfo
end
end
if(useRaw or continuous or nativeRedirect or opts.whenReady==true)and not info and target.Character then
local targetRoot:BasePart?=target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if targetRoot then
local vel:Vector3=Vector3.zero
pcall(function():()
vel=targetRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity or Vector3.zero
end)
local fallbackEndpoint:Vector3=targetRoot.Position+vel*0.08
info={
endpoint=fallbackEndpoint,
velocity=vel,
leadSeconds=0.08,
motionSource="raw",
model="raw",
sampleAge=0,
samples=0,
capturedPosition=targetRoot.Position,
rtt=0,
echoDelay=0,
rttEngine="raw",
echoSamples=0,
turnRate=0,
brakeSeconds=0,
leadCorrection=0,
rootPosition=targetRoot.Position,
}
end
end
if info and origin and targetCharacter then
local cleaner:Vector3?,cleanerPart:string?=
avoidGhostAim(target,info,origin.Position)
if cleaner then
info.endpoint=cleaner
info.targetPosition=cleaner
info.ghostAvoided=cleanerPart
end
end
if origin and info then
local root:BasePart?=nil
pcall(function():()
root=targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")::BasePart?
end)
local aimVector:Vector3=info.endpoint-origin.Position
local toRoot:Vector3=root and(root.Position-origin.Position)or aimVector
local inverted:boolean=aimVector.Magnitude<0.05
or aimVector:Dot(toRoot)<=0
if root and(inverted or aimVector.Magnitude<POINT_BLANK_STUDS)then
info.capturedPosition=root.Position
info.endpoint=root.Position
info.aimPartChosen="HumanoidRootPart"
info.pointBlank=true
end
end
local rejectMessages:{[string]:string}={
["no body"]="The murderer has no usable body to aim at.",
["no samples"]="The murderer has no fresh motion samples yet.",
["stale sample"]="The murderer's position is stale; waiting for a fresh sample.",
["unreachable"]="The murderer is moving too fast to intercept.",
}
if not remote
or not remote:IsA("RemoteEvent")
or not origin
or not info then
local message:string=rejectMessages[reason or""]
or(
not origin
and"The gun has no usable origin."
or"The murderer is unavailable or obstructed."
)
return reject(message)
end
local function safeToSend(
shotOrigin:CFrame,
shotEnd:CFrame,
muzzle:CFrame?
):boolean
if gun.Parent~=character or(not nativeRedirect and(gun.Enabled==false
or gun:GetAttribute("Disabled")==true or gun:GetAttribute("Reloading")==true
or gun:GetAttribute("IsReloading")==true))then
return reject("The gun is disabled or reloading.")
end
if useRaw then
if not useWallCheck or not isWeaponPathClear then
return true
end
local rawMuzzle:Vector3=(muzzle or shotOrigin).Position
local rawClear,rawBlocker=isWeaponPathClear(
rawMuzzle,
shotEnd.Position,
targetCharacter,
nil,
{ignoreTarget=true}
)
if not rawClear then
if type(rawBlocker)=="table"and trajectoryCalibration
and type(trajectoryCalibration.noteBlockedShot)=="function"then
pcall(trajectoryCalibration.noteBlockedShot,
trajectoryCalibration,rawBlocker)
end
return reject("A wall or another body blocks the shot.")
end
return true
end
local muzzlePosition:Vector3=(muzzle or shotOrigin).Position
local ok,allowed,why,blocker,occluder=pcall(validateGunShot,target,character,targetCharacter,
muzzlePosition,shotOrigin.Position,shotEnd.Position,useWallCheck)
if not ok or not allowed then
if type(blocker)=="table"and trajectoryCalibration
and type(trajectoryCalibration.noteBlockedShot)=="function"then
pcall(trajectoryCalibration.noteBlockedShot,trajectoryCalibration,blocker)
end
return reject(why or"Shot safety check failed.")
end
if type(occluder)=="table"then
lastGunOccluder=occluder
else
lastGunOccluder=nil
end
return true
end
if silent then
local variant:string="Through"
local configuredSpawn:any=mm2Settings.silentSpawn
if configuredSpawn=="Top"or configuredSpawn=="Behind"
or configuredSpawn=="Front"then
variant=configuredSpawn
end
local shotOrigin:CFrame?,shotEnd:CFrame?,offset:number,
sweep:number,resolvedVariant:string
if info.pointBlank and origin then
shotOrigin=origin
shotEnd=CFrame.new(info.endpoint)
offset=0
sweep=0
resolvedVariant="PointBlank"
else
shotOrigin,shotEnd,offset,sweep,resolvedVariant=
buildSilentShot(target,info,origin,variant)
end
if not shotOrigin or not shotEnd then
if continuous and target.Character then
local tr:BasePart?=target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if tr then
local ep:Vector3=info.endpoint or tr.Position
local capPt:Vector3=info.capturedPosition or tr.Position
local startPt:Vector3=capPt+Vector3.new(0,1.2,-1.5)
shotOrigin=CFrame.lookAt(startPt,ep)
shotEnd=CFrame.new(ep)
offset=1.5
sweep=0
resolvedVariant="Through"
end
end
if not shotOrigin or not shotEnd then
return reject("Could not construct the silent shot.")
end
end
variant=resolvedVariant
if not useRaw
and not isWeaponPathClear(shotOrigin.Position,shotEnd.Position,targetCharacter,nil,{ignoreTarget=true})then
shotOrigin,shotEnd=origin,CFrame.new(info.endpoint)
variant="Muzzle"
offset=0
sweep=0
end
if not safeToSend(shotOrigin,shotEnd,origin)then return false end
if trackFeedback then
pcall(state.mm2ShotFeedback.queue,target,gun,shotOrigin,shotEnd)
end
state.lastSilentShotInfo={
variant=variant,
at=os.clock(),
offset=math.floor(offset*10)/10,
}
if opts.redirect then
opts.redirect(shotOrigin,shotEnd)
else
remote:FireServer(shotOrigin,shotEnd)
end
local function recordSilent():()
pcall(function():()
if not trajectoryCalibration or type(trajectoryCalibration.noteAuthoredShot)~="function"then return end
trajectoryCalibration:noteAuthoredShot({
kind="gun",
source=nativeRedirect and"native-redirect"or"menu",
tool=gun,
toolName=gun and gun.Name or"Gun",
originPos=shotOrigin.Position,
aimPos=shotEnd.Position,
target=target,
silent=true,
variant=variant,
offset=offset,
settings={mode="Silent",spawn=variant,
sweep=sweep,wallCheck=useWallCheck,
leadSeconds=info.leadSeconds,motionSource=info.motionSource,
model=info.model,
sampleAge=info.sampleAge,samples=info.samples,
referencePoint=info.capturedPosition and{info.capturedPosition.X,info.capturedPosition.Y,info.capturedPosition.Z},
predictedPoint={info.endpoint.X,info.endpoint.Y,info.endpoint.Z},
sampledVelocity={info.velocity.X,info.velocity.Y,info.velocity.Z},
rtt=info.rtt,echoDelay=info.echoDelay,
rttEngine=info.rttEngine,rttData=info.rttData,
occluder=lastGunOccluder,
echoSamples=info.echoSamples,
turnRate=info.turnRate,brakeSeconds=info.brakeSeconds,
leadCorrection=info.leadCorrection,
leadCorrectionTarget=info.leadCorrectionTarget,
leadSamples=info.leadSamples,aimMode=info.aimMode,
ghostAvoided=info.ghostAvoided},
})
end)
end
if nativeRedirect and task and type(task.defer)=="function"then
task.defer(recordSilent)
else
recordSilent()
end
if not quiet and ShootFeature then
ShootFeature:SetStatus(experimental and"Experimental · CFrame · request sent"or nil)
end
if not continuous then
macroUnequip(gun)
end
return true
end
local shotEndCFrame:CFrame=CFrame.new(info.endpoint)
if not safeToSend(origin,shotEndCFrame)then return false end
if trackFeedback then
pcall(state.mm2ShotFeedback.queue,target,gun,origin,shotEndCFrame)
end
local shotOriginCFrame:CFrame=CFrame.lookAt(origin.Position,info.endpoint)
if opts.redirect then
opts.redirect(shotOriginCFrame,shotEndCFrame)
else
remote:FireServer(shotOriginCFrame,shotEndCFrame)
end
local function recordBullet():()
pcall(function():()
if not trajectoryCalibration or type(trajectoryCalibration.noteAuthoredShot)~="function"then return end
trajectoryCalibration:noteAuthoredShot({
kind="gun",
source=nativeRedirect and"native-redirect"or"menu",
tool=gun,
toolName=gun and gun.Name or"Gun",
originPos=origin.Position,
aimPos=info.endpoint,
target=target,
silent=false,
settings={mode="Normal",wallCheck=useWallCheck,
leadSeconds=info.leadSeconds,motionSource=info.motionSource,
model=info.model,
sampleAge=info.sampleAge,samples=info.samples,
referencePoint=info.capturedPosition and{info.capturedPosition.X,info.capturedPosition.Y,info.capturedPosition.Z},
predictedPoint={info.endpoint.X,info.endpoint.Y,info.endpoint.Z},
sampledVelocity={info.velocity.X,info.velocity.Y,info.velocity.Z},
rtt=info.rtt,echoDelay=info.echoDelay,
rttEngine=info.rttEngine,rttData=info.rttData,
occluder=lastGunOccluder,
echoSamples=info.echoSamples,
turnRate=info.turnRate,brakeSeconds=info.brakeSeconds,
leadCorrection=info.leadCorrection,
leadCorrectionTarget=info.leadCorrectionTarget,
leadSamples=info.leadSamples,aimMode=info.aimMode,
ghostAvoided=info.ghostAvoided},
})
end)
end
if nativeRedirect and task and type(task.defer)=="function"then
task.defer(recordBullet)
else
recordBullet()
end
if not quiet and ShootFeature then
ShootFeature:SetStatus(experimental and"Experimental · Bullet · request sent"or nil)
end
if not continuous then
macroUnequip(gun)
end
return true
end
local shootOptionsReady:boolean=false
local shootFeatureActive:boolean=false
local autoShootActive:boolean=false
local autoShootWanted:boolean=false
local transientShotActive:boolean=false
local transientShotGeneration:number=0
local updateAutoShootConnection:(()->())?=nil
local observerGeneration:number=0
local cachedShootRemote:Instance?=nil
local lastCacheProbeAt:number=-math.huge
local function refreshShootRemoteCache():()
local remote:Instance?=nil
local okGun:boolean,foundGun:any=pcall(getPlayerWeapon,LocalPlayer,"Gun",true)
local gun:Tool?=if okGun then foundGun else nil
if gun then
pcall(function():()
local found:Instance?=gun:FindFirstChild("Shoot")
if found and found:IsA("RemoteEvent")then
remote=found
end
end)
end
cachedShootRemote=remote
end
local function refreshShootInfrastructure():()
observerGeneration+=1
local generation:number=observerGeneration
local wantActive:boolean=shootFeatureActive or autoShootActive or transientShotActive
state.mm2ShotFeedback.shootActive=wantActive
if wantActive then
local function observe():()
local weaponService:any=getWeaponServiceModule()
if generation~=observerGeneration or not state.mm2ShotFeedback.shootActive then return end
if type(weaponService)=="table"then connectGunFiredSignal(weaponService)end
end
if state.isMobile then
observe()
else
task.spawn(observe)
end
refreshShootRemoteCache()
return
end
disconnectGunFiredObserver()
state.mm2ShotFeedback.pending=nil
state.mm2ShotFeedback.lastAccepted=nil
state.mm2ShotFeedback.hide()
refreshShootRemoteCache()
end
local shotHookInstalled:boolean=false
local shotHookWrapper:any=nil
local originalNamecall:any=nil
local function installNativeGunHook():boolean
if state.isMobile then return true end
if shotHookInstalled then return true end
local hook:any=state.hookMetamethod
local getMethod:any=state.getNamecallMethod
local checkCaller:any=state.checkCaller
if type(hook)~="function"or type(getMethod)~="function"
or type(checkCaller)~="function"then
return false
end
if type(task)~="table"or type(task.defer)~="function"then
return false
end
local predecessor:any=nil
local pendingRedirects:number=0
local MAX_PENDING_REDIRECTS:number=2
local sendingRedirect:boolean=false
local function sendAsExecutor(remote:any,arguments:any):boolean
local previous:boolean=sendingRedirect
sendingRedirect=true
local ok:boolean,failure:any=pcall(function():()
remote:FireServer(table.unpack(arguments,1,arguments.n))
end)
sendingRedirect=previous
if not ok then
warn("[Wurst] native gun redirect failed: "..tostring(failure))
end
return ok
end
local function redirectDeferred(remote:any,arguments:any):()
if not shootFeatureActive or not shootOptionsReady then
sendAsExecutor(remote,arguments)
return
end
local shotOrigin:CFrame?=nil
local shotEnd:CFrame?=nil
local ok:boolean=pcall(function():any
lastRejectReason=""
return fireGunAtTarget(getSelectedShootTarget(),{
quiet=true,
nativeRedirect=true,
nativeOrigin=arguments[1],
redirect=function(origin:CFrame,endpoint:CFrame):()
shotOrigin,shotEnd=origin,endpoint
end,
})
end)
if shotOrigin and shotEnd then
if ShootFeature then
pcall(function():()ShootFeature:SetStatus("Native click redirected")end)
end
local redirected:any=table.pack(shotOrigin,shotEnd)
local copied:number=2
for index:number=3,arguments.n do
copied+=1
redirected[copied]=arguments[index]
end
redirected.n=copied
sendAsExecutor(remote,redirected)
return
end
if ShootFeature then
pcall(function():()
ShootFeature:SetStatus(if not ok then"Native redirect failed; original click kept"
else(lastRejectReason~=""and lastRejectReason or"Native click unchanged"))
end)
end
sendAsExecutor(remote,arguments)
end
local function redirectNamecall(remote:any,...:any):any
if sendingRedirect then
return predecessor(remote,...)
end
if not shootFeatureActive or not shootOptionsReady then
return predecessor(remote,...)
end
if remote~=cachedShootRemote then
local now:number=os.clock()
if now-lastCacheProbeAt>=0.5 then
lastCacheProbeAt=now
task.defer(refreshShootRemoteCache)
end
return predecessor(remote,...)
end
local okMethod:boolean,method:any=pcall(getMethod)
if not okMethod or method~="FireServer"then
return predecessor(remote,...)
end
local okCaller:boolean,fromExecutor:any=pcall(checkCaller)
if not okCaller or fromExecutor~=false then
return predecessor(remote,...)
end
local arguments:any=table.pack(...)
if typeof(arguments[1])~="CFrame"or typeof(arguments[2])~="CFrame"then
return predecessor(remote,...)
end
if pendingRedirects>=MAX_PENDING_REDIRECTS then
return predecessor(remote,...)
end
pendingRedirects+=1
task.defer(function():()
local handled:boolean,failure:any=pcall(redirectDeferred,remote,arguments)
pendingRedirects-=1
if not handled then
warn("[Wurst] native gun redirect error: "..tostring(failure))
end
end)
return nil
end
local callback:any=redirectNamecall
if type(state.newCClosure)=="function"then
local ok:boolean,wrapped:any=pcall(state.newCClosure,callback)
if ok and type(wrapped)=="function"then callback=wrapped end
end
local ok:boolean,old:any=pcall(hook,game,"__namecall",callback)
if not ok or type(old)~="function"then return false end
predecessor=old
originalNamecall,shotHookWrapper,shotHookInstalled=old,callback,true
return true
end
local function uninstallNativeGunHook():()
if not shotHookInstalled or type(state.hookMetamethod)~="function"then return end
local ok:boolean,displaced:any=pcall(state.hookMetamethod,game,"__namecall",originalNamecall)
if not ok then return end
if displaced==shotHookWrapper then
shotHookInstalled,shotHookWrapper,originalNamecall=false,nil,nil
elseif type(displaced)=="function"then
pcall(state.hookMetamethod,game,"__namecall",displaced)
end
end
local manualShotPending:boolean=false
local function triggerManualShot():()
if state.isMobile and not shootFeatureActive then
notify("Enable Gun Aimbot first.")
return
end
if manualShotPending then return end
local character,humanoid=getCharacterParts()
local gun:Tool?=getPlayerWeapon(LocalPlayer,"Gun")
if not character or not humanoid or not gun then
notify("No sheriff gun is available.")
return
end
manualShotPending=true
local ok:boolean,message:any=pcall(function():()
if gun.Parent~=character then
humanoid:EquipTool(gun)
RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
end
if(not state.isMobile or shootFeatureActive)
and LocalPlayer.Character==character and gun.Parent==character then
if not shootFeatureActive and not state.isMobile then
transientShotGeneration+=1
local generation:number=transientShotGeneration
transientShotActive=true
refreshShootInfrastructure()
task.delay(2,function():()
if generation~=transientShotGeneration then return end
transientShotActive=false
refreshShootInfrastructure()
end)
end
fireGunAtTarget(getSelectedShootTarget(),{whenReady=true})
end
end)
manualShotPending=false
if not ok then notify("Shoot failed: "..tostring(message))end
end
local function toggleShootMurderer(enabled:boolean):()
shootFeatureActive=enabled
state.mm2SilentAimEnabled=enabled
if state.mm2KnifeThrowRefresh then state.mm2KnifeThrowRefresh()end
autoShootActive=shootFeatureActive and autoShootWanted
if enabled and not state.isMobile then
if not installNativeGunHook()then
notify("Native click redirect needs executor hookmetamethod, getnamecallmethod and checkcaller; the keybind can still fire.")
end
elseif not enabled then
uninstallNativeGunHook()
end
refreshShootInfrastructure()
if updateAutoShootConnection then updateAutoShootConnection()end
end
local lastAutoStatus:string?=nil
local function autoStatus(message:string):()
if ShootFeature and lastAutoStatus~=message then
lastAutoStatus=message
ShootFeature:SetStatus(message)
end
end
local function autoShootTick():()
if not shootFeatureActive or not autoShootActive then return end
local gun:Tool?=getPlayerWeapon(LocalPlayer,"Gun",true)
if not gun or gun.Parent~=LocalPlayer.Character then
autoStatus("Equip gun")
return
end
if state and state.mm2ShotFeedback
and state.mm2ShotFeedback.pending then
autoStatus("Esperando confirmación")
return
end
local target:Player?=getSelectedShootTarget()
if not target then autoStatus("No target");return end
lastRejectReason=""
local sent:boolean=fireGunAtTarget(target,{whenReady=true,quiet=true})
autoStatus(sent and"Auto: request sent"or lastRejectReason)
end
updateAutoShootConnection=function():()
disconnectFeatureConnection("MM2AutoShoot")
autoShootActive=shootOptionsReady and shootFeatureActive and autoShootWanted
if not autoShootActive then
lastAutoStatus=nil
if ShootFeature then ShootFeature:SetStatus(nil)end
return
end
featureConnections.MM2AutoShoot=
TaskManager:Connect(autoShootTick,nil,"MM2 Auto Shoot")
autoShootTick()
end
local function toggleAutoShoot(enabled:boolean):()
autoShootWanted=enabled
autoShootActive=shootFeatureActive and autoShootWanted
refreshShootInfrastructure()
if updateAutoShootConnection then updateAutoShootConnection()end
end
if type(configData)=="table"then
local changed:boolean=false
if configData.states["MM2.Shoot.AutoShoot"]==nil and configData.states["MM2.AutoShoot"]~=nil then
local enabled:boolean=configData.states["MM2.AutoShoot"]==true
configData.states["MM2.Shoot.AutoShoot"]=enabled
if enabled then configData.states["MM2.Shoot"]=true end
changed=true
end
local oldKey:any=configData.values["Shortcut.MM2AutoShoot"]
if configData.values["Shortcut.MM2ShootAutoShoot"]==nil and type(oldKey)=="string"then
configData.values["Shortcut.MM2ShootAutoShoot"]=oldKey
changed=true
end
if changed and type(queueConfigSave)=="function"then queueConfigSave()end
end
if type(configData)=="table"and configData.values["MM2.Shoot.Aimmethod"]==nil then
configData.values["MM2.Shoot.Aimmethod"]=configData.values["MM2.Shoot.Mode"]
end
ShootFeature=createUniversalFeature(
"Gun Aimbot",
state.isMobile
and"Gun Aimbot with Normal(Bullet), Silent(Cframe), and Experimental modes. The assigned action fires/equips Gun; WallCheck is configurable per mode."
or"Redirect native clicks with Bullet or Silent(CFrame). One optional keybind fires even while the module is off.",
4,
toggleShootMurderer,
{
categoryName="Exploits",
configKey="MM2.Shoot",
parent=MM2Scroll,
registry=mm2Features,
}
)
state.mm2SilentAimFeature=ShootFeature
state.mm2ChooseExperimentalAim=chooseExperimentalGunMode
state.mm2FireSilentShot=function(target:Player?,shotOpts:any):boolean
local opts:any=type(shotOpts)=="table"and shotOpts or{}
return fireGunAtTarget(target,{
silent=true,
quiet=opts.quiet~=false,
continuous=opts.continuous~=false,
wallCheck=opts.wallCheck,
})
end
state.mm2FireGunShot=function(target:Player?,shotOpts:any):boolean
local opts:any=type(shotOpts)=="table"and shotOpts or{}
return fireGunAtTarget(target,{
silent=opts.silent==true,
wallCheck=opts.wallCheck,
quiet=opts.quiet~=false,
continuous=opts.continuous~=false,
})
end
if addSectionOption then addSectionOption(ShootFeature,"Gun")end
local states:any=type(configData)=="table"and configData.states or{}
local values:any=type(configData)=="table"and configData.values or{}
local function modeKey(value:any):string
if value=="Experimental"then return"Experimental"end
if value=="CFrame"or value=="Silent(Cframe)"or value=="Silent(CFrame)"then return"CFrame"end
return"Bullet"
end
local savedMode:any=values["MM2.Shoot.Aimmethod"]
local initialMode:string=modeKey(savedMode
or(mm2Settings.experimentalAim and"Experimental"or(mm2Settings.silentAim and"CFrame"or"Bullet")))
if state.isMobile then
if savedMode=="Bullet"or savedMode=="CFrame"or savedMode=="Silent(CFrame)"then
values["MM2.Shoot.Aimmethod"]=(savedMode=="CFrame"or savedMode=="Silent(CFrame)")
and"Silent(Cframe)"or"Normal(Bullet)"
if type(queueConfigSave)=="function"then queueConfigSave()end
end
else
if initialMode=="Experimental"then initialMode="Bullet"end
local normalized:string=initialMode=="CFrame"and"Silent(CFrame)"or"Bullet"
if values["MM2.Shoot.Aimmethod"]~=normalized then
values["MM2.Shoot.Aimmethod"]=normalized
if type(queueConfigSave)=="function"then queueConfigSave()end
end
end
local function modeConfig(mode:string):any
local prefix:string="MM2.Shoot."..mode.."."
local legacy:boolean=mode==initialMode
local wall:any=states[prefix.."WallCheck"]
local macro:any=states[prefix.."Macro"]
local targetMode:any=values[prefix.."Target"]
local targetName:any=values[prefix.."TargetPlayer"]
if legacy then
if wall==nil then wall=states["MM2.Shoot.WallCheck"]end
if macro==nil then macro=states["MM2.Shoot.Macro"]end
if targetMode==nil then targetMode=values["MM2.Shoot.Target"]end
if targetName==nil then targetName=values["MM2.Shoot.Targetplayer"]end
end
return{wallCheck=wall==nil or wall==true,macro=macro==true,
targetMode=targetMode=="Custom"and"Custom"or"Murder",
targetName=type(targetName)=="string"and targetName or""}
end
local modeConfigs:any={Bullet=modeConfig("Bullet"),CFrame=modeConfig("CFrame"),
Experimental=modeConfig("Experimental")}
local activeModeKey:string=initialMode
local switching:boolean=false
local optionsReady:boolean=false
local ShootTargetBox
local function refreshShootOptions():()
if ShootTargetBox then
setOptionVisible(ShootFeature,ShootTargetBox.Parent::GuiObject,mm2Settings.shootMode=="Custom")
end
end
local function syncMode():()
local cfg:any=modeConfigs[activeModeKey]or modeConfigs.Bullet
switching=true
mm2Settings.shootWallCheck=cfg.wallCheck
shootMacro,mm2Settings.shootMode,mm2Settings.shootTarget=cfg.macro,cfg.targetMode,cfg.targetName
if state and type(state.toggleControls)=="table"then
for _,item:any in ipairs({{"WallCheck",cfg.wallCheck},{"Macro",cfg.macro}})do
local control:any=state.toggleControls["MM2.Shoot."..item[1] ]
if control and control.setChecked then pcall(control.setChecked,item[2])end
end
end
if state and type(state.valueControls)=="table"then
local target:any=state.valueControls["MM2.Shoot.Target"]
local name:any=state.valueControls["MM2.Shoot.Targetplayer"]
if target and target.apply then pcall(target.apply,cfg.targetMode)end
if name and name.apply then pcall(name.apply,cfg.targetName)end
end
switching=false
refreshShootOptions()
end
local aimMethods:{string}=state.isMobile
and{"Normal(Bullet)","Silent(Cframe)","Experimental"}
or{"Bullet","Silent(CFrame)"}
addCycleOption(ShootFeature,"Aim method",aimMethods,
initialMode=="Experimental"and 3 or(initialMode=="CFrame"and 2 or 1),
function(value:string):()
activeModeKey=modeKey(value)
mm2Settings.experimentalAim=activeModeKey=="Experimental"
mm2Settings.silentAim=activeModeKey=="CFrame"
syncMode()
end,
state.isMobile
and"Experimental selects one route based on movement and range; hit-rate improvement is unverified. Options are saved per mode."
or"One keybind is shared; WallCheck and target settings are saved per mode.")
addCycleOption(ShootFeature,"Target",{"Murder","Custom"},1,function(value:string):()
mm2Settings.shootMode=value
if optionsReady and not switching then
modeConfigs[activeModeKey].targetMode=value
values["MM2.Shoot."..activeModeKey..".Target"]=value
end
refreshShootOptions()
end)
if state.bindFeatureActivationKey then
state.bindFeatureActivationKey(ShootFeature,mm2Settings.shootKey,triggerManualShot)
end
if not state.isMobile then
local binding:any=state.shortcutBindings and state.shortcutBindings[ShootFeature.configKey]
if binding then binding.feedback=nil end
if type(state.addFeatureShortcutOption)=="function"then
state.addFeatureShortcutOption(ShootFeature,"Keybind",
"Optional: click the box and press a key. It fires in either mode, even when Gun Aimbot is off.")
end
end
addToggleOption(ShootFeature,"WallCheck",true,function(value:boolean):()
mm2Settings.shootWallCheck=value==true
if optionsReady and not switching then
modeConfigs[activeModeKey].wallCheck=value==true
states["MM2.Shoot."..activeModeKey..".WallCheck"]=value==true
end
end,"Check for walls; saved independently per mode.")
addToggleOption(ShootFeature,"Macro",false,function(value:boolean):()
shootMacro=value==true
if optionsReady and not switching then
modeConfigs[activeModeKey].macro=value==true
states["MM2.Shoot."..activeModeKey..".Macro"]=value==true
end
end,"Pistol macro: hides the gun after the shot; saved per mode.")
ShootTargetBox=addTextOption(ShootFeature,"Target player","",function(value:string):()
mm2Settings.shootTarget=value
if optionsReady and not switching then
modeConfigs[activeModeKey].targetName=value
values["MM2.Shoot."..activeModeKey..".TargetPlayer"]=value
if type(queueConfigSave)=="function"then queueConfigSave()end
end
end,false)
optionsReady=true
syncMode()
shootOptionsReady=true
if shootFeatureActive and not state.isMobile and not shotHookInstalled then
if not installNativeGunHook()then
notify("Native click redirect needs executor hookmetamethod, getnamecallmethod and checkcaller; the keybind can still fire.")
end
end
if updateAutoShootConnection then updateAutoShootConnection()end
activeCleanup=function():()
transientShotGeneration+=1
transientShotActive=false
toggleShootMurderer(false)
state.mm2SilentAimFeature=nil
state.mm2FireSilentShot=nil
state.mm2FireGunShot=nil
state.mm2ChooseExperimentalAim=nil
toggleAutoShoot(false)
state.mm2ShotFeedback.hide()
end
Module.Events=featureConnections
Module.Initialized=true
return Module
end
function Module.destroy():()
if not Module.Initialized then
return
end
Module.Initialized=false
pcall(activeCleanup)
activeCleanup=function():()end
Module.Events={}
Module.Runtime=nil
end
return Module
-- FIN src/games/MM2/Blatant/Shoot.lua

-- INICIO src/games/MM2/Combat/AutoShoot.lua
local Module={
Name="MM2 Auto Shoot",
Events={}::{[string]:any},
Initialized=false,
Runtime=nil::any,
}
local activeCleanup:()->()=function():()end
function Module.init(runtime:any):any
if Module.Initialized then
return Module
end
local core:any=state.mm2Core
assert(type(core)=="table","MM2 Auto Shoot requires the MM2 core module")
local prediction:any=state.mm2Prediction
assert(type(prediction)=="table","MM2 Auto Shoot requires MM2 Prediction")
Module.Runtime=runtime
local findMurderer:any=core.findMurderer
local getPlayerRole:any=core.getPlayerRole
local getPlayerWeapon:any=core.getPlayerWeapon
local isPlayerAlive:any=core.isPlayerAlive
local isProtectedTarget:any=core.isProtectedTarget
local isWeaponPathClear:any=core.isWeaponPathClear
local getGunOriginCFrame:any=core.getGunOriginCFrame
local settings={
mode="Bullet",
wallCheck=true,
proximity=false,
aura=25,
}
local autoShootActive:boolean=false
local AutoShootFeature:any=nil
local toolConnections:{RBXScriptConnection}={}
local lastShotSentAt:number=0
local AUTO_SHOT_PACE_SECONDS:number=0.22
local function clearToolConnections():()
for _,conn:RBXScriptConnection in ipairs(toolConnections)do
pcall(function():()
conn:Disconnect()
end)
end
table.clear(toolConnections)
end
local function findLocalGun():(Tool?,Model?,Humanoid?,BasePart?)
local character:Model?=LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character
if not character then
return nil,nil,nil,nil
end
local humanoid:Humanoid?=character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
local root:BasePart?=character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")::BasePart?
if not humanoid or humanoid.Health<=0 or not root then
return nil,character,humanoid,root
end
local gun:Tool?=nil
if type(getPlayerWeapon)=="function"then
gun=getPlayerWeapon(LocalPlayer,"Gun",false)
end
if not gun then
local charGun:Instance?=character:FindFirstChild("Gun")
if charGun and charGun:IsA("Tool")then
gun=charGun
end
end
if not gun then
local backpack:Instance?=LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
local bpGun:Instance?=backpack and backpack:FindFirstChild("Gun")
if bpGun and bpGun:IsA("Tool")then
gun=bpGun
end
end
return gun,character,humanoid,root
end
local function ensureGunEquipped(
character:Model,
humanoid:Humanoid,
gun:Tool
):boolean
if gun.Parent==character then
return true
end
pcall(function():()
humanoid:EquipTool(gun)
end)
if gun.Parent~=character then
pcall(function():()
gun.Parent=character
end)
end
return gun.Parent==character
end
local function resolveMurdererTarget(
localRoot:BasePart,
enforceProximity:boolean
):(Player?,BasePart?)
local murderer:Player?=nil
if type(findMurderer)=="function"then
murderer=findMurderer()
end
if not murderer then
local playersService:Players=game:GetService("Players")
for _,player:Player in ipairs(playersService:GetPlayers())do
if player~=LocalPlayer then
local role:any=type(getPlayerRole)=="function"
and getPlayerRole(player)
or nil
local hasKnife:boolean=type(getPlayerWeapon)=="function"
and getPlayerWeapon(player,"Knife",false)~=nil
if role=="Murderer"or hasKnife then
murderer=player
break
end
end
end
end
if not murderer or murderer==LocalPlayer then
return nil,nil
end
if type(isPlayerAlive)=="function"and not isPlayerAlive(murderer)then
return nil,nil
end
if type(isProtectedTarget)=="function"and isProtectedTarget(murderer)then
return nil,nil
end
local targetChar:Model?=murderer.Character
local targetRoot:BasePart?=targetChar
and targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")::BasePart?
if not targetRoot then
return nil,nil
end
if enforceProximity then
local okDist:boolean,distance:any=pcall(function():any
return(targetRoot.Position-localRoot.Position).Magnitude
end)
if okDist and type(distance)=="number"and distance>settings.aura then
return nil,nil
end
end
return murderer,targetRoot
end
local function computeTargetEndpoint(
target:Player,
targetRoot:BasePart,
originPos:Vector3,
aimMode:string?
):Vector3
if type(prediction)=="table"and type(prediction.gunAim)=="function"then
local ok:boolean,endpoint:any=pcall(
prediction.gunAim,
target,
originPos,
aimMode
)
if ok and typeof(endpoint)=="Vector3"then
return endpoint
end
end
local vel:Vector3=Vector3.zero
pcall(function():()
vel=targetRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity
end)
return targetRoot.Position+vel*0.08
end
local function fireCFrameShotImmediate(
target:Player,
targetRoot:BasePart,
character:Model,
localRoot:BasePart,
gun:Tool
):boolean
local shootRemote:Instance?=gun:FindFirstChild("Shoot")
if not(shootRemote and shootRemote:IsA("RemoteEvent"))then
return false
end
if gun.Enabled==false
or gun:GetAttribute("Disabled")==true
or gun:GetAttribute("Reloading")==true
or gun:GetAttribute("IsReloading")==true then
return false
end
local originCF:CFrame=(type(getGunOriginCFrame)=="function"
and getGunOriginCFrame(character,gun))
or localRoot.CFrame
local endpoint:Vector3=computeTargetEndpoint(
target,
targetRoot,
originCF.Position,
"CFrame"
)
local capturedPos:Vector3=targetRoot.Position
local chordFlat:Vector3=Vector3.new(
endpoint.X-capturedPos.X,
0,
endpoint.Z-capturedPos.Z
)
local pierceAxis:Vector3
if chordFlat.Magnitude>0.15 then
pierceAxis=chordFlat.Unit
else
local toTargetFlat:Vector3=Vector3.new(
endpoint.X-originCF.Position.X,
0,
endpoint.Z-originCF.Position.Z
)
if toTargetFlat.Magnitude>0.05 then
pierceAxis=toTargetFlat.Unit
else
pierceAxis=Vector3.new(0,0,-1)
end
end
local startPoint:Vector3=capturedPos-pierceAxis*1.5
local endPoint:Vector3=endpoint+pierceAxis*1.5
if(endPoint-startPoint).Magnitude<0.2 then
startPoint=endpoint+Vector3.new(0,2.0,-1.5)
endPoint=endpoint
end
local shotOrigin:CFrame=CFrame.lookAt(startPoint,endPoint)
local shotEnd:CFrame=CFrame.new(endPoint)
local firedSilent:boolean=false
if type(state.mm2FireSilentShot)=="function"then
local okSilent:boolean,res:any=pcall(state.mm2FireSilentShot,target,{
continuous=true,
quiet=true,
wallCheck=settings.wallCheck,
})
firedSilent=okSilent and res==true
if not okSilent then lastShotSentAt=os.clock()end
if not firedSilent then return false end
end
if not firedSilent then
local clear:boolean=false
if settings.wallCheck and type(isWeaponPathClear)=="function"then
local okClear:boolean,allowed:any=pcall(
isWeaponPathClear,
originCF.Position,
endpoint,
target.Character,
nil,
{ignoreTarget=true}
)
clear=okClear and allowed==true
else
clear=true
end
if clear then
local okFire:boolean=pcall(function():()
(shootRemote::RemoteEvent):FireServer(shotOrigin,shotEnd)
end)
if not okFire then
lastShotSentAt=os.clock()
return false
end
else
lastShotSentAt=0
return false
end
end
lastShotSentAt=os.clock()
return true
end
local function fireBulletShotImmediate(
target:Player,
targetRoot:BasePart,
character:Model,
localRoot:BasePart,
gun:Tool
):boolean
local shootRemote:Instance?=gun:FindFirstChild("Shoot")
if not(shootRemote and shootRemote:IsA("RemoteEvent"))then
return false
end
if gun.Enabled==false
or gun:GetAttribute("Disabled")==true
or gun:GetAttribute("Reloading")==true
or gun:GetAttribute("IsReloading")==true then
return false
end
local originCF:CFrame=(type(getGunOriginCFrame)=="function"
and getGunOriginCFrame(character,gun))
or localRoot.CFrame
local endpoint:Vector3=computeTargetEndpoint(
target,
targetRoot,
originCF.Position,
"Bullet"
)
if settings.wallCheck and type(isWeaponPathClear)=="function"then
local okClear:boolean,clear:any=pcall(
isWeaponPathClear,
originCF.Position,
endpoint,
target.Character
)
if not okClear or clear~=true then
return false
end
end
local firedCore:boolean=false
if type(state.mm2FireGunShot)=="function"then
local okShot:boolean,res:any=pcall(state.mm2FireGunShot,target,{
silent=false,
wallCheck=settings.wallCheck,
continuous=true,
quiet=true,
})
firedCore=okShot and res==true
if not okShot then lastShotSentAt=os.clock()end
if not firedCore then return false end
end
if not firedCore then
local shotOrigin:CFrame=CFrame.lookAt(originCF.Position,endpoint)
local shotEnd:CFrame=CFrame.new(endpoint)
local okFire:boolean=pcall(function():()
(shootRemote::RemoteEvent):FireServer(shotOrigin,shotEnd)
end)
if not okFire then
lastShotSentAt=os.clock()
return false
end
end
lastShotSentAt=os.clock()
return true
end
local function executeAutoShootCFrameOnce(enforceProximity:boolean):boolean
local gun:Tool?,character:Model?,humanoid:Humanoid?,
localRoot:BasePart?=findLocalGun()
if not gun or not character or not humanoid or not localRoot then
lastShotSentAt=0
return false
end
local target:Player?,targetRoot:BasePart?=
resolveMurdererTarget(localRoot,enforceProximity)
if not target or not targetRoot then
return false
end
if not ensureGunEquipped(character,humanoid,gun)then
return false
end
if os.clock()-lastShotSentAt<AUTO_SHOT_PACE_SECONDS then
return true
end
return fireCFrameShotImmediate(target,targetRoot,character,localRoot,gun)
end
state.mm2AutoShootCFrameOnce=executeAutoShootCFrameOnce
local function autoShootTick():()
if not autoShootActive then
return
end
local gun:Tool?,character:Model?,humanoid:Humanoid?,
localRoot:BasePart?=findLocalGun()
if not gun or not character or not humanoid or not localRoot then
lastShotSentAt=0
return
end
local target:Player?,targetRoot:BasePart?=
resolveMurdererTarget(localRoot,settings.proximity==true)
if not target or not targetRoot then
return
end
if not ensureGunEquipped(character,humanoid,gun)then
return
end
if os.clock()-lastShotSentAt<AUTO_SHOT_PACE_SECONDS then
return
end
local isCFrame:boolean=settings.mode=="CFrame"
or settings.mode=="Cframe"
or settings.mode=="Silent AIM"
if settings.mode=="Experimental"then
local mode:string="Bullet"
if type(state.mm2ChooseExperimentalAim)=="function"then
local origin:CFrame=(type(getGunOriginCFrame)=="function"
and getGunOriginCFrame(character,gun))or localRoot.CFrame
local okMode:boolean,chosen:any=pcall(state.mm2ChooseExperimentalAim,target,origin.Position)
if okMode and chosen=="CFrame"then mode="CFrame"end
end
local sent:boolean=false
local uncertain:boolean=false
if type(state.mm2FireGunShot)=="function"then
local ok:boolean,result:any=pcall(state.mm2FireGunShot,target,{
silent=mode=="CFrame",wallCheck=settings.wallCheck,
continuous=true,quiet=true,
})
sent=ok and result==true
uncertain=not ok
if uncertain then lastShotSentAt=os.clock()end
end
if sent then lastShotSentAt=os.clock()end
if AutoShootFeature then AutoShootFeature:SetStatus("Experimental · "..mode
..(sent and" · request sent"or(uncertain and" · send uncertain"or" · not sent")))end
elseif isCFrame then
fireCFrameShotImmediate(target,targetRoot,character,localRoot,gun)
else
fireBulletShotImmediate(target,targetRoot,character,localRoot,gun)
end
end
local function watchToolContainer(container:Instance?):()
if not container or type((container::any).ChildAdded)~="table"
and typeof((container::any).ChildAdded)~="RBXScriptSignal"then
return
end
pcall(function():()
local conn:RBXScriptConnection=(container::any).ChildAdded:Connect(
function(child:Instance):()
if autoShootActive and child and child.Name=="Gun"then
autoShootTick()
end
end
)
table.insert(toolConnections,conn)
end)
end
local function setupInstantGunWatchers():()
clearToolConnections()
if not LocalPlayer then
return
end
watchToolContainer(LocalPlayer.Character)
watchToolContainer(LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack"))
pcall(function():()
if LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded then
table.insert(
toolConnections,
LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar:Model):()
watchToolContainer(newChar)
watchToolContainer(LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack"))
end)
)
end
if LocalPlayer.ChildAdded then
table.insert(
toolConnections,
LocalPlayer.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child:Instance):()
if child and child:IsA("Backpack")then
watchToolContainer(child)
end
end)
)
end
end)
end
local function updateStatus():()
if not AutoShootFeature or not autoShootActive then
return
end
if settings.proximity then
AutoShootFeature:SetStatus(
settings.mode.." · "..tostring(settings.aura).." studs"
)
else
AutoShootFeature:SetStatus(settings.mode)
end
end
local CONNECTION_KEY:string="MM2AutoShootCard"
local function toggleAutoShoot(enabled:boolean):()
autoShootActive=enabled==true
if enabled then
updateStatus()
setupInstantGunWatchers()
disconnectFeatureConnection(CONNECTION_KEY)
featureConnections[CONNECTION_KEY]=TaskManager:Connect(
autoShootTick,
nil,
"Auto Shoot"
)
autoShootTick()
else
clearToolConnections()
disconnectFeatureConnection(CONNECTION_KEY)
if AutoShootFeature then
AutoShootFeature:SetStatus(nil)
end
end
end
AutoShootFeature=createUniversalFeature(
"Auto Shoot",
"Automatically equips the Gun the instant you receive it (as Sheriff or Hero) and shoots the Murderer continuously using Bullet (respects WallCheck) or CFrame (Silent AIM). Optional Proximity radius filter.",
4,
toggleAutoShoot,
{
categoryName="Utilities",
parent=MM2Scroll,
registry=mm2Features,
}
)
state.mm2AutoShootFeature=AutoShootFeature
local auraSlider:GuiObject?=nil
local states:any=type(configData)=="table"and configData.states or{}
local values:any=type(configData)=="table"and configData.values or{}
local initialMode:string=values["MM2.AutoShoot.Mode"]or"Bullet"
if initialMode~="CFrame"and initialMode~="Experimental"then initialMode="Bullet"end
local function modeConfig(mode:string):any
local prefix:string="MM2.AutoShoot."..mode.."."
local legacy:boolean=mode==initialMode
local wall:any=states[prefix.."WallCheck"]
local proximity:any=states[prefix.."Proximity"]
local aura:any=values[prefix.."Aura"]
if wall==nil and legacy then wall=states["MM2.AutoShoot.WallCheck"]end
if proximity==nil and legacy then proximity=states["MM2.AutoShoot.Proximity"]end
if aura==nil and legacy then aura=values["MM2.AutoShoot.Aura"]end
return{wallCheck=wall==nil or wall==true,proximity=proximity==true,
aura=type(aura)=="number"and aura or 25}
end
local modeConfigs:any={Bullet=modeConfig("Bullet"),CFrame=modeConfig("CFrame"),
Experimental=modeConfig("Experimental")}
local switching:boolean=false
local optionsReady:boolean=false
local function refreshProximityOption():()
if auraSlider and type(setOptionVisible)=="function"then
setOptionVisible(AutoShootFeature,auraSlider::GuiObject,settings.proximity==true)
end
updateStatus()
end
local function syncMode():()
local cfg:any=modeConfigs[settings.mode]or modeConfigs.Bullet
switching=true
settings.wallCheck,settings.proximity,settings.aura=cfg.wallCheck,cfg.proximity,cfg.aura
if state and type(state.toggleControls)=="table"then
local wc:any=state.toggleControls["MM2.AutoShoot.WallCheck"]
local px:any=state.toggleControls["MM2.AutoShoot.Proximity"]
if wc and wc.setChecked then pcall(wc.setChecked,cfg.wallCheck)end
if px and px.setChecked then pcall(px.setChecked,cfg.proximity)end
end
if state and type(state.valueControls)=="table"then
local au:any=state.valueControls["MM2.AutoShoot.Aura"]
if au and au.apply then pcall(au.apply,cfg.aura)end
end
switching=false
refreshProximityOption()
end
addCycleOption(AutoShootFeature,"Mode",{"Bullet","CFrame","Experimental"},1,
function(value:string):()settings.mode=value;syncMode()end,
"Experimental selects one path based on range and movement; the heuristic does not guarantee hits. Options are independent per mode.")
addToggleOption(AutoShootFeature,"WallCheck",true,function(value:boolean):()
settings.wallCheck=value==true
if optionsReady and not switching then
modeConfigs[settings.mode].wallCheck=settings.wallCheck
states["MM2.AutoShoot."..settings.mode..".WallCheck"]=settings.wallCheck
end
end,"Check for walls; saved independently per mode.")
addToggleOption(AutoShootFeature,"Proximity",false,function(value:boolean):()
settings.proximity=value==true
if optionsReady and not switching then
modeConfigs[settings.mode].proximity=settings.proximity
states["MM2.AutoShoot."..settings.mode..".Proximity"]=settings.proximity
end
refreshProximityOption()
end,"Only shoot inside Aura radius; saved independently per mode.")
auraSlider=addNumberOption(AutoShootFeature,"Aura",25,5,150,function(value:number):()
settings.aura=value
if optionsReady and not switching then
modeConfigs[settings.mode].aura=value
values["MM2.AutoShoot."..settings.mode..".Aura"]=value
end
updateStatus()
end,"Maximum distance with Proximity enabled, saved per mode.",1)
optionsReady=true
syncMode()
activeCleanup=function():()
toggleAutoShoot(false)
state.mm2AutoShootFeature=nil
state.mm2AutoShootCFrameOnce=nil
end
Module.Events=featureConnections
Module.Initialized=true
return Module
end
function Module.destroy():()
if not Module.Initialized then
return
end
Module.Initialized=false
pcall(activeCleanup)
activeCleanup=function():()end
Module.Events={}
Module.Runtime=nil
end
return Module
-- FIN src/games/MM2/Combat/AutoShoot.lua

-- INICIO src/games/MM2/Combat/KnifeThrow.lua
local Module={
Name="MM2 Knife Throw",
PlaceId=142823291,
Events={}::{[string]:any},
Initialized=false,
Runtime=nil::any,
}
local activeCleanup:()->()=function():()end
function Module.init(runtime:any):any
if Module.Initialized then
return Module
end
local core:any=state.mm2Core
assert(type(core)=="table","MM2 Knife Throw requires the MM2 core module")
Module.Runtime=runtime
local findMurderer:any=core.findMurderer
local getPlayerRole:any=core.getPlayerRole
local getPlayerWeapon:any=core.getPlayerWeapon
local isPlayerAlive:any=core.isPlayerAlive
local isProtectedTarget:any=core.isProtectedTarget
local getEstimatedLatency:any=core.getEstimatedLatency
local isWeaponPathClear:any=core.isWeaponPathClear
local prediction:any=state.mm2Prediction
local settings={
enabled=false,
autoThrow=false,
wallCheck=true,
knifeTracer=false,
delay=0.6,
}
local lastThrowAttempt:number=0
local KnifeThrowFeature:any=nil
local lastKnifeStatus:string?=nil
local function setKnifeStatus(message:string?):()
if KnifeThrowFeature and lastKnifeStatus~=message then
lastKnifeStatus=message
KnifeThrowFeature:SetStatus(message)
end
end
local tracerPart:BasePart?=nil
local targetDotPart:BasePart?=nil
local hookedWeaponService:any=nil
local origGetMouseTargetCFrame:any=nil
local origGetTargetPosition:any=nil
local lastRedirectInstallAttempt:number=-math.huge
local function destroyTracerVisuals():()
if tracerPart then
pcall(function():()
tracerPart:Destroy()
end)
tracerPart=nil
end
if targetDotPart then
pcall(function():()
targetDotPart:Destroy()
end)
targetDotPart=nil
end
end
local function isValidTarget(player:Player):boolean
if not player or player==LocalPlayer then
return false
end
if type(isProtectedTarget)=="function"and isProtectedTarget(player)then
return false
end
if type(isPlayerAlive)=="function"and not isPlayerAlive(player)then
return false
end
if type(getPlayerRole)=="function"and getPlayerRole(player)=="Murderer"then
return false
end
local character:Model?=player.Character
if not character then
return false
end
local root:BasePart?=character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")::BasePart?
local humanoid:Humanoid?=character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not root or not humanoid or humanoid.Health<=0 then
return false
end
return true
end
local function getKnife():Tool?
local character:Model?=LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character
if not character then
return nil
end
if type(getPlayerWeapon)=="function"then
local equippedKnife:Tool?=getPlayerWeapon(LocalPlayer,"Knife",true)
if equippedKnife then
return equippedKnife
end
local anyKnife:Tool?=getPlayerWeapon(LocalPlayer,"Knife",false)
if anyKnife then
return anyKnife
end
end
for _,item:Instance in ipairs(character:GetChildren())do
if item:IsA("Tool")then
local name:string=item.Name:lower()
if name:find("knife")or item:FindFirstChild("Events")then
local events:Instance?=item:FindFirstChild("Events")
if name:find("knife")or(events and events:FindFirstChild("KnifeThrown"))then
return item::Tool
end
end
end
end
local backpack:Instance?=LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
if backpack then
for _,item:Instance in ipairs(backpack:GetChildren())do
if item:IsA("Tool")then
local name:string=item.Name:lower()
local events:Instance?=item:FindFirstChild("Events")
if name:find("knife")or(events and events:FindFirstChild("KnifeThrown"))then
return item::Tool
end
end
end
end
return nil
end
local originHandleTool:Tool?=nil
local originHandle:BasePart?=nil
local function getThrowOriginCFrame(knife:Tool?):CFrame?
local character:Model?=LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character
if not character then
return nil
end
if knife and knife.Parent==character then
if originHandleTool~=knife or not originHandle or originHandle.Parent~=knife then
local handle:Instance?=knife:FindFirstChild("Handle")
originHandleTool=knife
originHandle=handle and handle:IsA("BasePart")and(handle::BasePart)or nil
end
if originHandle then return originHandle.CFrame end
end
local hand:Instance?=character:FindFirstChild("RightHand")
or character:FindFirstChild("Right Arm")
or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if hand and hand:IsA("BasePart")then
return(hand::BasePart).CFrame
end
return nil
end
local CORRIDOR_STUDS:number=0.45
local function raycastClear(
from:Vector3,
to:Vector3,
ignoreList:{Instance},
requireBody:Model?
):boolean
local ok:boolean,clear:any=pcall(function():boolean
local params:RaycastParams=RaycastParams.new()
params.FilterType=Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
params.FilterDescendantsInstances=ignoreList
params.RespectCanCollide=true
params.IgnoreWater=true
local hit:RaycastResult?=workspace:Raycast(from,to-from,params)
if requireBody then
return hit~=nil and hit.Instance:IsDescendantOf(requireBody)
end
return hit==nil
end)
return ok and clear==true
end
local function sharedPathClear(
from:Vector3,
to:Vector3,
targetChar:Model?,
requireBody:boolean
):boolean?
if type(isWeaponPathClear)~="function"or not targetChar then
return nil
end
local options:any=requireBody and{requireTarget=true}or{ignoreTarget=true}
local ok:boolean,clear:any=pcall(isWeaponPathClear,from,to,targetChar,true,options)
if not ok or type(clear)~="boolean"then
return nil
end
return clear
end
local function ignoreListFor(targetChar:Model?):{Instance}
local list:{Instance}={}
local localChar:Model?=LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character
if localChar then table.insert(list,localChar)end
if targetChar then table.insert(list,targetChar)end
return list
end
local function bodyVisible(originPos:Vector3,bodyPos:Vector3,targetChar:Model?):boolean
if not settings.wallCheck then
return true
end
local from:Vector3=originPos
local to:Vector3=bodyPos
if(to-from).Magnitude<0.05 then
return true
end
local shared:boolean?=sharedPathClear(from,to,targetChar,true)
if shared~=nil then
return shared
end
return raycastClear(from,to,ignoreListFor(nil),targetChar)
end
local function checkLineOfSight(originPos:Vector3,targetPos:Vector3,targetChar:Model?):boolean
if not settings.wallCheck then
return true
end
local direction:Vector3=targetPos-originPos
local length:number=direction.Magnitude
if length<0.05 then
return true
end
local unit:Vector3=direction/length
local side:Vector3=unit:Cross(Vector3.new(0,1,0))
if side.Magnitude<0.05 then
side=Vector3.new(1,0,0)
end
side=side.Unit
local up:Vector3=side:Cross(unit).Unit
local offsets:{Vector3}={
Vector3.zero,
side*CORRIDOR_STUDS,
side*-CORRIDOR_STUDS,
up*CORRIDOR_STUDS,
up*-CORRIDOR_STUDS,
}
for _index:number,offset:Vector3 in ipairs(offsets)do
local from:Vector3=originPos+offset
local to:Vector3=targetPos+offset
local shared:boolean?=sharedPathClear(from,to,targetChar,false)
if shared~=nil then
if not shared then
return false
end
elseif not raycastClear(from,to,ignoreListFor(targetChar),nil)then
return false
end
end
return true
end
local function resolveKnifeAim(
target:Player,
originPos:Vector3,
knife:Tool?,
cheap:boolean?
):(Vector3?,number?)
local character:Model?=target.Character
if not character then
return nil,nil
end
local root:BasePart?=character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")::BasePart?
if not root then
return nil,nil
end
if not bodyVisible(originPos,root.Position,character)then
return nil,nil
end
local speed:number=96
if knife then
pcall(function():()
local attrSpeed:any=knife:GetAttribute("ThrowSpeed")
if type(attrSpeed)=="number"and attrSpeed>=24 and attrSpeed<=300 then
speed=attrSpeed
end
end)
end
local predicted:Vector3?=nil
local aimInfo:any=nil
if type(prediction)=="table"and type(prediction.knifeAim)=="function"then
local okPred:boolean,aimPoint:any,info:any=
pcall(prediction.knifeAim,target,originPos,speed)
if okPred and typeof(aimPoint)=="Vector3"then
predicted=aimPoint::Vector3
aimInfo=info
end
end
if not predicted then
local velocity:Vector3=root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
if velocity.X~=velocity.X or velocity.Y~=velocity.Y or velocity.Z~=velocity.Z
or math.abs(velocity.X)==math.huge or math.abs(velocity.Y)==math.huge
or math.abs(velocity.Z)==math.huge then
velocity=Vector3.zero
end
local latency:number=0.06
if type(getEstimatedLatency)=="function"then
local okLat:boolean,l:any=pcall(getEstimatedLatency)
if okLat and type(l)=="number"and l==l then
latency=math.clamp(l,0,0.5)
end
end
local horizVel:Vector3=Vector3.new(velocity.X,0,velocity.Z)
local observed:Vector3=root.Position+horizVel*latency
local rel:Vector3=observed-originPos
local a:number=horizVel:Dot(horizVel)-speed*speed
local b:number=2*rel:Dot(horizVel)
local c:number=rel:Dot(rel)
local flight:number?=nil
if c<0.0025 then
flight=0
elseif math.abs(a)<1e-6 then
if b<-1e-6 then
flight=-c/b
end
else
local disc:number=b*b-4*a*c
if disc>=0 then
local sqrtDisc:number=math.sqrt(disc)
local t1:number=(-b-sqrtDisc)/(2*a)
local t2:number=(-b+sqrtDisc)/(2*a)
if t1>0 then
flight=t1
end
if t2>0 and(not flight or t2<flight)then
flight=t2
end
end
end
if flight and flight==flight then
flight=math.clamp(flight,0,1.5)
local yLead:number=math.clamp(
velocity.Y*math.min(latency+flight,0.28),-2.0,4.0
)
predicted=observed+horizVel*flight+Vector3.new(0,yLead,0)
else
local yLead:number=math.clamp(velocity.Y*math.min(latency,0.28),-2.0,4.0)
predicted=observed+Vector3.new(0,yLead,0)
end
end
if not predicted then
return nil,nil
end
local flight:number?=nil
if aimInfo and type(aimInfo.flightSeconds)=="number"then
flight=math.max(0,aimInfo.flightSeconds)
else
flight=(predicted-originPos).Magnitude/speed
end
if cheap or checkLineOfSight(originPos,predicted,character)then
return predicted,flight
end
local leadOffset:Vector3=predicted-root.Position
if aimInfo and typeof(aimInfo.capturedPosition)=="Vector3"then
leadOffset=predicted-(aimInfo.capturedPosition::Vector3)
end
local candidateNames:{string}={
"UpperTorso","Torso","HumanoidRootPart","Head",
"RightUpperArm","LeftUpperArm","Right Arm","Left Arm",
}
for _,partName:string in ipairs(candidateNames)do
local part:Instance?=character:FindFirstChild(partName)
if part and part:IsA("BasePart")then
local partPosition:Vector3=(part::BasePart).Position
local candidatePoint:Vector3=partPosition+leadOffset
if bodyVisible(originPos,partPosition,character)
and(cheap or checkLineOfSight(originPos,candidatePoint,character))then
return candidatePoint,flight
end
end
end
return nil,nil
end
local function findTarget():Player?
local localChar:Model?=LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character
if not localChar then
return nil
end
local localRoot:BasePart?=localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")::BasePart?
if not localRoot then
return nil
end
local knife:Tool?=getKnife()
local originCF:CFrame?=getThrowOriginCFrame(knife)
local originPos:Vector3=originCF and originCF.Position or localRoot.Position
local bestTarget:Player?=nil
local bestScore:number=math.huge
for _,player:Player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers())do
if isValidTarget(player)then
local targetChar:Model?=player.Character
local targetRoot:BasePart?=targetChar
and targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")::BasePart?
if targetRoot then
local distance:number=(targetRoot.Position-originPos).Magnitude
if distance<=250 then
local aimPoint:Vector3?,flight:number?=
resolveKnifeAim(player,originPos,knife,true)
if aimPoint then
local score:number=flight or(distance/96)
if type(getPlayerRole)=="function"then
local role:string?=getPlayerRole(player)
if role=="Sheriff"or role=="Hero"then
score-=0.08
end
end
if score<bestScore then
bestScore=score
bestTarget=player
end
end
end
end
end
end
return bestTarget
end
local TRACER_SMOOTH_RATE:number=26
local TRACER_SNAP_DISTANCE:number=20
local tracerPoint:Vector3?=nil
local tracerTarget:Player?=nil
local tracerShown:boolean=false
local tracerOpacity:number=0
local drawnOpacity:number=-1
local drawnOrigin:Vector3?=nil
local drawnPoint:Vector3?=nil
local function hideTracerParts():()
if tracerPart and tracerPart.Parent then tracerPart.Transparency=1 end
if targetDotPart and targetDotPart.Parent then targetDotPart.Transparency=1 end
end
local function hideTracerVisuals():()
if tracerShown then pcall(hideTracerParts)end
tracerShown=false
tracerOpacity=0
drawnOpacity=-1
tracerPoint=nil
tracerTarget=nil
drawnOrigin=nil
drawnPoint=nil
end
local function drawTracerParts(
originPos:Vector3,point:Vector3,delta:Vector3,dist:number,
updateGeometry:boolean,opacity:number,updateOpacity:boolean
):()
if not tracerPart or not tracerPart.Parent then
local beamPart:Part=Instance.new("Part")
beamPart.Name="WurstKnifeTracer"
beamPart.Anchored=true
beamPart.CanCollide=false
beamPart.CanQuery=false
beamPart.CanTouch=false
beamPart.Material=Enum.Material.Neon
beamPart.Color=Color3.fromRGB(255,65,65)
beamPart.Transparency=1
beamPart.Parent=workspace
tracerPart=beamPart
tracerShown=false
end
if not targetDotPart or not targetDotPart.Parent then
local dot:Part=Instance.new("Part")
dot.Name="WurstKnifeAimDot"
dot.Shape=Enum.PartType.Ball
dot.Size=Vector3.new(1.1,1.1,1.1)
dot.Anchored=true
dot.CanCollide=false
dot.CanQuery=false
dot.CanTouch=false
dot.Material=Enum.Material.Neon
dot.Color=Color3.fromRGB(255,220,60)
dot.Transparency=1
dot.Parent=workspace
targetDotPart=dot
tracerShown=false
end
if updateGeometry then
tracerPart.Size=Vector3.new(0.14,0.14,dist)
tracerPart.CFrame=CFrame.lookAt(originPos+delta*0.5,point)
targetDotPart.CFrame=CFrame.new(point)
end
if updateOpacity or not tracerShown then
tracerPart.Transparency=1-0.75*opacity
targetDotPart.Transparency=1-0.85*opacity
tracerShown=true
end
end
local function updateTracerVisual(
originPos:Vector3?,aimPos:Vector3?,target:Player?,deltaTime:number?
):()
if not settings.enabled or not settings.knifeTracer then
hideTracerVisuals()
destroyTracerVisuals()
return
end
if not originPos or not aimPos or not target then
hideTracerVisuals()
return
end
local dt:number=tonumber(deltaTime)or(1/60)
if dt~=dt then dt=1/60 end
local alpha:number=1-math.exp(-TRACER_SMOOTH_RATE*math.clamp(dt,0,0.25))
local previous:Vector3?=tracerPoint
if not previous or tracerTarget~=target
or(aimPos-previous).Magnitude>TRACER_SNAP_DISTANCE then
tracerPoint=aimPos
tracerOpacity=0
else
local nextPoint:Vector3=previous+(aimPos-previous)*alpha
tracerPoint=(aimPos-nextPoint).Magnitude<0.002 and aimPos or nextPoint
end
tracerOpacity+=(1-tracerOpacity)*alpha
if tracerOpacity>0.998 then tracerOpacity=1 end
tracerTarget=target
local point:Vector3=tracerPoint::Vector3
local delta:Vector3=point-originPos
local dist:number=delta.Magnitude
if dist<0.25 or dist>400 then
hideTracerVisuals()
return
end
local missing:boolean=not tracerPart or not tracerPart.Parent
or not targetDotPart or not targetDotPart.Parent
local geometryChanged:boolean=missing or not drawnOrigin or not drawnPoint
or(originPos-(drawnOrigin::Vector3)).Magnitude>=0.002
or(point-(drawnPoint::Vector3)).Magnitude>=0.002
local opacityChanged:boolean=missing or not tracerShown
or math.abs(tracerOpacity-drawnOpacity)>=0.002
if not geometryChanged and not opacityChanged then return end
local ok:boolean=pcall(
drawTracerParts,originPos,point,delta,dist,
geometryChanged,tracerOpacity,opacityChanged
)
if ok then
if geometryChanged then
drawnOrigin=originPos
drawnPoint=point
end
if opacityChanged then drawnOpacity=tracerOpacity end
else
pcall(hideTracerParts)
tracerShown=false
drawnOpacity=-1
drawnOrigin=nil
drawnPoint=nil
end
end
local equipRequestedAt:number=-math.huge
local equipRequestedTool:Tool?=nil
local function ensureEquipped(knife:Tool,humanoid:Humanoid):boolean
local character:Model?=LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character
if not character then
return false
end
if knife.Parent==character then
equipRequestedAt=-math.huge
equipRequestedTool=nil
return true
end
local now:number=os.clock()
if equipRequestedTool~=knife or now-equipRequestedAt>=0.25 then
equipRequestedAt=now
equipRequestedTool=knife
pcall(function():()
humanoid:EquipTool(knife)
end)
end
return false
end
local function throwKnife(target:Player?,knife:Tool):(boolean,string?)
local character:Model?=LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character
if not character then
return false,"Character unavailable"
end
local humanoid:Humanoid?=character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not humanoid or humanoid.Health<=0 then
return false,"Character unavailable"
end
if not ensureEquipped(knife,humanoid)then
return false,"Equipping knife…"
end
local originCF:CFrame?=getThrowOriginCFrame(knife)
if not originCF then
return false,"No throw origin"
end
if not target or not isValidTarget(target)then
return false,"No visible target"
end
local aimPos:Vector3?=resolveKnifeAim(target,originCF.Position,knife)
if not aimPos then
return false,"Target blocked · WallCheck"
end
local fired:boolean=false
local noted:boolean=false
local function noteThrowAttempt():()
if noted then return end
noted=true
local recorder:any=core and core.trajectoryCalibration
if recorder and type(recorder.noteAuthoredShot)=="function"then
local root:BasePart?=target.Character
and target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")::BasePart?
pcall(recorder.noteAuthoredShot,recorder,{
kind="knife",tool=knife,toolName=knife.Name,
originPos=originCF.Position,aimPos=aimPos,target=target,
aimVelocity=root and root.AssemblyLinearVelocity or nil,
source="menu-auto-throw",
settings={mode="Auto Knife Throw",wallCheck=settings.wallCheck},
})
end
end
local eventsFolder:Instance?=knife:FindFirstChild("Events")
local knifeThrownRemote:Instance?=eventsFolder and eventsFolder:FindFirstChild("KnifeThrown")
if knifeThrownRemote and knifeThrownRemote:IsA("RemoteEvent")then
noteThrowAttempt()
local okFire:boolean=pcall(function():()
(knifeThrownRemote::RemoteEvent):FireServer(originCF,CFrame.new(aimPos))
end)
if okFire then
fired=true
end
end
if not fired then
pcall(function():()
local rs:Instance?=game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local remotes:Instance?=rs and rs:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
local throwRemote:Instance?=remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Event")
if throwRemote and throwRemote:IsA("RemoteEvent")then
noteThrowAttempt()
local dir:Vector3=(aimPos-originCF.Position).Unit
local rem:RemoteEvent=throwRemote::RemoteEvent
rem:FireServer("Throw",aimPos,dir)
fired=true
end
end)
end
if not fired then
return false,"KnifeThrown remote unavailable"
end
return true,nil
end
local function installWeaponServiceRedirect():()
if hookedWeaponService then
return
end
pcall(function():()
local rs:Instance?=game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local clientServices:Instance?=rs and rs:FindFirstChild("ClientServices")
local wsModule:Instance?=clientServices and clientServices:FindFirstChild("WeaponService")
if not wsModule then
return
end
local okReq:boolean,wsTable:any=pcall(require,wsModule)
if not okReq or type(wsTable)~="table"then
return
end
origGetMouseTargetCFrame=wsTable.GetMouseTargetCFrame
origGetTargetPosition=wsTable.GetTargetPosition
hookedWeaponService=wsTable
if type(origGetMouseTargetCFrame)=="function"then
wsTable.GetMouseTargetCFrame=function(self:any,...:any):any
if settings.enabled then
local knife:Tool?=getKnife()
local character:Model?=LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character
if knife and character and knife.Parent==character then
local originCF:CFrame?=getThrowOriginCFrame(knife)
local target:Player?=findTarget()
if originCF and target then
local aim:Vector3?=resolveKnifeAim(target,originCF.Position,knife)
if aim then return CFrame.new(aim)end
end
end
end
return origGetMouseTargetCFrame(self,...)
end
end
if type(origGetTargetPosition)=="function"then
wsTable.GetTargetPosition=function(self:any,...:any):any
if settings.enabled then
local knife:Tool?=getKnife()
local character:Model?=LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character
if knife and character and knife.Parent==character then
local originCF:CFrame?=getThrowOriginCFrame(knife)
local target:Player?=findTarget()
if originCF and target then
local aim:Vector3?=resolveKnifeAim(target,originCF.Position,knife)
if aim then return CFrame.new(aim)end
end
end
end
return origGetTargetPosition(self,...)
end
end
end)
end
local function uninstallWeaponServiceRedirect():()
if not hookedWeaponService then
return
end
pcall(function():()
if origGetMouseTargetCFrame then
hookedWeaponService.GetMouseTargetCFrame=origGetMouseTargetCFrame
end
if origGetTargetPosition then
hookedWeaponService.GetTargetPosition=origGetTargetPosition
end
end)
hookedWeaponService=nil
origGetMouseTargetCFrame=nil
origGetTargetPosition=nil
end
local AIM_CACHE_SECONDS:number=1/15
local TRACER_CACHE_SECONDS:number=1/10
local cachedTarget:Player?=nil
local cachedAim:Vector3?=nil
local cachedOrigin:CFrame?=nil
local cachedVisualRoot:BasePart?=nil
local cachedVisualPosition:Vector3?=nil
local cachedAt:number=-math.huge
local function solveAim(knife:Tool,throwDue:boolean):(Player?,Vector3?,CFrame?)
local now:number=os.clock()
local interval:number=throwDue and AIM_CACHE_SECONDS or TRACER_CACHE_SECONDS
if now-cachedAt>=interval then
cachedAt=now
cachedTarget=findTarget()
cachedAim=nil
cachedVisualRoot=nil
cachedVisualPosition=nil
cachedOrigin=getThrowOriginCFrame(knife)
if cachedOrigin and cachedTarget then
cachedAim=resolveKnifeAim(cachedTarget,cachedOrigin.Position,knife)
if cachedAim and settings.knifeTracer then
local character:Model?=cachedTarget.Character
local root:BasePart?=character
and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")::BasePart?
if root then
cachedVisualRoot=root
cachedVisualPosition=root.Position
end
end
end
elseif cachedOrigin then
cachedOrigin=getThrowOriginCFrame(knife)or cachedOrigin
end
return cachedTarget,cachedAim,cachedOrigin
end
local function invalidateAim():()
cachedAt=-math.huge
cachedTarget=nil
cachedAim=nil
cachedOrigin=nil
cachedVisualRoot=nil
cachedVisualPosition=nil
end
local loopKnife:Tool?=nil
local loopKnifeParent:Instance?=nil
local loopKnifeCharacter:Model?=nil
local loopKnifeAt:number=-math.huge
local function resetLoopKnife():()
loopKnife=nil
loopKnifeParent=nil
loopKnifeCharacter=nil
loopKnifeAt=-math.huge
originHandleTool=nil
originHandle=nil
end
local function getLoopKnife(now:number,throwDue:boolean):Tool?
local character:Model?=LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character
if character~=loopKnifeCharacter then resetLoopKnife()end
loopKnifeCharacter=character
if not throwDue then
if loopKnife and loopKnife.Parent==character and character then
return loopKnife
end
local age:number=now-loopKnifeAt
if age>=0 and age<TRACER_CACHE_SECONDS then
if not loopKnife then return nil end
if loopKnifeParent and loopKnife.Parent==loopKnifeParent then
return loopKnife
end
end
end
loopKnife=getKnife()
loopKnifeParent=loopKnife and loopKnife.Parent or nil
loopKnifeAt=now
return loopKnife
end
local function tracerGoal(target:Player?,aimPos:Vector3?):Vector3?
if not target or not aimPos then return nil end
local root:BasePart?=cachedVisualRoot
local captured:Vector3?=cachedVisualPosition
if not root or not captured then return aimPos end
if target~=cachedTarget or root.Parent~=target.Character then
invalidateAim()
return nil
end
local moved:Vector3=root.Position-captured
if moved.Magnitude>8 then
invalidateAim()
return nil
end
return aimPos+moved
end
local function knifeThrowLoop(deltaTime:number?):()
if not settings.enabled then
setKnifeStatus(nil)
hideTracerVisuals()
destroyTracerVisuals()
invalidateAim()
return
end
local now:number=os.clock()
if not hookedWeaponService and now-lastRedirectInstallAttempt>=1 then
lastRedirectInstallAttempt=now
installWeaponServiceRedirect()
end
local cooldown:number=math.clamp(tonumber(settings.delay)or 0,0,10)
local wantThrow:boolean=settings.autoThrow
and now-lastThrowAttempt>=cooldown
if not settings.knifeTracer and not wantThrow then
hideTracerVisuals()
destroyTracerVisuals()
if not settings.autoThrow then
setKnifeStatus(nil)
invalidateAim()
end
return
end
local knife:Tool?=getLoopKnife(now,wantThrow)
if not knife then
setKnifeStatus("Waiting for knife")
hideTracerVisuals()
invalidateAim()
return
end
local target,aimPos,originCF=solveAim(knife,wantThrow)
if not aimPos then
setKnifeStatus(target and"Target blocked · WallCheck"or"No visible target")
hideTracerVisuals()
return
end
setKnifeStatus("Predicted target")
if settings.knifeTracer and originCF then
updateTracerVisual(originCF.Position,tracerGoal(target,aimPos),target,deltaTime)
else
hideTracerVisuals()
destroyTracerVisuals()
end
if wantThrow then
local thrown:boolean,why:string?=throwKnife(target,knife)
if thrown then
setKnifeStatus("Throw sent · hit unconfirmed")
lastThrowAttempt=now
invalidateAim()
elseif why then
setKnifeStatus(why)
end
end
end
local function toggleKnifeThrow(enabled:boolean):()
settings.enabled=enabled
disconnectFeatureConnection("MM2KnifeThrow")
if not enabled then
uninstallWeaponServiceRedirect()
setKnifeStatus(nil)
hideTracerVisuals()
destroyTracerVisuals()
invalidateAim()
resetLoopKnife()
return
end
installWeaponServiceRedirect()
featureConnections.MM2KnifeThrow=
TaskManager:Connect(knifeThrowLoop,nil,"MM2 Knife Throw")
end
KnifeThrowFeature=createUniversalFeature(
"Knife Throw",
"Redirects PC & Mobile knife throws to the Knife Predictor's aim point, with optional Auto Knife Throw and Knife Tracer.",
15,
toggleKnifeThrow,
{
categoryName="Exploits",
parent=MM2Scroll,
registry=mm2Features,
}
)
addToggleOption(
KnifeThrowFeature,
"WallCheck",
true,
function(value:boolean):()
settings.wallCheck=value
end,
"Checks the blade's path from the hand to the target, including a thin corridor and invisible solid barriers. With nobody visible, nothing is thrown."
)
addToggleOption(
KnifeThrowFeature,
"Auto Knife Throw",
false,
function(value:boolean):()
settings.autoThrow=value==true
end,
"Throws at the predicted intercept of a visible target. With nobody visible, or with the path blocked, no knife is spent. A sent throw is not a confirmed hit."
)
addNumberOption(
KnifeThrowFeature,
"Delay",
0.6,
0,
10,
function(value:number):()
settings.delay=math.clamp(tonumber(value)or 0,0,10)
end,
"Delay in seconds between automatic knife throws (0 to 10 seconds).",
0.1
)
addInformationOption(
KnifeThrowFeature,
"Seconds"
)

end
end,
"Smooth 3D preview from your hand to the predicted point. The actual throw is re-solved separately; the preview hides when no target passes WallCheck."
)

end
