-- INICIO src/games/MM2/Fun/BlurtRoles.lua
local Module={
Name="MM2 Blurt Roles",
Events={}::{[string]:any},
Initialized=false,
Runtime=nil::any,
}
local activeCleanup:()->()=function():()end
function Module.init(runtime:any):any
if Module.Initialized then
return Module
end
local core:any=state.mm2Core
assert(type(core)=="table","MM2 Blurt Roles requires the MM2 core module")
Module.Runtime=runtime
local findMurderer:any=core.findMurderer
local findSheriff:any=core.findSheriff
local hasActiveRoundRoles:any=core.hasActiveRoundRoles
local mm2Settings:any=core.mm2Settings
local blurtEnabled:boolean=false
local blurtSignature:string=""
local blurtPending:boolean=false
local blurtGeneration:number=0
local lastBlurtAt:number=-math.huge
local function cancelPendingBlurt():()
blurtGeneration+=1
blurtPending=false
end
local function sendPublicChat(message:string):boolean
local sent:boolean=false
local textChatOk:boolean=pcall(function():()
local textChatService:TextChatService=
game:GetService("TextChatService")
if textChatService.ChatVersion~=Enum.ChatVersion.TextChatService then
return
end
local channels:Instance?=textChatService:FindFirstChild("TextChannels")
local channel:Instance?=channels
and(channels:FindFirstChild("RBXGeneral")
or channels:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextChannel"))
if channel and channel:IsA("TextChannel")then
(channel::TextChannel):SendAsync(message)
sent=true
end
end)
if sent then
return true
end
local legacyOk:boolean=pcall(function():()
local events:Instance?=game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
:FindFirstChild("DefaultChatSystemChatEvents")
local say:Instance?=events
and events:FindFirstChild("SayMessageRequest")
if say and say:IsA("RemoteEvent")then
(say::RemoteEvent):FireServer(message,"All")
sent=true
end
end)
return sent and(textChatOk or legacyOk)
end
local function buildBlurtMessage():(string?,string?)
if not hasActiveRoundRoles()then
return nil,nil
end
local murderer:Player?=findMurderer()
local sheriff:Player?=findSheriff()
if not murderer and not sheriff then
return nil,nil
end
local murdererName:string=murderer and murderer.Name or"?"
local sheriffName:string=sheriff and sheriff.Name or"?"
local message:string
local signature:string
if mm2Settings.blurtFake then
message=string.format(
'Murder; "%s" Sheriff; "%s"',
sheriffName,
murdererName
)
signature="fake/"..sheriffName.."/"..murdererName
else
message=string.format(
'Murder; "%s" Sheriff; "%s" | Wurst',
murdererName,
sheriffName
)
signature=murdererName.."/"..sheriffName
end
return message,signature
end
local function blurtRoles(manual:boolean):()
local message:string?,signature:string?=buildBlurtMessage()
if not message then
if manual then
notify("MM2 · no roles to blurt yet.")
end
return
end
if not manual then
if signature==blurtSignature and not mm2Settings.blurtRepeat then
return
end
if os.clock()-lastBlurtAt<2.5 then
return
end
end
blurtSignature=signature::string
lastBlurtAt=os.clock()
task.spawn(function():()
if sendPublicChat(message::string)then
notify("MM2 · blurted: "..(message::string))
else
notify("MM2 · the chat refused the message (filtered or disabled).")
end
end)
end
local function scheduleBlurt():()
if not blurtEnabled or blurtPending then
return
end
local message:string?,signature:string?=buildBlurtMessage()
if not message then
return
end
if signature==blurtSignature and not mm2Settings.blurtRepeat then
return
end
blurtPending=true
local generation:number=blurtGeneration
task.delay(math.max(mm2Settings.blurtDelay,0),function():()
if generation~=blurtGeneration then return end
blurtPending=false
if blurtEnabled then blurtRoles(false)end
end)
end
local unsubscribe:()->()=core.onRoundRoles(function(active:boolean):()
if not active then
cancelPendingBlurt()
blurtSignature=""
return
end
scheduleBlurt()
end)
local function toggleBlurtRoles(enabled:boolean):()
blurtEnabled=enabled
cancelPendingBlurt()
disconnectFeatureConnection("MM2BlurtRoles")
if not enabled then
return
end
blurtSignature=""
local elapsed:number=0
featureConnections.MM2BlurtRoles=TaskManager:Connect(function(deltaTime:number):()
elapsed+=deltaTime
if elapsed<1 then
return
end
elapsed=0
if not hasActiveRoundRoles()then
blurtSignature=""
return
end
scheduleBlurt()
end,nil,"MM2 Blurt Roles")
end
local BlurtFeature=createUniversalFeature(
"Blurt Roles",
"Types the murderer and sheriff in the public chat. Click the card to blurt right now.",
3,
function():()
blurtRoles(true)
end,
{
categoryName="World",
parent=MM2Scroll,
registry=mm2Features,
kind="action",
}
)
addToggleOption(
BlurtFeature,
"Auto blurt",
false,
function(value:boolean):()
toggleBlurtRoles(value)
end,
"Blurt automatically once per round, after the Delay below. Off by default: the card alone is an instant, one-shot blurt."
)
addNumberOption(
BlurtFeature,
"Delay",
mm2Settings.blurtDelay,
0,
15,
function(value:number):()
mm2Settings.blurtDelay=value
end,
"Seconds to wait after the roles are known before typing.",
0.5
)
addToggleOption(
BlurtFeature,
"Repeat on change",
mm2Settings.blurtRepeat,
function(value:boolean):()
mm2Settings.blurtRepeat=value
end,
"Blurt again when the sheriff dies and the gun changes hands."
)
addToggleOption(
BlurtFeature,
"Fake Blurt Roles",
mm2Settings.blurtFake,
function(value:boolean):()
mm2Settings.blurtFake=value
end,
"Announces the roles SWAPPED (and without the Wurst tag) so the chat "
.."lies about who the murderer and sheriff are."
)
addInformationOption(
BlurtFeature,
"This types in the real public chat: everyone reads it and you will be"
.." reported. Roblox also rate-limits and filters messages."
)
activeCleanup=function():()
pcall(unsubscribe)
toggleBlurtRoles(false)
end
Module.Events=featureConnections
Module.Initialized=true
return Module
end
function Module.destroy():()
if not Module.Initialized then
return
end
Module.Initialized=false
activeCleanup()
activeCleanup=function():()end
Module.Events={}
Module.Runtime=nil
end
return Module
-- FIN src/games/MM2/Fun/BlurtRoles.lua

-- INICIO src/games/MM2/Other/AutoUnbox.lua
local Module={
Name="MM2 Auto Unbox",
Events={}::{[string]:any},
Initialized=false,
Runtime=nil::any,
}
local activeCleanup:()->()=function():()end
type BoxEntry={
id:string,
name:string,
category:string,
prices:{[string]:number}
}
local AUTO_CURRENCY:string="Auto"
local REVEAL_ON:string="On"
local REVEAL_OFF:string="Off"
local DEFAULT_BOX_LABEL:string="Mystery Box #1"
local FALLBACK_BOXES:{BoxEntry}={
{id="MysteryBox1",name="Mystery Box #1",category="MysteryBox",prices={Coins=1000,Gems=100,Key=1}},
{id="MysteryBox2",name="Mystery Box #2",category="MysteryBox",prices={Coins=1000,Gems=100,Key=1}},
{id="KnifeBox1",name="Knife Box #1",category="MysteryBox",prices={Coins=1000,Gems=100,Key=1}},
{id="KnifeBox2",name="Knife Box #2",category="MysteryBox",prices={Coins=1000,Gems=100,Key=1}},
{id="KnifeBox3",name="Knife Box #3",category="MysteryBox",prices={Coins=1000,Gems=100,Key=1}},
{id="KnifeBox4",name="Knife Box #4",category="MysteryBox",prices={Coins=1000,Gems=100,Key=1}},
{id="KnifeBox5",name="Knife Box #5",category="MysteryBox",prices={Coins=1000,Gems=100,Key=1}},
{id="MLG Box",name="Rainbow Box",category="MysteryBox",prices={Coins=1000,Gems=100,Key=1}},
{id="GunBox1",name="Gun Box #1",category="MysteryBox",prices={Coins=1000,Gems=100,Key=1}},
{id="GunBox2",name="Gun Box #2",category="MysteryBox",prices={Coins=1000,Gems=100,Key=1}},
{id="GunBox3",name="Gun Box #3",category="MysteryBox",prices={Coins=1000,Gems=100,Key=1}},
{id="Egg",name="Common Egg",category="Eggs",prices={Coins=1000,Gems=100}},
{id="Christmas2024Box",name="Christmas Box '24",category="MysteryBox",prices={SnowTokens2024=800,SnowKey2024=1}},
{id="Summer2025Box",name="Summer Box '25",category="MysteryBox",prices={BeachBalls2025=800,SummerKey2025=1}},
{id="Halloween2025Box",name="Halloween Box '25",category="MysteryBox",prices={Candies2025=800,HalloweenKey2025=1}},
}
local FAMILY_CURRENCIES:{[string]:{string}}={
Coin={"Coins"},
Candy={"Candies2025","Candies2024","Candies2022"},
SnowToken={"SnowTokens2024","SnowTokens2023","SnowTokens2022","SnowTokens2020"},
BeachBall={"BeachBalls2025"},
Egg={"Eggs2025","RareEggs2025"},
Heart={"Hearts2023"},
}
local BAG_TO_FAMILY:{[string]:string}={
Coin="Coin",
Candy="Candy",
SnowToken="SnowToken",
BeachBall="BeachBall",
Egg="Egg",
}
local function lower(value:string):string
return string.lower(value)
end
local function contains(value:string,needle:string):boolean
return string.find(value,needle,1,true)~=nil
end
local function sortedKeys(map:{[string]:any}):{string}
local keys:{string}={}
for key:string in pairs(map)do
table.insert(keys,key)
end
table.sort(keys)
return keys
end
function Module.init(runtime:any):any
if Module.Initialized then
return Module
end
local core:any=state.mm2Core
assert(type(core)=="table","MM2 Auto Unbox requires the MM2 core module")
Module.Runtime=runtime
local settings={
boxLabel=DEFAULT_BOX_LABEL,
currency=AUTO_CURRENCY,
delay=1.5,
maxBoxes=10,
reveal=REVEAL_ON,
}
local session={
opened=0,
failures=0,
consecutiveFailures=0,
nextAt=0,
finished=false,
}
local serverFamily:string?=nil
local function safeRequire(instance:Instance?):any?
if not instance then
return nil
end
local ok:boolean,result:any=pcall(function():any
return(state::any).requireModule(instance)
end)
if ok then
return result
end
return nil
end
local function priceOf(boxId:string,sync:any):{[string]:number}?
if type(sync)=="table"and type(sync.NewShop)=="table"then
local newShop:any=sync.NewShop
local holders:{any}={}
if type(newShop[boxId])=="table"then
table.insert(holders,newShop[boxId])
end
if type(newShop.Weapons)=="table"and type(newShop.Weapons[boxId])=="table"then
table.insert(holders,newShop.Weapons[boxId])
end
if type(newShop.MysteryBox)=="table"and type(newShop.MysteryBox[boxId])=="table"then
table.insert(holders,newShop.MysteryBox[boxId])
end
for _,holder:any in ipairs(holders)do
if type(holder.Price)=="table"then
return holder.Price
end
end
end
for _,entry:BoxEntry in ipairs(FALLBACK_BOXES)do
if entry.id==boxId then
return entry.prices
end
end
return nil
end
local function eventBoxModules():{Instance}
local found:{Instance}={}
local replicated:Instance=game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local shared:Instance?=replicated:FindFirstChild("SharedServices")
local service:Instance?=shared and shared:FindFirstChild("EventInfoService1")
if not service then
return found
end
local direct:Instance?=service:FindFirstChild("MysteryBoxes")
if direct then
for _,child:Instance in ipairs(direct:GetChildren())do
if child:IsA("ModuleScript")then
table.insert(found,child)
end
end
end
local eventList:Instance?=service:FindFirstChild("EventList")
if eventList then
for _,event:Instance in ipairs(eventList:GetChildren())do
local boxModule:Instance?=event:FindFirstChild("MysteryBox")
if boxModule and boxModule:IsA("ModuleScript")then
table.insert(found,boxModule)
end
end
end
return found
end
local function buildCatalog():({BoxEntry},{string})
local catalog:{BoxEntry}={}
local seen:{[string]:boolean}={}
local function add(entry:BoxEntry):()
if seen[entry.id]then
return
end
seen[entry.id]=true
table.insert(catalog,entry)
end
local replicated:Instance=game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local database:Instance?=replicated:FindFirstChild("Database")
local sync:any=safeRequire(database and database:FindFirstChild("Sync"))
if type(sync)=="table"then
for _,bankName:string in ipairs({"MysteryBox","Eggs"})do
local bank:any=(sync::any)[bankName]
if type(bank)=="table"then
for id:string,data:any in pairs(bank)do
add({
id=id,
name=(type(data)=="table"and type(data.Name)=="string"and data.Name)or id,
category=bankName=="Eggs"and"Eggs"or"MysteryBox",
prices=priceOf(id,sync)or{},
})
end
end
end
end
for _,boxModule:Instance in ipairs(eventBoxModules())do
local data:any=safeRequire(boxModule)
local display:string?=nil
if type(data)=="table"then
if type(data.ItemData)=="table"and type(data.ItemData.Name)=="string"then
display=data.ItemData.Name
elseif type(data.Name)=="string"and data.Name~=boxModule.Name then
display=data.Name
end
end
add({
id=boxModule.Name,
name=display or boxModule.Name,
category="MysteryBox",
prices=priceOf(boxModule.Name,sync)or{},
})
end
for _,entry:BoxEntry in ipairs(FALLBACK_BOXES)do
add(entry)
end
table.sort(catalog,function(a:BoxEntry,b:BoxEntry):boolean
return lower(a.name)<lower(b.name)
end)
local labelCounts:{[string]:number}={}
for _,entry:BoxEntry in ipairs(catalog)do
labelCounts[entry.name]=(labelCounts[entry.name]or 0)+1
end
local labels:{string}={}
for _,entry:BoxEntry in ipairs(catalog)do
if labelCounts[entry.name]>1 then
table.insert(labels,entry.name.." ("..entry.id..")")
else
table.insert(labels,entry.name)
end
end
return catalog,labels
end
local catalog:{BoxEntry},boxLabels:{string}=buildCatalog()
local labelToEntry:{[string]:BoxEntry}={}
for index:number,entry:BoxEntry in ipairs(catalog)do
labelToEntry[boxLabels[index] ]=entry
end
if not labelToEntry[settings.boxLabel]then
settings.boxLabel=boxLabels[1]or""
end
local function currentEntry():BoxEntry?
return labelToEntry[settings.boxLabel]or catalog[1]
end
local currencyChoices:{string}={AUTO_CURRENCY}
do
local seen:{[string]:boolean}={[AUTO_CURRENCY]=true}
for _,entry:BoxEntry in ipairs(catalog)do
for currency:string in pairs(entry.prices)do
if not seen[currency]then
seen[currency]=true
table.insert(currencyChoices,currency)
end
end
end
end
local function familyFromWorkspace():string?
local eventName:string=lower(tostring(workspace:GetAttribute("CurrentEvent")or""))
if contains(eventName,"halloween")then
return"Candy"
elseif contains(eventName,"christmas")then
return"SnowToken"
elseif contains(eventName,"summer")then
return"BeachBall"
elseif contains(eventName,"easter")then
return"Egg"
elseif contains(eventName,"valentine")then
return"Heart"
end
for attribute:string,family:string in pairs({
isValentines="Heart",
isChristmas="SnowToken",
isChristmasEnding="SnowToken",
IsSummer="BeachBall",
IsEaster="Egg",
})do
if workspace:GetAttribute(attribute)==true then
return family
end
end
return nil
end
local function activeFamily():string?
return serverFamily or familyFromWorkspace()
end
local function readSyncDatabase():any
local replicated:Instance=game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local database:Instance?=replicated:FindFirstChild("Database")
return safeRequire(database and database:FindFirstChild("Sync"))
end
local function readProfileData():any
local replicated:Instance=game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local modules:Instance?=replicated:FindFirstChild("Modules")
return safeRequire(modules and modules:FindFirstChild("ProfileData"))
end
local function getPlayerCurrencyBalance(currency:string):number?
if currency=="Robux"then
return nil
end
local profile:any=readProfileData()
if type(profile)~="table"then
return nil
end
local direct:any=profile[currency]
if type(direct)=="number"then
return direct
end
local materials:any=profile.Materials
if type(materials)=="table"and type(materials.Owned)=="table"then
local matOwned:any=materials.Owned[currency]
if type(matOwned)=="number"then
return matOwned
end
end
local weapons:any=profile.Weapons
if type(weapons)=="table"and type(weapons.Owned)=="table"then
local wepOwned:any=weapons.Owned[currency]
if type(wepOwned)=="number"then
return wepOwned
end
end
if type(profile.Coins)=="number"
or type(profile.Gems)=="number"
or type(materials)=="table"
or type(weapons)=="table"then
return 0
end
return nil
end
local function canAffordCurrency(entry:BoxEntry,currency:string):boolean
local price:number?=entry.prices and entry.prices[currency]or nil
if type(price)~="number"or price<=0 then
return true
end
local balance:number?=getPlayerCurrencyBalance(currency)
if balance==nil then
return true
end
return balance>=price
end
local function autoCurrency(entry:BoxEntry):string?
local prices:{[string]:number}=entry.prices or{}
local orderedCandidates:{string}={}
local seenCandidate:{[string]:boolean}={}
local function pushCandidate(c:string):()
if prices[c]and not seenCandidate[c]then
seenCandidate[c]=true
table.insert(orderedCandidates,c)
end
end
local family:string?=activeFamily()
if family then
for _,currency:string in ipairs(FAMILY_CURRENCIES[family]or{})do
pushCandidate(currency)
end
end
for _,currency:string in ipairs({"Coins","Gems"})do
pushCandidate(currency)
end
for _,currency:string in ipairs(sortedKeys(prices))do
if not contains(lower(currency),"key")then
pushCandidate(currency)
end
end
for _,currency:string in ipairs(sortedKeys(prices))do
pushCandidate(currency)
end
for _,currency:string in ipairs(orderedCandidates)do
if canAffordCurrency(entry,currency)then
return currency
end
end
return orderedCandidates[1]
end
local function resolveCurrency(entry:BoxEntry):(string?,boolean)
if settings.currency~=AUTO_CURRENCY then
if entry.prices and entry.prices[settings.currency]then
return settings.currency,false
end
return autoCurrency(entry),true
end
return autoCurrency(entry),false
end
local function shopRemotes():Instance?
local replicated:Instance=game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local remotes:Instance?=replicated:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
return remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Shop")or nil
end
local function report(text:string):()
local card:any=state.mm2AutoUnboxFeature
if card then
card:SetStatus(text)
end
end
local function summary():string
local entry:BoxEntry?=currentEntry()
local currency:string?=entry and resolveCurrency(entry)or nil
local text:string=(entry and entry.name or"no box")
.." · pay "..(currency or"nothing")
local family:string?=activeFamily()
if family then
text..=" · server coin: "..family
end
if session.opened>0 or session.failures>0 then
text..=" · "..tostring(session.opened).." opened"
if session.failures>0 then
text..=" · "..tostring(session.failures).." failed"
end
end
if session.finished then
text..=" · done"
end
return text
end
local function turnCardOff():()
local card:any=state.mm2AutoUnboxFeature
if not card then
return
end
card.enabled=false
pcall(function():()
card.onToggle(false)
end)
if type(refreshFeatureToggle)=="function"then
pcall(refreshFeatureToggle,card)
end
end
local function stop(reason:string?):()
disconnectFeatureConnection("MM2AutoUnbox")
if reason then
notify(reason)
end
turnCardOff()
end
local openingBusy:boolean=false
local revealUntil:number=0
local REVEAL_SECONDS:number=5
local function clearStuckUnboxOverlays():()
pcall(function():()
local playerGui:Instance?=LocalPlayer
and LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
if not playerGui then
return
end
for _,gui:Instance in ipairs(playerGui:GetChildren())do
if gui.Name=="MysteryBoxOpen"or gui.Name=="Unboxing2"then
gui:Destroy()
elseif gui.Name=="MainGUI"then
for _,subName:string in ipairs({"Lobby","Game"})do
local sub:Instance?=gui:FindFirstChild(subName)
local proc:Instance?=sub and sub:FindFirstChild("Processing")
if proc and proc:IsA("GuiObject")then
(proc::GuiObject).Visible=false
end
end
end
end
end)
end
local function isValidReward(reward:any,category:string):boolean
if type(reward)~="string"or reward==""or reward=="false"or reward=="nil"then
return false
end
local sync:any=readSyncDatabase()
if type(sync)=="table"then
if category=="Eggs"then
local pets:any=sync.Pets
if type(pets)=="table"and next(pets)~=nil then
return pets[reward]~=nil
end
else
local weapons:any=sync.Weapons
local items:any=sync.Item
if type(weapons)=="table"and next(weapons)~=nil then
if weapons[reward]~=nil then
return true
end
if type(items)=="table"and items[reward]~=nil then
return true
end
return false
end
end
end
return true
end
local function canPlayBoxAnimationSafely(boxId:string,reward:string):boolean
local sync:any=readSyncDatabase()
if type(sync)~="table"then
return true
end
local weapons:any=sync.Weapons
if type(weapons)~="table"or next(weapons)==nil then
return true
end
if type(weapons[reward])~="table"then
return false
end
local mysteryBoxes:any=sync.MysteryBox
if type(mysteryBoxes)=="table"and next(mysteryBoxes)~=nil then
local boxDef:any=mysteryBoxes[boxId]
if type(boxDef)~="table"or type(boxDef.Contents)~="table"then
return false
end
end
return true
end
local function reveal(boxId:string,category:string,reward:any):()
local remotes:Instance?=shopRemotes()
if not remotes then
return
end
if settings.reveal==REVEAL_ON then
if category=="Eggs"then
revealUntil=os.clock()+REVEAL_SECONDS
local eggController:Instance?=remotes:FindFirstChild("EggController")
if eggController then
pcall(function():()
(eggController::any):Fire(boxId,reward)
end)
end
return
elseif canPlayBoxAnimationSafely(boxId,tostring(reward))then
revealUntil=os.clock()+REVEAL_SECONDS
local boxController:Instance?=remotes:FindFirstChild("BoxController")
if boxController then
pcall(function():()
(boxController::any):Fire({{MysteryBoxId=boxId,RewardedItemId=reward}})
end)
end
return
end
end
local crateComplete:Instance?=remotes:FindFirstChild("CrateComplete")
if crateComplete then
pcall(function():()
(crateComplete::any):FireServer(reward)
end)
end
end
local function openOnce():boolean
if openingBusy then
return false
end
openingBusy=true
local entry:BoxEntry?=currentEntry()
if not entry then
session.finished=true
openingBusy=false
stop("Auto Unbox: no box catalogue found.")
return false
end
local currency:string?,forced:boolean=resolveCurrency(entry)
if not currency then
session.finished=true
openingBusy=false
stop("Auto Unbox: "..entry.name.." has no known price currency.")
return false
end
local requiredPrice:number?=entry.prices and entry.prices[currency]or nil
local currentBalance:number?=getPlayerCurrencyBalance(currency)
if type(requiredPrice)=="number"
and requiredPrice>0
and type(currentBalance)=="number"
and currentBalance<requiredPrice then
clearStuckUnboxOverlays()
openingBusy=false
report(
summary()
.." · waiting for "
..tostring(currency)
.." ("
..tostring(currentBalance)
.."/"
..tostring(requiredPrice)
..")"
)
return false
end
local remotes:Instance?=shopRemotes()
local openCrate:Instance?=remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("OpenCrate")
if not(openCrate and openCrate:IsA("RemoteFunction"))then
openingBusy=false
report(summary().." · waiting for shop")
return false
end
local note:string=forced
and(" · "..settings.currency.." rejected, using "..currency)
or""
local ok:boolean,reward:any=pcall(function():any
return(openCrate::any):InvokeServer(entry.id,entry.category,currency)
end)
if ok and isValidReward(reward,entry.category)then
reveal(entry.id,entry.category,reward)
session.opened+=1
session.consecutiveFailures=0
report(summary()..note)
openingBusy=false
return true
end
clearStuckUnboxOverlays()
session.failures+=1
session.consecutiveFailures+=1
report(summary()..note.." · waiting for "..tostring(currency))
openingBusy=false
return false
end
local function tick(_deltaTime:number):()
local now:number=os.clock()
if now<session.nextAt or now<revealUntil or openingBusy then
return
end
local opened:boolean=openOnce()
local settle:number=(opened and settings.reveal==REVEAL_ON)
and REVEAL_SECONDS or 0
session.nextAt=os.clock()+settings.delay+settle
end
local AutoUnboxFeature:any=createUniversalFeature(
"Auto Unbox",
"Opens any mystery box or egg of the game through its own OpenCrate remote, paying with the currency you choose or the coin the server is giving.",
4,
function(enabled:boolean):()
disconnectFeatureConnection("MM2AutoUnbox")
if os.clock()>=revealUntil then
clearStuckUnboxOverlays()
end
if not enabled then
return
end
session.opened=0
session.failures=0
session.consecutiveFailures=0
session.nextAt=math.max(os.clock(),revealUntil)
session.finished=false
report(summary())
featureConnections.MM2AutoUnbox=TaskManager:Connect(tick,nil,"MM2 Auto Unbox")
end,
{
categoryName="Utilities",
parent=MM2Scroll,
registry=mm2Features,
}
)
state.mm2AutoUnboxFeature=AutoUnboxFeature
local startedRemote:Instance?=core.mm2GameplayRemotes
and core.mm2GameplayRemotes:FindFirstChild("CoinsStarted")
if startedRemote and startedRemote:IsA("RemoteEvent")then
featureConnections.MM2AutoUnboxCoinsStarted=
(startedRemote::any).OnClientEvent:Connect(function(bagTable:any):()
if type(bagTable)=="table"then
for bag:any in pairs(bagTable)do
local family:string?=BAG_TO_FAMILY[tostring(bag)]
if family then
serverFamily=family
end
end
end
if state.mm2AutoUnboxFeature then
report(summary())
end
end)
end
local defaultBoxIndex:number=table.find(boxLabels,DEFAULT_BOX_LABEL)or 1
addCycleOption(
AutoUnboxFeature,
"Box to open",
boxLabels,
defaultBoxIndex,
function(value:string):()
settings.boxLabel=value
end,
"Every box and egg of the game, including the event boxes."
)
addCycleOption(
AutoUnboxFeature,
"Pay with",
currencyChoices,
1,
function(value:string):()
settings.currency=value
end,
"Auto pays with the currency the server's current coin fills."
)
addCycleOption(
AutoUnboxFeature,
"Reveal animation",
{REVEAL_ON,REVEAL_OFF},
1,
function(value:string):()
settings.reveal=value
end,
"On replays the game's own opening animation and crate report."
)
addNumberOption(
AutoUnboxFeature,
"Delay between boxes",
settings.delay,
0.8,
5,
function(value:number):()
settings.delay=value
end
)
addNumberOption(
AutoUnboxFeature,
"Max boxes per run",
settings.maxBoxes,
1,
50,
function(value:number):()
settings.maxBoxes=value
end
)
addActionOption(
AutoUnboxFeature,
"Report boxes and coin",
function():()
local entry:BoxEntry?=currentEntry()
local prices:{string}={}
if entry then
for _,currency:string in ipairs(sortedKeys(entry.prices))do
table.insert(prices,currency.."="..tostring(entry.prices[currency]))
end
end
notify(
"Boxes found: "..tostring(#catalog)
.." · chosen: "..(entry and entry.name or"none")
.." ("..(entry and entry.id or"?")..")"
.." · price: "..(#prices>0 and table.concat(prices,", ")or"unknown")
.." · server coin: "..tostring(activeFamily()or"unknown")
)
end
)
addInformationOption(
AutoUnboxFeature,
"Uses the shop's own OpenCrate remote with the box id and currency the game expects, then the game's own reveal. Robux prompts are never pressed."
)
activeCleanup=function():()
state.mm2AutoUnboxFeature=nil
disconnectFeatureConnection("MM2AutoUnbox")
disconnectFeatureConnection("MM2AutoUnboxCoinsStarted")
end
Module.Events=featureConnections
Module.Initialized=true
return Module
end
function Module.destroy():()
if not Module.Initialized then
return
end
Module.Initialized=false
pcall(activeCleanup)
activeCleanup=function():()
end
Module.Events={}
Module.Runtime=nil
end
return Module
-- FIN src/games/MM2/Other/AutoUnbox.lua

-- INICIO src/games/MM2/Other/AutoVote.lua
local Module={
Name="MM2 Auto Vote",
Events={}::{[string]:any},
Initialized=false,
Runtime=nil::any,
}
local activeCleanup:()->()=function():()end
function Module.init(runtime:any):any
if Module.Initialized then
return Module
end
local core:any=state.mm2Core
assert(type(core)=="table","MM2 Auto Vote requires the MM2 core module")
Module.Runtime=runtime
local TICK_INTERVAL:number=0.10
local MAP_REFRESH_INTERVAL:number=0.50
local VOTE_HOLD_BEFORE_RESET:number=0.22
local RESET_RETRY_INTERVAL:number=0.25
local MAX_LOBBY_PAD_DISTANCE:number=250
local DEFAULT_MAP_CHOICES:{string}={
"House 2",
"Hospital 3",
"Police Station",
}
local mapChoices:{string}={
DEFAULT_MAP_CHOICES[1],
DEFAULT_MAP_CHOICES[2],
DEFAULT_MAP_CHOICES[3],
}
local selectedSlot:number=1
local selectedMapName:string=mapChoices[1]
local selectMapButton:TextButton?=nil
local selectMapOptionKey:string="MM2.AutoVote.SelectMap"
local AutoVoteFeature:any=nil
local cachedLobby:Instance?=nil
local cachedPadModels:{[number]:Instance?}={}
local cachedVotePadsFolder:Instance?=nil
local cachedVoteIconsFolder:Instance?=nil
local lastLobbyCheckAt:number=-math.huge
local lastMapRefreshAt:number=-math.huge
local savedDetectorCFrames:{[BasePart]:CFrame}={}
local roundBlockingVote:boolean=false
local lastBillboardVotingState:boolean=false
local function trim(s:string):string
return(string.gsub(s,"^%s*(.-)%s*$","%1"))
end
local function isPlaceholderMapLabel(title:string?):boolean
if type(title)~="string"then
return true
end
local clean:string=string.upper(trim(title))
return clean==""
or clean=="MAP NAME"
or clean=="LOADING"
or clean=="NONE"
or clean=="MAP 1"
or clean=="MAP 2"
or clean=="MAP 3"
end
local function isValidMapTitle(title:string?):boolean
return not isPlaceholderMapLabel(title)
end
local function isLobbyContainer(candidate:Instance?):boolean
if not candidate or not candidate.Parent then
return false
end
return candidate:FindFirstChild("VotePad1")~=nil
or candidate:FindFirstChild("VotePads")~=nil
or candidate:FindFirstChild("VoteIcons")~=nil
end
local function findLobbyModel():Instance?
local now:number=os.clock()
if cachedLobby and cachedLobby:IsDescendantOf(workspace)and now-lastLobbyCheckAt<1.0 then
return cachedLobby
end
lastLobbyCheckAt=now
for _,name:string in ipairs({"RegularLobby","Lobby","MainLobby","HalloweenLobby"})do
local candidate:Instance?=workspace:FindFirstChild(name)
if isLobbyContainer(candidate)then
cachedLobby=candidate
return candidate
end
end
local okChildren:boolean,children:any=pcall(function():any
return workspace:GetChildren()
end)
if okChildren and type(children)=="table"then
for _,child:Instance in ipairs(children)do
if(child:IsA("Model")or child:IsA("Folder"))and isLobbyContainer(child)then
cachedLobby=child
return child
end
end
end
local okTagged:boolean,tagged:any=pcall(function():any
return game:GetService("CollectionService"):GetTagged("VotePad")
end)
if okTagged and type(tagged)=="table"then
for _,inst:Instance in ipairs(tagged)do
if inst and inst:IsDescendantOf(workspace)then
local slotNum:number?=tonumber(string.match(inst.Name,"^VotePad(%d)$"))
if slotNum then
cachedPadModels[slotNum]=inst
end
if inst.Parent and inst.Parent~=workspace then
cachedLobby=inst.Parent
end
end
end
if cachedLobby and cachedLobby:IsDescendantOf(workspace)then
return cachedLobby
end
end
local okDeep:boolean,deepPad:any=pcall(function():any
return workspace:FindFirstChild("VotePads",true)
or workspace:FindFirstChild("VotePad1",true)
or workspace:FindFirstChild("VoteIcons",true)
end)
if okDeep and deepPad and(deepPad::Instance):IsDescendantOf(workspace)then
local parent:Instance?=(deepPad::Instance).Parent
if parent and parent.Name=="VoteIcons"and parent.Parent then
parent=parent.Parent
end
cachedLobby=parent
return cachedLobby
end
cachedLobby=nil
return nil
end
local function getPadModel(lobby:Instance?,slot:number):Instance?
local cached:Instance?=cachedPadModels[slot]
if cached and cached:IsDescendantOf(workspace)and not(cached.Parent and cached.Parent.Name=="VoteIcons")then
return cached
end
local padName:string="VotePad"..tostring(slot)
if lobby then
local direct:Instance?=lobby:FindFirstChild(padName)
if direct and not(direct.Parent and direct.Parent.Name=="VoteIcons")then
cachedPadModels[slot]=direct
return direct
end
local okDeep:boolean,deep:any=pcall(function():any
return lobby:FindFirstChild(padName,true)
end)
if okDeep and deep and not((deep::Instance).Parent and(deep::Instance).Parent.Name=="VoteIcons")then
cachedPadModels[slot]=deep::Instance
return deep::Instance
end
end
return nil
end
local function getVoteIconsFolder(lobby:Instance?):Instance?
if cachedVoteIconsFolder and cachedVoteIconsFolder:IsDescendantOf(workspace)then
return cachedVoteIconsFolder
end
if lobby then
local direct:Instance?=lobby:FindFirstChild("VoteIcons")
or lobby:FindFirstChild("VoteIcons",true)
if direct then
cachedVoteIconsFolder=direct
return direct
end
end
return nil
end
local function getVotePadsFolder(lobby:Instance?):Instance?
if cachedVotePadsFolder and cachedVotePadsFolder:IsDescendantOf(workspace)then
return cachedVotePadsFolder
end
if lobby then
local direct:Instance?=lobby:FindFirstChild("VotePads")
or lobby:FindFirstChild("VotePads",true)
if direct then
cachedVotePadsFolder=direct
return direct
end
end
return nil
end
local function readSlotMapName(lobby:Instance?,slot:number):string?
if not lobby then
return nil
end
local padModel:Instance?=getPadModel(lobby,slot)
if padModel then
local voteInfoGui:Instance?=padModel:FindFirstChild("VoteInfoGui")
local container:Instance?=voteInfoGui and voteInfoGui:FindFirstChild("Container")
local mapLabel:Instance?=(container and container:FindFirstChild("MapName"))
or padModel:FindFirstChild("MapName",true)
if mapLabel and mapLabel:IsA("TextLabel")then
local text:string=(mapLabel::TextLabel).Text
if isValidMapTitle(text)then
return trim(text)
end
end
end
local voteIcons:Instance?=getVoteIconsFolder(lobby)
local iconPart:Instance?=voteIcons and voteIcons:FindFirstChild("VotePad"..tostring(slot))
if iconPart then
local mapLabel:Instance?=iconPart:FindFirstChild("MapName",true)
if mapLabel and mapLabel:IsA("TextLabel")then
local text:string=(mapLabel::TextLabel).Text
if isValidMapTitle(text)then
return trim(text)
end
end
local mapObj:Instance?=iconPart:FindFirstChild("Map")
if mapObj and mapObj:IsA("ObjectValue")then
local val:Instance?=(mapObj::ObjectValue).Value
if val and isValidMapTitle(val.Name)then
return trim(val.Name)
end
end
end
return nil
end
local function readSlotVoteCount(lobby:Instance?,slot:number):number?
if not lobby then
return nil
end
local voteIcons:Instance?=getVoteIconsFolder(lobby)
local iconPart:Instance?=voteIcons and voteIcons:FindFirstChild("VotePad"..tostring(slot))
if iconPart then
local votesVal:Instance?=iconPart:FindFirstChild("Votes")
if votesVal and votesVal:IsA("IntValue")then
return(votesVal::IntValue).Value
end
end
local padModel:Instance?=getPadModel(lobby,slot)
if padModel then
local voteInfoGui:Instance?=padModel:FindFirstChild("VoteInfoGui")
local container:Instance?=voteInfoGui and voteInfoGui:FindFirstChild("Container")
local votesLabel:Instance?=(container and container:FindFirstChild("Votes"))
or padModel:FindFirstChild("Votes",true)
if votesLabel and votesLabel:IsA("TextLabel")then
local numStr:string?=string.match((votesLabel::TextLabel).Text,"(%d+)")
if numStr then
return tonumber(numStr)
end
end
end
return nil
end
local function syncSelectMapControl():()
local control:any=state.valueControls
and state.valueControls[selectMapOptionKey]
if type(control)=="table"and type(control.setIndex)=="function"then
pcall(control.setIndex,selectedSlot)
elseif selectMapButton then
pcall(function():()
selectMapButton.Text=mapChoices[selectedSlot]
end)
end
end
local function refreshMapChoices(force:boolean?):()
local now:number=os.clock()
if not force and now-lastMapRefreshAt<MAP_REFRESH_INTERVAL then
return
end
lastMapRefreshAt=now
local lobby:Instance?=findLobbyModel()
local changed:boolean=false
for slot:number=1,3 do
local title:string?=readSlotMapName(lobby,slot)
local current:string?=mapChoices[slot]
local nextLabel:string=title
or(isValidMapTitle(current)and(current::string)or DEFAULT_MAP_CHOICES[slot])
if mapChoices[slot]~=nextLabel then
mapChoices[slot]=nextLabel
changed=true
end
end
if isValidMapTitle(selectedMapName)then
local matchedSlot:number?=nil
local targetLower:string=string.lower(selectedMapName)
for slot:number=1,3 do
if string.lower(mapChoices[slot])==targetLower then
matchedSlot=slot
break
end
end
if matchedSlot and selectedSlot~=matchedSlot then
selectedSlot=matchedSlot
changed=true
end
end
selectedSlot=math.clamp(selectedSlot,1,3)
selectedMapName=mapChoices[selectedSlot]
if changed or force then
syncSelectMapControl()
end
end
local function getSlotDetectorParts(lobby:Instance?,slot:number):{BasePart}
local parts:{BasePart}={}
if not lobby then
return parts
end
local padModel:Instance?=getPadModel(lobby,slot)
if padModel then
local padPart:Instance?=padModel:FindFirstChild("Pad")
or padModel:FindFirstChild("Pad",true)
if padPart and padPart:IsA("BasePart")then
parts[#parts+1]=padPart::BasePart
end
end
local votePads:Instance?=getVotePadsFolder(lobby)
if votePads then
local detector:Instance?=votePads:FindFirstChild("Detector"..tostring(slot))
or votePads:FindFirstChild("Detector"..tostring(slot),true)
if detector and detector:IsA("BasePart")then
parts[#parts+1]=detector::BasePart
end
end
return parts
end
local function rememberDetectorPart(part:BasePart):()
if not savedDetectorCFrames[part]then
savedDetectorCFrames[part]=part.CFrame
end
end
local function restoreAllDetectorParts():()
for part,cf in pairs(savedDetectorCFrames)do
if part and part.Parent then
pcall(function():()
part.CFrame=cf
end)
end
end
table.clear(savedDetectorCFrames)
end
local function areVotingBillboardsActive(lobby:Instance?):boolean
if not lobby then
return false
end
for slot:number=1,3 do
local padModel:Instance?=getPadModel(lobby,slot)
if padModel then
local voteGui:Instance?=padModel:FindFirstChild("VoteInfoGui")
if voteGui and voteGui:IsA("SurfaceGui")and(voteGui::SurfaceGui).Enabled==true then
return true
end
local mapGui:Instance?=padModel:FindFirstChild("MapInfoGui")
if mapGui and mapGui:IsA("SurfaceGui")and(mapGui::SurfaceGui).Enabled==true then
return true
end
end
local voteIcons:Instance?=getVoteIconsFolder(lobby)
local iconPart:Instance?=voteIcons and voteIcons:FindFirstChild("VotePad"..tostring(slot))
if iconPart then
local votingFlag:Instance?=iconPart:FindFirstChild("Voting")
if votingFlag and votingFlag:IsA("BoolValue")and(votingFlag::BoolValue).Value==true then
return true
end
local surface:Instance?=iconPart:FindFirstChild("Surface")
if surface and surface:IsA("SurfaceGui")and(surface::SurfaceGui).Enabled==true then
return true
end
local mapInfo:Instance?=iconPart:FindFirstChild("MapInfo")
local infoSurface:Instance?=mapInfo and mapInfo:FindFirstChild("Surface")
if infoSurface and infoSurface:IsA("SurfaceGui")and(infoSurface::SurfaceGui).Enabled==true then
return true
end
end
end
return false
end
local function isVotingIntermissionActive(root:BasePart?,detector:BasePart?):boolean
local lobby:Instance?=findLobbyModel()
if not lobby then
return false
end
local billboardsOn:boolean=areVotingBillboardsActive(lobby)
if billboardsOn and not lastBillboardVotingState then
roundBlockingVote=false
elseif not billboardsOn and lastBillboardVotingState then
roundBlockingVote=true
end
lastBillboardVotingState=billboardsOn
if roundBlockingVote then
return false
end
local timerPart:Instance?=workspace:FindFirstChild("RoundTimerPart")
local timerIndicatesLobby:boolean=false
if timerPart then
local okTime:boolean,roundTime:any=pcall(function():any
return timerPart:GetAttribute("Time")
end)
if okTime and type(roundTime)=="number"then
if roundTime>0 then
return false
end
timerIndicatesLobby=true
end
end
local isNearPad:boolean=false
if root and detector then
local okDist:boolean,dist:any=pcall(function():any
return(root.Position-detector.Position).Magnitude
end)
if okDist and type(dist)=="number"then
if dist>MAX_LOBBY_PAD_DISTANCE then
return false
end
isNearPad=true
end
end
if not isNearPad and type(core.isRoundLifecycleActive)=="function"
and core.isRoundLifecycleActive()==true then
return false
end
if billboardsOn then
return true
end
if isNearPad or timerIndicatesLobby then
for slot:number=1,3 do
if readSlotMapName(lobby,slot)~=nil then
return true
end
end
end
return false
end
local function resolveTargetSlot(lobby:Instance?):number
refreshMapChoices(false)
if lobby and isValidMapTitle(selectedMapName)then
local targetLower:string=string.lower(selectedMapName)
for slot:number=1,3 do
local nameOnBoard:string?=readSlotMapName(lobby,slot)
if nameOnBoard and string.lower(nameOnBoard)==targetLower then
selectedSlot=slot
return slot
end
end
end
return math.clamp(selectedSlot,1,3)
end
local function pulseVotePad(root:BasePart,detectors:{BasePart}):()
local primaryPad:BasePart?=detectors[1]
if primaryPad then
local saved:CFrame?=savedDetectorCFrames[primaryPad]
local padPos:Vector3=if saved then saved.Position else primaryPad.Position
pcall(function():()
local currentCF:CFrame=root.CFrame
local rotOnly:CFrame=currentCF-currentCF.Position
root.CFrame=CFrame.new(padPos)*rotOnly
root.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.zero
end)
end
local fireTouch:any=state.fireTouchInterest
local rootCF:CFrame=root.CFrame
for _,part:BasePart in ipairs(detectors)do
rememberDetectorPart(part)
pcall(function():()
part.CanCollide=false
part.CFrame=rootCF
end)
if type(fireTouch)=="function"then
pcall(fireTouch,root,part,0)
pcall(fireTouch,root,part,1)
end
end
end
local function resetLocalCharacter(character:Model,humanoid:Humanoid):()
pcall(function():()
humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
end)
pcall(function():()
humanoid.Health=0
end)
pcall(function():()
if humanoid.Health>0 then
character:BreakJoints()
end
end)
end
local lastStatus:string?=nil
local function setStatus(text:string?):()
if text==lastStatus then
return
end
lastStatus=text
if AutoVoteFeature then
AutoVoteFeature:SetStatus(text)
end
end
local function connectGameplayRemotes():()
local okRs:boolean,rs:any=pcall(function():any
return game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
end)
if not okRs or not rs then
return
end
local remotes:Instance?=rs:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
local gameplay:Instance?=remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Gameplay")
if not gameplay then
return
end
for _,stopName:string in ipairs({
"LoadingMap",
"Fade",
"RoleSelect",
"ShowRoleSelect",
"ShowRoleSelectNew",
"RoundStart",
"CoinsStarted",
})do
local rem:Instance?=gameplay:FindFirstChild(stopName)
if rem and rem:IsA("RemoteEvent")then
local key:string="MM2AutoVoteStop_"..stopName
disconnectFeatureConnection(key)
featureConnections[key]=(rem::RemoteEvent).OnClientEvent:Connect(function():()
roundBlockingVote=true
restoreAllDetectorParts()
end)
end
end
for _,endName:string in ipairs({
"RoundEndFade",
"VictoryScreen",
"GameOver",
})do
local rem:Instance?=gameplay:FindFirstChild(endName)
if rem and rem:IsA("RemoteEvent")then
local key:string="MM2AutoVoteEnd_"..endName
disconnectFeatureConnection(key)
featureConnections[key]=(rem::RemoteEvent).OnClientEvent:Connect(function():()
roundBlockingVote=false
refreshMapChoices(true)
end)
end
end
end
local function disconnectAllAutoVoteConnections():()
disconnectFeatureConnection("MM2AutoVote")
for key in pairs(featureConnections)do
if string.sub(key,1,11)=="MM2AutoVote"and key~="MM2AutoVoteMapSync"then
disconnectFeatureConnection(key)
end
end
end
local function toggleAutoVote(enabled:boolean):()
disconnectAllAutoVoteConnections()
restoreAllDetectorParts()
if not enabled then
setStatus(nil)
return
end
roundBlockingVote=false
lastBillboardVotingState=false
refreshMapChoices(true)
connectGameplayRemotes()
local lastTickAt:number=-math.huge
local currentChar:Model?=nil
local firstTouchAt:number=0
local lastResetAttemptAt:number=0
local voteCountBeforeTouch:number?=nil
local function autoVoteTick():()
local now:number=os.clock()
if now-lastTickAt<TICK_INTERVAL then
return
end
lastTickAt=now
refreshMapChoices(false)
local lobby:Instance?=findLobbyModel()
local slot:number=resolveTargetSlot(lobby)
local targetMapLabel:string=mapChoices[slot]or DEFAULT_MAP_CHOICES[slot]
local detectors:{BasePart}=getSlotDetectorParts(lobby,slot)
local primaryDetector:BasePart?=detectors[1]
local character:Model?=LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character
local root:BasePart?=character
and(character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")::BasePart?)
local humanoid:Humanoid?=character
and(character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")::Humanoid?)
if not isVotingIntermissionActive(root,primaryDetector)then
restoreAllDetectorParts()
firstTouchAt=0
lastResetAttemptAt=0
setStatus("Waiting for vote · "..tostring(targetMapLabel))
return
end
if#detectors==0 then
setStatus("No vote pad")
return
end
if not character or not root or not humanoid or humanoid.Health<=0 then
restoreAllDetectorParts()
return
end
if character~=currentChar then
currentChar=character
firstTouchAt=0
lastResetAttemptAt=0
voteCountBeforeTouch=readSlotVoteCount(lobby,slot)
end
pulseVotePad(root,detectors)
if firstTouchAt==0 then
firstTouchAt=now
end
local currentVotes:number?=readSlotVoteCount(lobby,slot)
local voteIncremented:boolean=(
currentVotes~=nil
and voteCountBeforeTouch~=nil
and currentVotes>voteCountBeforeTouch
)
setStatus(
"Voting "..targetMapLabel
..(currentVotes~=nil and(" ("..tostring(currentVotes)..")")or"")
)
if(voteIncremented or(now-firstTouchAt>=VOTE_HOLD_BEFORE_RESET))
and(now-lastResetAttemptAt>=RESET_RETRY_INTERVAL)then
restoreAllDetectorParts()
if isVotingIntermissionActive(root,primaryDetector)then
lastResetAttemptAt=now
resetLocalCharacter(character,humanoid)
end
end
end
featureConnections.MM2AutoVote=TaskManager:Connect(autoVoteTick,nil,"MM2 Auto Vote")
autoVoteTick()
end
pcall(function():()
if type(configData)=="table"and type(configData.values)=="table"then
local saved:any=configData.values[selectMapOptionKey]
if isPlaceholderMapLabel(saved)then
configData.values[selectMapOptionKey]=nil
end
end
end)
pcall(refreshMapChoices,true)
AutoVoteFeature=createUniversalFeature(
"Auto Vote",
"Detects the 3 maps on the lobby voting billboard, votes on the wooden floor pad for your selected map, and resets repeatedly to stack votes until voting intermission ends.",
7,
toggleAutoVote,
{
categoryName="Utilities",
parent=MM2Scroll,
registry=mm2Features,
}
)
state.mm2AutoVoteFeature=AutoVoteFeature
selectMapButton=addCycleOption(
AutoVoteFeature,
"Select Map",
mapChoices,
1,
function(value:string):()
refreshMapChoices(false)
selectedMapName=tostring(value)
for slot:number=1,3 do
if mapChoices[slot]==selectedMapName then
selectedSlot=slot
break
end
end
if AutoVoteFeature and AutoVoteFeature.enabled then
setStatus("Voting "..tostring(mapChoices[selectedSlot]or selectedMapName))
end
end,
"Select which of the 3 lobby billboard maps to vote for."
)
pcall(function():()
local control:any=state.valueControls
and state.valueControls[selectMapOptionKey]
if type(control)=="table"then
control.onBeforeOpen=function():()
refreshMapChoices(true)
end
end
end)
local lastBgSyncAt:number=-math.huge
disconnectFeatureConnection("MM2AutoVoteMapSync")
featureConnections.MM2AutoVoteMapSync=TaskManager:Connect(function():()
local now:number=os.clock()
if now-lastBgSyncAt<1.0 then
return
end
lastBgSyncAt=now
refreshMapChoices(false)
end,nil,"Auto Vote sync")
activeCleanup=function():()
disconnectFeatureConnection("MM2AutoVoteMapSync")
toggleAutoVote(false)
end
Module.Events=featureConnections
Module.Initialized=true
return Module
end
function Module.destroy():()
if not Module.Initialized then
return
end
Module.Initialized=false
pcall(activeCleanup)
activeCleanup=function():()end
Module.Events={}
Module.Runtime=nil
end
return Module
-- FIN src/games/MM2/Other/AutoVote.lua

-- INICIO src/games/MM2/Other/AutoWin.lua

then return Module end local core:any=state.mm2Core assert(type(core)=='table','MM2 Coins Aura requires the MM2 core module')Module.Runtime=runtime local MAX_SERVER_REACH:number=15 local return end local cf:CFrame?=savedCFrames[serverPart]if cf and serverPart.Parent then pcall(function():()serverPart.CFrame=cf end)end if activeCoin==serverPart then activeCoin=nil end end local fireTouch:any=state.fireTouchInterest if type(fireTouch)=='function'then pcall(fireTouch,root,serverPart,0)pcall(fireTouch,root,serverPart,1)end end local CoinsAuraFeature:any=nil local function not candidate then return end local serverPart:BasePart?=nil if candidate:IsA('BasePart')then if candidate.Name=='Coin_Server'or candidate.Name=='ServerCoinPart'then serverPart=candidate::BasePart elseif candidate.Parent and candidate.Parent:IsA('BasePart')and(candidate.Parent.Name=='Coin_Server'or candidate.Parent.Name=='ServerCoinPart')then serverPart=candidate.Parent::BasePart else serverPart=candidate::BasePart end elseif candidate:IsA('Model')then local child:Instance?=candidate:FindFirstChild('Coin_Server')or candidate:FindFirstChildWhichIsA('BasePart')if child and child:IsA('BasePart')then serverPart=child::BasePart end end if serverPart and not seen[serverPart]and isCoinCollectable(serverPart)then seen[serverPart]=true _,pickup:Instance in ipairs(pickups)do addCoin(pickup)end end end cachedCoins=list end local function auraTick():()local now:number=os.clock()if now-lastTickAt<TICK_INTERVAL then return end restoreCoin(activeCoin)end return end refreshCoins(now)local rootPos:Vector3=root.Position local bestCoin:BasePart?=nil local bestDistSq:number=MAX_SERVER_REACH_SQ for i:number=1,#cachedCoins do local coin:BasePart=cachedCoins[i]if isCoinCollectable(coin)then local coinPos:Vector3?=savedPositions[coin]or rememberCoin(coin)if coinPos then local diff:Vector3=coinPos-rootPos local distSq:number=diff.X*diff.X+diff.Y*diff.Y+diff.Z*diff.Z if distSq<=bestDistSq then bestDistSq=distSq bestCoin=coin end end elseif coin==activeCoin then restoreCoin(coin)end end if activeCoin and activeCoin~=bestCoin then restoreCoin(activeCoin)end if bestCoin then activeCoin=bestCoin pcall(pulseCoinTouch,root,bestCoin)end end -- INICIO src/games/MM2/Other/GetAllEmotes.lua
local Module={
Name="MM2 Get All Emotes",
Events={}::{[string]:any},
Initialized=false,
Runtime=nil::any,
}
local activeCleanup:()->()=function():()end
function Module.init(runtime:any):any
if Module.Initialized then
return Module
end
local core:any=state.mm2Core
assert(type(core)=="table","MM2 Get All Emotes requires the MM2 core module")
Module.Runtime=runtime
type EmoteDef={
id:string,
label:string,
command:string,
animationId:string,
looped:boolean?
}
local EMOTES:{EmoteDef}={
{id="sit",label="Sit",command="sit2",animationId="rbxassetid://2431845940",looped=true},
{id="ninja",label="Ninja Rest",command="ninja",animationId="rbxassetid://2431864798",looped=true},
{id="zen",label="Zen",command="zen",animationId="rbxassetid://2431812646",looped=true},
{id="dab",label="Dab",command="dab",animationId="rbxassetid://2445521505"},
{id="floss",label="Floss",command="floss",animationId="rbxassetid://2452938820",looped=true},
{id="zombie",label="Zombie",command="zombie",animationId="rbxassetid://2513692312",looped=true},
{id="headless",label="Headless",command="headless",animationId="rbxassetid://2513664073"},
}
local LABELS:{string}={}
local ID_BY_LABEL:{[string]:string}={}
local KNOWN_IDS:{[string]:boolean}={}
local DEFAULT_IDS:{[string]:boolean}={
back=true,wave=true,cheer=true,laugh=true,point=true,
dance=true,dance1=true,dance2=true,dance3=true,
}
for _,def:EmoteDef in ipairs(EMOTES)do
table.insert(LABELS,def.label)
ID_BY_LABEL[def.label]=def.id
KNOWN_IDS[def.id]=true
end
local selectedLabel:string="Floss"
local selectionTouched:boolean=false
local activeTrack:any=nil
local feature:any=nil
local generation:number=0
local grantInFlight:boolean=false
local catalogInFlight:boolean=false
local activated:boolean=false
local function stopActiveTrack():()
if activeTrack then
pcall(function():()activeTrack:Stop(0.1)end)
activeTrack=nil
end
end
local function animationUri(value:any):string?
if type(value)=="number"and value>0 and value<math.huge then
return"rbxassetid://"..string.format("%.0f",value)
end
if type(value)=="string"and value~=""then
return string.match(value,"^%d+$")and("rbxassetid://"..value)or value
end
return nil
end
local function discoverLiveEmotes(ticket:number):()
local rs:Instance=game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local db:Instance?=rs:FindFirstChild("Database")
local sync:Instance?=db and db:FindFirstChild("Sync")
local emotesModule:Instance?=sync and sync:FindFirstChild("Emotes")
if not emotesModule then return end
local ok:boolean,catalog:any=pcall(require,emotesModule)
if ticket~=generation or not Module.Initialized
or not ok or type(catalog)~="table"then
return
end
local extra:{string}={}
for id:any,live:any in pairs(catalog)do
if type(id)=="string"and not KNOWN_IDS[id]
and not DEFAULT_IDS[string.lower(id)]and type(live)=="table"
and type(live.Command)=="string"and live.Command~=""
and not DEFAULT_IDS[string.lower(live.Command)]
and animationUri(live.AnimationID)then
table.insert(extra,id)
end
end
table.sort(extra)
for _,id:string in ipairs(extra)do
local live:any=catalog[id]
local label:string=type(live.Name)=="string"and live.Name~=""and live.Name or id
if ID_BY_LABEL[label]then label..=" ("..id..")"end
local def:EmoteDef={
id=id,
label=label,
command=live.Command,
animationId=animationUri(live.AnimationID)::string,
looped=live.Loop==true,
}
table.insert(EMOTES,def)
table.insert(LABELS,label)
ID_BY_LABEL[label]=id
KNOWN_IDS[id]=true
end
local values:any=type(configData)=="table"and configData.values or nil
local saved:any=type(values)=="table"and values["MM2.GetAllEmotes.SelectEmote"]or nil
local index:number?=type(saved)=="string"and table.find(LABELS,saved)or nil
if not selectionTouched and index and ID_BY_LABEL[saved]then
selectedLabel=saved
local control:any=state.valueControls and state.valueControls["MM2.GetAllEmotes.SelectEmote"]
if control and type(control.setIndex)=="function"then
control.setIndex(index)
end
end
end
local function emoteDefinition(id:string):EmoteDef?
local result:EmoteDef?=nil
for _,def:EmoteDef in ipairs(EMOTES)do
if def.id==id then
result=table.clone(def)
break
end
end
if not result then
return nil
end
pcall(function():()
local rs:Instance=game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local db:Instance?=rs:FindFirstChild("Database")
local sync:Instance?=db and db:FindFirstChild("Sync")
local emotesModule:Instance?=sync and sync:FindFirstChild("Emotes")
if not emotesModule then return end
local ok:boolean,catalog:any=pcall(require,emotesModule)
local live:any=ok and type(catalog)=="table"and catalog[id]or nil
if type(live)~="table"then return end
local def:EmoteDef=result::EmoteDef
if type(live.Command)=="string"and live.Command~=""then
def.command=live.Command
end
local asset:string?=animationUri(live.AnimationID)
if asset then def.animationId=asset end
def.looped=live.Loop==true
end)
return result
end
local function refreshLegacyGui(rs:Instance,owned:{string}):boolean
local globals:any=_G::any
local gui:any=globals and globals.EmoteFrame
if not gui or type(globals.EmoteController)~="table"then return false end
local valid:boolean,pages:any=pcall(function():any
return gui:FindFirstChild("EmotePages")
end)
if not valid or not pages then return false end
local modules:Instance?=rs:FindFirstChild("Modules")
local scriptModule:Instance?=modules and modules:FindFirstChild("EmoteModule")
if not scriptModule then return false end
local ok:boolean,emoteModule:any=pcall(require,scriptModule)
if not ok or type(emoteModule)~="table"
or type(emoteModule.GenerateEmotes)~="function"then
return false
end
emoteModule.EmoteGUI=gui
return pcall(emoteModule.GenerateEmotes,owned,gui)
end
local function grantShopEmotes(ticket:number):(boolean,number,boolean)
if ticket~=generation or not Module.Initialized then return false,0,false end
local ok:boolean,ready:any,added:any,refreshed:any=pcall(function():(boolean,number,boolean)
local rs:Instance=game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local modules:Instance?=rs:FindFirstChild("Modules")
local profileModule:Instance?=modules and modules:FindFirstChild("ProfileData")
if not profileModule then return false,0,false end
local profileOk:boolean,profile:any=pcall(require,profileModule)
if ticket~=generation or not Module.Initialized then return false,0,false end
if not profileOk or type(profile)~="table"
or type(profile.Emotes)~="table"
or type(profile.Emotes.Owned)~="table"then
return false,0,false
end
local owned:{string}=profile.Emotes.Owned
local known:{[string]:boolean}={}
for _,existing:any in ipairs(owned)do
if type(existing)=="string"then known[existing]=true end
end
local newIds:{string}={}
for _,def:EmoteDef in ipairs(EMOTES)do
if not known[def.id]then
table.insert(owned,def.id)
known[def.id]=true
table.insert(newIds,def.id)
end
end
local remotes:Instance?=rs:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
local inventory:Instance?=remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Inventory")
local changed:any=inventory and inventory:FindFirstChild("InventoryDataChanged")
local signalled:boolean=false
if changed then
local wakeIds:{string}=#newIds>0 and newIds or{EMOTES[1].id}
for _,id:string in ipairs(wakeIds)do
local fired:boolean=pcall(function():()
changed:Fire("Emotes",id,1)
end)
signalled=signalled or fired
end
end
if not signalled then
signalled=refreshLegacyGui(rs,owned)
end
return true,#newIds,signalled
end)
if not ok then return false,0,false end
return ready==true,tonumber(added)or 0,refreshed==true
end
local function requestGrant(showNotification:boolean):()
if grantInFlight then
if showNotification and type(notify)=="function"then
pcall(notify,"Get All Emotes: waiting for the MM2 profile.")
end
return
end
grantInFlight=true
local ticket:number=generation
task.spawn(function():()
local ready:boolean,added:number,refreshed:boolean=grantShopEmotes(ticket)
if ticket~=generation or not Module.Initialized then return end
grantInFlight=false
if not ready then
if feature then feature:SetStatus("profile not ready")end
if showNotification and type(notify)=="function"then
pcall(notify,"Get All Emotes: MM2 profile unavailable or not ready; try again after the game loads.")
end
return
end
local count:number=#EMOTES
if feature then
feature:SetStatus(refreshed and(tostring(count).." local emotes")
or(tostring(count).." local · UI not refreshed"))
end
if showNotification and type(notify)=="function"then
local suffix:string=refreshed
and"The emote UI was notified."
or"The MM2 emote UI could not be notified; try again after it loads."
pcall(notify,string.format(
"Get All Emotes: %d local entries (%d new). %s Server ownership is not verified.",
count,added,suffix
))
end
if not catalogInFlight then
catalogInFlight=true
task.spawn(function():()
if ticket~=generation or not Module.Initialized then
catalogInFlight=false
return
end
local previous:number=#EMOTES
pcall(discoverLiveEmotes,ticket)
catalogInFlight=false
if ticket==generation and Module.Initialized and#EMOTES>previous then
requestGrant(false)
end
end)
end
end)
end
local function activate():()
activated=true
if featureConnections.MM2GetAllEmotesRespawn or not(LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded)then
return
end
featureConnections.MM2GetAllEmotesRespawn=LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function():()
local ticket:number=generation
task.delay(0.5,function():()
if ticket==generation and Module.Initialized and activated then
requestGrant(false)
end
end)
end)
end
local function requestEmotePlayback(id:string):boolean
stopActiveTrack()
local def:EmoteDef?=emoteDefinition(id)
if not def then return false end
local fired:boolean=false
pcall(function():()
local rs:Instance=game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local remotes:Instance?=rs:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
local misc:Instance?=remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Misc")
local event:any=misc and misc:FindFirstChild("PlayEmote")
if event then
fired=pcall(function():()event:Fire(id)end)
end
end)
if fired then return true end
if def.animationId==""then return false end
local played:boolean=false
pcall(function():()
local character:Model?=LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character
local humanoid:Humanoid?=character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not humanoid or humanoid.Health<=0 then return end
local anim:Animation=Instance.new("Animation")
anim.Name=def.command
anim.AnimationId=def.animationId
local animator:Animator?=humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
local loaded:boolean,track:any=pcall(function():any
return if animator then animator:LoadAnimation(anim)
else(humanoid::any):LoadAnimation(anim)
end)
if loaded and track then
activeTrack=track
played=pcall(function():()
track.Looped=def.looped==true
track:Play(0.1)
end)
end
anim:Destroy()
end)
return played
end
local function playSelected():()
activate()
requestGrant(false)
local id:string=ID_BY_LABEL[selectedLabel]or"floss"
if not requestEmotePlayback(id)and type(notify)=="function"then
pcall(notify,"Could not request that emote; check the character and animation permissions.")
end
end
feature=createUniversalFeature(
"Get All Emotes",
"Adds MM2's playable non-default emotes locally, including Sit, Ninja Rest, Zen, Dab, Floss, Zombie and Headless. "
.."It does not grant server ownership; playback and replication depend on the game.",
11,
function():()
activate()
requestGrant(true)
end,
{
categoryName="Utilities",
parent=MM2Scroll,
registry=mm2Features,
action=true,
noOptions=false,
configKey="MM2.GetAllEmotes",
}
)
state.mm2GetAllEmotesFeature=feature
local cycleRestored:boolean=false
addCycleOption(feature,"Select Emote",LABELS,5,function(value:string):()
selectedLabel=value
if not cycleRestored then
cycleRestored=true
return
end
selectionTouched=true
playSelected()
end,"Select and request an MM2 emote; server ownership is not changed.")
addActionOption(feature,"Play Emote",playSelected,"Request the selected emote again.")
addInformationOption(feature,
"MM2's playable emotes are added locally. Existing inventory is preserved; avatar defaults "
.."are not added, and the server controls ownership and replication.")
activeCleanup=function():()
generation+=1
activated=false
disconnectFeatureConnection("MM2GetAllEmotesRespawn")
stopActiveTrack()
if state.mm2GetAllEmotesFeature==feature then
state.mm2GetAllEmotesFeature=nil
end
end
Module.Events=featureConnections
Module.Initialized=true
return Module
end
function Module.destroy():()
if not Module.Initialized then return end
Module.Initialized=false
pcall(activeCleanup)
activeCleanup=function():()end
Module.Events={}
Module.Runtime=nil
end
return Module
-- FIN src/games/MM2/Other/GetAllEmotes.lua

-- INICIO src/games/MM2/Other/GunAura.lua
local Module={
Name="MM2 Gun Aura",
Events={}::{[string]:any},
Initialized=false,
Runtime=nil::any,
}
local activeCleanup:()->()=function():()end
function Module.init(runtime:any):any
if Module.Initialized then
return Module
end
local core:any=state.mm2Core
assert(type(core)=="table","MM2 Gun Aura requires the MM2 core module")
Module.Runtime=runtime
local settings={
studs=25,
showHitbox=true,
}
local TICK_INTERVAL:number=0.08
local SCAN_INTERVAL:number=0.4
local ESP_INTERVAL:number=0.5
local gunAuraActive=false
local gunHitboxes:{[Instance]:BasePart}={}
local espParts:{[Player]:BasePart}={}
local savedGunStates:{[BasePart]:{cframe:CFrame,canCollide:boolean}}={}
local trackedDrops:{[Instance]:boolean}={}
local lastScanAt:number=-math.huge
local lastTickAt:number=-math.huge
local lastEspAt:number=-math.huge
local function resolveGunTouchPart(gun:Instance?):BasePart?
if not gun then
return nil
end
if gun:IsA("BasePart")then
return gun::BasePart
end
local handle:Instance?=gun:FindFirstChild("Handle")
or gun:FindFirstChild("GunDrop")
or gun:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
if handle and handle:IsA("BasePart")then
return handle::BasePart
end
return nil
end
local function rememberGunPartState(part:BasePart):{cframe:CFrame,canCollide:boolean}
local existing=savedGunStates[part]
if existing then
return existing
end
local snap={cframe=part.CFrame,canCollide=part.CanCollide}
savedGunStates[part]=snap
return snap
end
local function restoreGunPartState(part:BasePart):()
local snap=savedGunStates[part]
if not snap then
return
end
if part and part.Parent then
pcall(function():()
part.CFrame=snap.cframe
part.CanCollide=snap.canCollide
end)
end
savedGunStates[part]=nil
end
local function restoreAllGunPartStates():()
for part in pairs(savedGunStates)do
restoreGunPartState(part)
end
table.clear(savedGunStates)
end
local function trueGunPartPosition(part:BasePart):Vector3
local snap=savedGunStates[part]
return if snap then snap.cframe.Position else part.Position
end
local function canGrabGun():boolean
local character=LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character
if not character then
return false
end
local humanoid=character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not humanoid or humanoid.Health<=0 then
return false
end
if type(core.getPlayerRole)=="function"then
local role=core.getPlayerRole(LocalPlayer)
if role=="Murderer"or role=="Sheriff"or role=="Hero"then
return false
end
end
local backpack=LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
if(backpack and(backpack:FindFirstChild("Gun")or backpack:FindFirstChild("Knife")))
or character:FindFirstChild("Gun")
or character:FindFirstChild("Knife")then
return false
end
return true
end
local function isValidGunDrop(obj:Instance?):boolean
if not obj or not obj.Parent then
return false
end
if obj.Name=="GunDrop"and(obj:IsA("BasePart")or obj:IsA("Model"))then
return resolveGunTouchPart(obj)~=nil
end
return false
end
local function createGunHitbox(gun:Instance):BasePart?
local handle=resolveGunTouchPart(gun)
if not handle then
return nil
end
rememberGunPartState(handle)
local pos=trueGunPartPosition(handle)
if not pos then
return nil
end
local part=Instance.new("Part")
part.Name="WurstGunAuraHitbox"
part.Size=Vector3.new(settings.studs,settings.studs,settings.studs)
part.Transparency=0.65
part.BrickColor=BrickColor.new("White")
part.Material=Enum.Material.Neon
part.CanCollide=false
part.CanQuery=false
part.CanTouch=false
part.Anchored=true
part.CFrame=CFrame.new(pos)
part.Parent=workspace
return part
end
local function refreshTrackedDrops(now:number):()
if now-lastScanAt<SCAN_INTERVAL then
return
end
lastScanAt=now
local wsDrop:Instance?=workspace:FindFirstChild("GunDrop")
if wsDrop and isValidGunDrop(wsDrop)then
trackedDrops[wsDrop]=true
end
local okChildren:boolean,children:any=pcall(function():any
return workspace:GetChildren()
end)
if okChildren and type(children)=="table"then
for _,child:Instance in ipairs(children)do
if child.Name=="GunDrop"and isValidGunDrop(child)then
trackedDrops[child]=true
elseif child:IsA("Model")or child:IsA("Folder")then
local gd:Instance?=child:FindFirstChild("GunDrop")
if gd and isValidGunDrop(gd)then
trackedDrops[gd]=true
end
end
end
end
if type(core.findDroppedGun)=="function"then
local okCore:boolean,drop:any=pcall(core.findDroppedGun)
if okCore and drop and isValidGunDrop(drop)then
trackedDrops[drop]=true
end
end
end
local function isWithinAura(truePos:Vector3,rootPos:Vector3):boolean
local diff=rootPos-truePos
local radius=settings.studs
if diff.X*diff.X+diff.Y*diff.Y+diff.Z*diff.Z<=radius*radius then
return true
end
local half=radius*0.5
return math.abs(diff.X)<=half
and math.abs(diff.Y)<=math.max(half,8)
and math.abs(diff.Z)<=half
end
local function tryGrabGun(root:BasePart,handle:BasePart):()
rememberGunPartState(handle)
handle.CanCollide=false
local rootCF:CFrame?=root.CFrame
if rootCF then
handle.CFrame=rootCF
end
local fireTouch:any=state.fireTouchInterest
if type(fireTouch)=="function"then
pcall(fireTouch,root,handle,0)
pcall(fireTouch,root,handle,1)
end
end
local function clearAllHitboxes():()
for gun,hitbox in pairs(gunHitboxes)do
if hitbox and hitbox.Parent then
pcall(function():()
hitbox:Destroy()
end)
end
gunHitboxes[gun]=nil
end
end
local function clearAllEsp():()
for player,esp in pairs(espParts)do
if esp and esp.Parent then
pcall(function():()
esp:Destroy()
end)
end
espParts[player]=nil
end
end
local function updateSheriffESP(player:Player):()
local character=player.Character
local root=character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local humanoid=character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not character or not root or not root:IsA("BasePart")
or not humanoid or humanoid.Health>0 then
local existing=espParts[player]
if existing then
if existing.Parent then
pcall(function():()
existing:Destroy()
end)
end
espParts[player]=nil
end
return
end
local esp=espParts[player]
if not esp or not esp.Parent then
esp=Instance.new("Part")
esp.Name="WurstDeadSheriffESP_"..player.Name
esp.Size=Vector3.new(3,3,3)
esp.Transparency=0.3
esp.BrickColor=BrickColor.new("White")
esp.Material=Enum.Material.Neon
esp.CanCollide=false
esp.CanQuery=false
esp.CanTouch=false
esp.Anchored=true
esp.Parent=workspace
espParts[player]=esp
end
esp.CFrame=(root::BasePart).CFrame
end
local GunAuraFeature:any=nil
local lastStatus:string?=nil
local function setStatus(text:string?):()
if text==lastStatus then
return
end
lastStatus=text
if GunAuraFeature then
GunAuraFeature:SetStatus(text)
end
end
local function gunAuraTick():()
if not gunAuraActive then
return
end
local now:number=os.clock()
if now-lastTickAt<TICK_INTERVAL then
return
end
lastTickAt=now
refreshTrackedDrops(now)
local character=LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character
local root:BasePart?=character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")::BasePart?
local canGrab=canGrabGun()
local activeGuns:{[Instance]:boolean}={}
local touchedParts:{[BasePart]:boolean}={}
local gunCount:number=0
for gun in pairs(trackedDrops)do
if isValidGunDrop(gun)then
local handle:BasePart?=resolveGunTouchPart(gun)
if handle then
gunCount+=1
activeGuns[gun]=true
rememberGunPartState(handle)
local truePos:Vector3?=trueGunPartPosition(handle)
if settings.showHitbox then
local hitbox=gunHitboxes[gun]
if not hitbox or not hitbox.Parent then
hitbox=createGunHitbox(gun)
if hitbox then
gunHitboxes[gun]=hitbox
end
elseif truePos then
local targetSize=Vector3.new(settings.studs,settings.studs,settings.studs)
if hitbox.Size~=targetSize then
hitbox.Size=targetSize
end
hitbox.CFrame=CFrame.new(truePos)
end
end
if canGrab and root and truePos and isWithinAura(truePos,root.Position)then
touchedParts[handle]=true
pcall(tryGrabGun,root,handle)
end
end
else
trackedDrops[gun]=nil
end
end
for part in pairs(savedGunStates)do
if not touchedParts[part]then
restoreGunPartState(part)
end
end
for gun,hitbox in pairs(gunHitboxes)do
if not activeGuns[gun]then
if hitbox and hitbox.Parent then
pcall(function():()
hitbox:Destroy()
end)
end
gunHitboxes[gun]=nil
end
end
if gunCount>0 then
setStatus("Gun detected · "..tostring(math.round(settings.studs)).." studs")
else
setStatus(tostring(math.round(settings.studs)).." studs")
end
if now-lastEspAt>=ESP_INTERVAL and type(core.getPlayerRole)=="function"then
lastEspAt=now
local seenSheriffs:{[Player]:boolean}={}
for _,player in ipairs(game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayers())do
if player~=LocalPlayer then
local role=core.getPlayerRole(player)
if role=="Sheriff"then
seenSheriffs[player]=true
updateSheriffESP(player)
end
end
end
for player,esp in pairs(espParts)do
if not seenSheriffs[player]then
if esp and esp.Parent then
pcall(function():()
esp:Destroy()
end)
end
espParts[player]=nil
end
end
end
end
local function toggleGunAura(enabled:boolean):()
gunAuraActive=enabled
state.mm2GunAuraEnabled=enabled==true
disconnectFeatureConnection("MM2GunAura")
disconnectFeatureConnection("MM2GunAuraDescendant")
if not enabled then
clearAllHitboxes()
clearAllEsp()
restoreAllGunPartStates()
table.clear(trackedDrops)
setStatus(nil)
return
end
lastScanAt=-math.huge
lastTickAt=-math.huge
lastEspAt=-math.huge
if workspace.DescendantAdded then
featureConnections.MM2GunAuraDescendant=workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj:Instance):()
if obj.Name=="GunDrop"then
trackedDrops[obj]=true
end
end)
end
setStatus(tostring(math.round(settings.studs)).." studs")
featureConnections.MM2GunAura=
TaskManager:Connect(gunAuraTick,nil,"MM2 Gun Aura")
gunAuraTick()
end
GunAuraFeature=createUniversalFeature(
"Gun Aura",
"Detects dropped guns in the map with event-driven tracking, draws a white aura box around them, and automatically grabs the gun within Studs range.",
7,
toggleGunAura,
{
categoryName="Utilities",
parent=MM2Scroll,
registry=mm2Features,
}
)
state.mm2GunAuraFeature=GunAuraFeature
addNumberOption(GunAuraFeature,"Studs",25,5,250,
function(value:number):()
settings.studs=math.clamp(tonumber(value)or 25,5,250)
if GunAuraFeature.enabled then
setStatus(tostring(math.round(settings.studs)).." studs")
end
end,
"Distance in studs around each dropped gun to automatically grab it.",1)
addToggleOption(GunAuraFeature,"Show Hitbox",true,
function(value:boolean):()
settings.showHitbox=value==true
if not settings.showHitbox then
clearAllHitboxes()
end
end,
"Shows or hides the white aura hitbox square around dropped guns.")
activeCleanup=function():()
toggleGunAura(false)
end
Module.Events=featureConnections
Module.Initialized=true
return Module
end
function Module.destroy():()
if not Module.Initialized then
return
end
Module.Initialized=false
pcall(activeCleanup)
activeCleanup=function():()end
Module.Events={}
Module.Runtime=nil
end
return Module
-- FIN src/games/MM2/Other/GunAura.lua

-- INICIO src/games/MM2/Other/Notifications.lua
local Module={
Name="MM2 Notifications",
Events={}::{[string]:any},
Initialized=false,
Runtime=nil::any,
}
local activeCleanup:()->()=function():()end
function Module.init(runtime:any):any
if Module.Initialized then return Module end
local core:any=state.mm2Core
assert(type(core)=="table","MM2 Notifications requires the MM2 core")
Module.Runtime=runtime
if type(configData)=="table"and type(configData.states)=="table"then
local states:any=configData.states
local oldKey:string="MM2.BlurtRoles.InstantRoles"
local newKey:string="MM2.Notifications.InstantRoles"
local old:any=states[oldKey]
local changed:boolean=false
if states[newKey]==nil and type(old)=="boolean"then
states[newKey]=old
if old and states["MM2.Notifications"]==nil then
states["MM2.Notifications"]=true
end
changed=true
end
if old~=nil then states[oldKey]=nil;changed=true end
if changed and type(queueConfigSave)=="function"then queueConfigSave()end
end
local settings:any={
instantRoles=false,
gun=true,
murderClose=true,
yourRole=true,
roundEvents=true,
murderDefeated=true,
sheriffDown=true,
closeRadius=30,
closeCooldown=8,
}
local enabled:boolean=false
local phase:string=type(core.getRoundPhase)=="function"and core.getRoundPhase()or"unknown"
local elapsed:number=0
local murdererSeen:boolean=false
local sheriffSeen:boolean=false
local lastLocalRole:string?=nil
local lastMurderer:Player?=nil
local lastSheriff:Player?=nil
local defeatNotified:boolean=false
local sheriffNotified:boolean=false
local nearMurderer:boolean=false
local lastCloseAt:number=-math.huge
local gunWasDropped:boolean=false
local gunLastSeenAt:number=-math.huge
local gunDropPreviousHolder:Player?=nil
local lastGunHolder:Player?=nil
local gunHolderInitialized:boolean=false
local function resetRound():()
murdererSeen=false
sheriffSeen=false
lastLocalRole=nil
lastMurderer=nil
lastSheriff=nil
defeatNotified=false
sheriffNotified=false
nearMurderer=false
lastCloseAt=-math.huge
gunWasDropped=false
gunLastSeenAt=-math.huge
gunDropPreviousHolder=nil
lastGunHolder=nil
gunHolderInitialized=false
end
local function inRound():boolean
if type(core.isRoundLifecycleActive)=="function"
and core.isRoundLifecycleActive()==false then return false end
if phase=="lobby"then return false end
return phase=="map"or(type(core.hasActiveRoundRoles)=="function"and core.hasActiveRoundRoles())
end
local function rootOf(player:Player?):BasePart?
local character:Model?=player and player.Character
return character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")::BasePart?or nil
end
local function isAlive(player:Player?):boolean
if not player then return false end
if type(core.isPlayerAlive)=="function"then return core.isPlayerAlive(player)==true end
return rootOf(player)~=nil
end
local function showRoles(murderer:Player?,sheriff:Player?):()
if not enabled or not settings.instantRoles or not inRound()then return end
if not murdererSeen and not sheriffSeen and murderer and sheriff then
murdererSeen=true
sheriffSeen=true
notify("Roles ready - Murderer: "..murderer.Name.." | Sheriff/Hero: "..sheriff.Name)
return
end
if murderer and not murdererSeen then
murdererSeen=true
notify("Murderer: "..murderer.Name)
end
if sheriff and not sheriffSeen then
sheriffSeen=true
notify("Sheriff/Hero: "..sheriff.Name)
end
end
local function gunDistance(drop:Instance?):number?
local root:BasePart?=rootOf(LocalPlayer)
if not root or not drop then return nil end
local ok:boolean,distance:any=pcall(function():number
local position:Vector3=if drop:IsA("BasePart")
then(drop::BasePart).Position
else(drop::Model):GetPivot().Position
return(position-root.Position).Magnitude
end)
return if ok and type(distance)=="number"then distance else nil
end
local function updateGun(sheriff:Player?):()
if not settings.gun or type(core.findDroppedGun)~="function"then return end
local now:number=os.clock()
local drop:Instance?=core.findDroppedGun()
local holder:Player?=nil
if sheriff and isAlive(sheriff)and type(core.getPlayerWeapon)=="function"
and core.getPlayerWeapon(sheriff,"Gun")~=nil then holder=sheriff end
if drop then
if not gunWasDropped then
gunDropPreviousHolder=lastGunHolder or holder
local distance:number?=gunDistance(drop)
notify("Gun dropped"..(distance and(" · "..tostring(math.round(distance)).." studs away")or""),"danger")
end
gunWasDropped=true
gunLastSeenAt=now
elseif gunWasDropped and holder and holder~=gunDropPreviousHolder then
notify("Gun picked up by "..holder.Name,"good")
gunWasDropped=false
gunDropPreviousHolder=nil
elseif gunWasDropped and now-gunLastSeenAt>=2 then
gunWasDropped=false
gunDropPreviousHolder=nil
elseif holder and gunHolderInitialized and lastGunHolder and holder~=lastGunHolder then
notify("Gun is now held by "..holder.Name)
end
if holder then
lastGunHolder=holder
gunHolderInitialized=true
end
end
local function confirmedDead(player:Player?):boolean
if not player then return false end
if type(core.getRoundRole)=="function"and core.getRoundRole(player)=="Dead"then return true end
local character:Model?=player.Character
local humanoid:Humanoid?=character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")::Humanoid?
return humanoid~=nil and humanoid.Health<=0
end
local COMPASS:{string}={"N","NE","E","SE","S","SW","W","NW"}
local function compassOf(x:number?,z:number?):string
local flatX:number=tonumber(x)or 0
local flatZ:number=tonumber(z)or 0
if math.abs(flatX)+math.abs(flatZ)<1e-3 then
return"here"
end
local angle:number=math.deg(math.atan2(flatX,flatZ))
local index:number=math.floor(((angle+360+22.5)%360)/45)+1
return COMPASS[index]
end
local function updateProximity(murderer:Player?):()
if not settings.murderClose or not murderer or murderer==LocalPlayer
or not isAlive(LocalPlayer)or not isAlive(murderer)then
nearMurderer=false
return
end
local localRoot:BasePart?=rootOf(LocalPlayer)
local murderRoot:BasePart?=rootOf(murderer)
if not localRoot or not murderRoot then nearMurderer=false;return end
local delta:Vector3=murderRoot.Position-localRoot.Position
local distance:number=delta.Magnitude
local radius:number=math.clamp(tonumber(settings.closeRadius)or 30,10,120)
local cooldown:number=math.clamp(tonumber(settings.closeCooldown)or 8,2,30)
local now:number=os.clock()
if distance<=radius and now-lastCloseAt>=cooldown then
nearMurderer=true
lastCloseAt=now
notify(
"Murderer close: "..murderer.Name
.." · "..tostring(math.round(distance)).." studs"
.." "..compassOf(delta.X,delta.Z)
..(distance<=radius*0.4 and" · VERY close"or""),
"danger"
)
elseif distance>radius*1.25 then
nearMurderer=false
end
end
local function sample():()
if not enabled or not inRound()then return end
local murderer:Player?=type(core.findMurderer)=="function"and core.findMurderer()or nil
local sheriff:Player?=type(core.findSheriff)=="function"and core.findSheriff()or nil
if murderer then lastMurderer=murderer end
if sheriff then lastSheriff=sheriff end
showRoles(murderer,sheriff)
if settings.yourRole and type(core.getPlayerRole)=="function"then
local myRole:any=core.getPlayerRole(LocalPlayer)
if(myRole=="Innocent"or myRole=="Sheriff"or myRole=="Hero"or myRole=="Murderer")
and myRole~=lastLocalRole then
lastLocalRole=myRole
notify("Your role: "..myRole)
end
end
updateGun(sheriff)
updateProximity(murderer)
if settings.murderDefeated and not defeatNotified and confirmedDead(lastMurderer)then
defeatNotified=true
notify("Murderer defeated: "..(lastMurderer::Player).Name,"good")
end
if settings.sheriffDown and not sheriffNotified and lastSheriff~=nil
and lastSheriff~=LocalPlayer and confirmedDead(lastSheriff)then
sheriffNotified=true
notify("Sheriff down: "..(lastSheriff::Player).Name.." · the gun is loose","danger")
end
end
local nextWarningAt:number=-math.huge
local sampling:boolean=false
local sampleAgain:boolean=false
local function trySample():()
if sampling then sampleAgain=true;return end
sampling=true
for _pass:number=1,2 do
sampleAgain=false
local ok:boolean,err:any=pcall(sample)
if not ok and os.clock()>=nextWarningAt then
nextWarningAt=os.clock()+5
warn("MM2 Notifications: "..tostring(err))
end
if not sampleAgain or not enabled then break end
end
sampling=false
end
local unsubscribeRoles:()->()=core.onRoundRoles(function(active:boolean):()
if active and enabled then trySample()end
end)
