local unsubscribePhase:()->()=core.onRoundPhase(function(nextPhase:string,reason:string):()
local previous:string=phase
phase=nextPhase
if nextPhase=="lobby"and previous~="lobby"then
if enabled and settings.roundEvents and previous=="map"and reason~="subscribe"then
notify("Round ended")
end
resetRound()
elseif nextPhase=="map"and previous~="map"then
resetRound()
if enabled and settings.roundEvents and reason~="subscribe"then
notify("Entered the map - round starting")
end
if enabled then trySample()end
end
end)
local function toggleNotifications(value:boolean):()
enabled=value==true
disconnectFeatureConnection("MM2NotificationsTick")
elapsed=0
if not enabled then return end
trySample()
featureConnections.MM2NotificationsTick=TaskManager:Connect(function(deltaTime:number):()
elapsed+=math.max(0,deltaTime)
if elapsed<0.25 then return end
elapsed=0
trySample()
end,nil,"MM2 Notifications")
end
local feature:any=createUniversalFeature(
"Notifications","Private MM2 alerts for roles, the gun, nearby danger and rounds.",
4,toggleNotifications,
{categoryName="Utilities",parent=MM2Scroll,registry=mm2Features}
)
addToggleOption(feature,"Instant Roles",false,function(value:boolean):()
settings.instantRoles=value
core.mm2Settings.instantRoleNotify=value
if enabled and value then trySample()end
end,"Private role alerts as soon as each role is known; never sends public chat.")
addToggleOption(feature,"Gun Notification",true,function(value:boolean):()
settings.gun=value
if not value then
gunWasDropped=false
gunDropPreviousHolder=nil
lastGunHolder=nil
gunHolderInitialized=false
elseif enabled then
trySample()
end
end,"Announces a dropped gun and a confirmed new holder.")
addToggleOption(feature,"Murder Close",true,function(value:boolean):()
settings.murderClose=value
if enabled and value then trySample()end
end,"Warns when the living Murderer gets close, with the distance and the compass side. Off = no proximity alerts.")
addNumberOption(feature,"Close radius",30,10,120,function(value:number):()
settings.closeRadius=math.clamp(tonumber(value)or 30,10,120)
nearMurderer=false
lastCloseAt=-math.huge
if enabled and settings.murderClose then trySample()end
end,"Studs at which the Murder Close warning fires. The alert repeats when you get closer or after the cooldown.",10,true)
addNumberOption(feature,"Close cooldown",8,2,30,function(value:number):()
settings.closeCooldown=math.clamp(tonumber(value)or 8,2,30)
end,"Minimum seconds between two Murder Close warnings.",1,true)
addToggleOption(feature,"Sheriff Down",true,function(value:boolean):()
settings.sheriffDown=value
if enabled and value then trySample()end
end,"Announces the known Sheriff/Hero death: that is when the gun is loose.")
addToggleOption(feature,"Your Role",true,function(value:boolean):()
settings.yourRole=value
if enabled and value then trySample()end
end,"Notifies you when your role becomes known or changes.")
addToggleOption(feature,"Round Events",true,function(value:boolean):()
settings.roundEvents=value
end,"Private alerts for entering the map and the round ending.")
addToggleOption(feature,"Murderer Defeated",true,function(value:boolean):()
settings.murderDefeated=value
if enabled and value then trySample()end
end,"Warns only when the known Murderer is confirmed dead.")
addInformationOption(feature,"These alerts are private. Blurt Roles is the separate public-chat feature.")
activeCleanup=function():()
toggleNotifications(false)
pcall(unsubscribeRoles)
pcall(unsubscribePhase)
core.mm2Settings.instantRoleNotify=false
end
Module.Events=featureConnections
Module.Initialized=true
return Module
end
function Module.destroy():()
if not Module.Initialized then return end
Module.Initialized=false
activeCleanup()
activeCleanup=function():()end
Module.Events={}
Module.Runtime=nil
end
return Module
-- FIN src/games/MM2/Other/Notifications.lua

-- INICIO src/games/MM2/Prediction.lua
local Module={
Name="MM2 Prediction",
Events={}::{[string]:any},
Initialized=false,
Runtime=nil::any,
}
type Sample={
at:number,
position:Vector3,
velocity:Vector3,
state:string,
grounded:boolean,
aimOffset:Vector3,
aimPart:string,
moveDirection:Vector3
}
type Track={
character:Model,
humanoid:Humanoid,
root:BasePart,
samples:{Sample},
aimPart:BasePart?,
aimName:string?,
partsAt:number?,
spare:{Sample}?,
knifeFloorCache:{any}?,
knifeFloorNext:number?,
knifeFloorSerial:number?
}
type Fit={
velocity:Vector3,
residual:number,
count:number,
span:number,
center:number
}
type Motion={
velocity:Vector3,
horizontal:Vector3,
vertical:number,
airborne:boolean,
sampleAge:number,
samples:number,
residual:number,
source:string,
turnRate:number,
longitudinalAccel:number,
accelX:number,
accelZ:number,
brakeSeconds:number?,
fitLag:number,
verticalLead:boolean,
moveDirection:Vector3?,
reportedHorizontal:Vector3?,
hasInputHistory:boolean?
}
type AimInfo={
endpoint:Vector3,
targetPosition:Vector3,
capturedPosition:Vector3,
rootPosition:Vector3,
velocity:Vector3,
leadSeconds:number,
turnRate:number,
brakeSeconds:number?,
sampleAge:number,
samples:number,
residual:number,
motionSource:string,
model:string,
rtt:number,
rttEngine:number?,
rttData:number?,
echoDelay:number?,
echoSamples:number,
leadCorrection:number,
leadCorrectionTarget:number?,
leadSamples:number?,
aimMode:string?,
flightSeconds:number?
}
local MAX_SAMPLE_AGE:number=0.35
local FIT_WINDOW:number=0.16
local FIT_TAU:number=0.055
local HISTORY_SECONDS:number=0.6
local SAMPLE_HZ:number=60
local MAX_HORIZONTAL_SPEED:number=60
local DEFAULT_KNIFE_SPEED:number=96
local MAX_ECHOES:number=8
local MIN_ECHO_SAMPLES:number=3
local MIN_ECHO_DELAY:number=0.05
local MAX_ECHO_DELAY:number=1.6
local ECHO_MAX_AGE:number=30
local ECHO_DIVERGENCE_RATIO:number=1.5
local MAX_HORIZON:number=1.0
local MAX_AUTO_LEAD:number=0.6
local TURN_SPAN:number=0.10
local TURN_MIN_SPEED:number=2.0
local TURN_MAX_RATE:number=10.0
local TURN_MIN_RATE:number=0.4
local TURN_MAX_ANGLE:number=math.rad(45)
local TURN_RESIDUAL:number=0.20
local VERTICAL_MIN_SPEED:number=8.0
local MAX_FLOOR_LIFT_STUDS:number=6
local RTT_SAMPLES:number=8
local RTT_SAMPLE_INTERVAL:number=0.25
local BRAKE_MIN_ACCEL:number=6.0
local BRAKE_MAX_ACCEL:number=120.0
local LEAD_FEEDBACK_MIN_SAMPLES:number=3
local LEAD_FEEDBACK_MAX_SAMPLES:number=8
local LEAD_FEEDBACK_SAMPLE_LIMIT:number=0.15
local MAX_LEAD_CORRECTION:number=0.06
local LEAD_SLEW_PER_SAMPLE:number=0.02
local settings={
autoLead=true,
extraLead=0,
interpolation=1/40,
}
local activeCleanup:()->()=function():()end
local function finiteNumber(value:any):boolean
return type(value)=="number"and value==value and math.abs(value)<math.huge
end
local function finiteVector(value:any):boolean
return value~=nil
and finiteNumber(value.X)
and finiteNumber(value.Y)
and finiteNumber(value.Z)
end
local function humanoidStateName(humanoid:Humanoid):string
local state:any=humanoid:GetState()
return state.Name or tostring(state)
end
local function bodyIsAlive(humanoid:Humanoid?):boolean
if not humanoid or humanoid.Health<=0 then return false end
local currentState:Enum.HumanoidStateType=humanoid:GetState()
if currentState==Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead
or currentState==Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics then
return false
end
if humanoid:GetAttribute("Dead")==true then return false end
local parent:Instance?=humanoid.Parent
return not parent or parent:GetAttribute("Dead")~=true
end
local function targetHumanoid(target:Player):any
local ok:boolean,humanoid:any=pcall(function():any
local character=target.Character
return character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
end)
if not ok then
return nil
end
return humanoid
end
local AIM_PARTS:{string}={
"UpperTorso",
"Torso",
"HumanoidRootPart",
"Head",
}
local function findAimPart(character:Model):(BasePart?,string)
for _,partName:string in ipairs(AIM_PARTS)do
local part:Instance?=character:FindFirstChild(partName)
if part and part:IsA("BasePart")then
return part::BasePart,partName
end
end
local root:Instance?=character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if root and root:IsA("BasePart")then
return root::BasePart,"HumanoidRootPart"
end
return nil,""
end
function Module.init(runtime:any):any
if Module.Initialized then
return Module
end
local core:any=state.mm2Core
assert(type(core)=="table","MM2 Prediction requires the MM2 core module")
local predictionMath:any=core.predictionMath
assert(type(predictionMath)=="table","MM2 Prediction requires predictionMath")
Module.Runtime=runtime
local tracks:{[Player]:Track}=
setmetatable({},{__mode="k"})::any
local echoes:{number}={}
local echoTimes:{number}={}
local elapsed:number=0
local PART_REFRESH_SECONDS:number=0.5
local function refreshTrackParts(
track:Track,
character:Model,
now:number
):boolean
local refresh:boolean=now-(track.partsAt or-math.huge)>=PART_REFRESH_SECONDS
or track.root==nil
or track.humanoid==nil
if not refresh then
return true
end
local humanoid:Humanoid?=character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
::Humanoid?
local root:BasePart?=character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
::BasePart?
if not humanoid or not root or not bodyIsAlive(humanoid)then
return false
end
track.humanoid=humanoid
track.root=root
local aimPart:BasePart?,aimName:string=findAimPart(character)
track.aimPart=aimPart
track.aimName=aimName
track.partsAt=now
return true
end
local function ensureTrack(player:Player,now:number):Track?
local character:Model?=player.Character
if not character then
tracks[player]=nil
return nil
end
local track:Track?=tracks[player]
if not track or track.character~=character then
track={
character=character,
humanoid=nil,
root=nil,
samples={},
partsAt=-math.huge,
}
tracks[player]=track
end
if not refreshTrackParts(track,character,now)then
tracks[player]=nil
return nil
end
return track
end
local function samplePlayer(player:Player,now:number):()
local track:Track?=ensureTrack(player,now)
if not track then
return
end
local humanoid:Humanoid?=track.humanoid
if not bodyIsAlive(humanoid)then
tracks[player]=nil
return
end
local root:BasePart=track.root
local position:Vector3=root.Position
local velocity:Vector3=root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
if not finiteVector(velocity)then
velocity=Vector3.zero
end
local stateName:string=humanoidStateName(track.humanoid)
local grounded:boolean=stateName~="Jumping"
and stateName~="Freefall"
local move:any=track.humanoid.MoveDirection
local moveDirection:Vector3=if typeof(move)=="Vector3"
and finiteVector(move)
then move
else Vector3.zero
local aimPart:BasePart?=track.aimPart
local aimPartName:string=track.aimName or""
local aimOffset:Vector3=aimPart
and(aimPart.Position-position)
or Vector3.zero
local samples:{Sample}=track.samples
local last:Sample?=samples[#samples]
if last then
local delta:number=now-last.at
if delta>0 and delta<0.4
and(position-last.position).Magnitude>8+120*delta then
local spare:{Sample}=track.spare or{}
track.spare=spare
for _,dropped:Sample in ipairs(samples)do
if#spare<8 then
table.insert(spare,dropped)
end
end
table.clear(samples)
end
end
local spare:{Sample}=track.spare or{}
track.spare=spare
local sample:Sample=table.remove(spare)or{}::Sample
sample.at=now
sample.position=position
sample.velocity=velocity
sample.state=stateName
sample.grounded=grounded
sample.aimOffset=aimOffset
sample.aimPart=aimPartName
sample.moveDirection=moveDirection
table.insert(samples,sample)
while#samples>2 and now-samples[1].at>HISTORY_SECONDS do
local retired:Sample=table.remove(samples,1)
if#spare<8 then
table.insert(spare,retired)
end
end
end
local function fitVelocity(track:Track,reference:number):Fit?
local samples:{Sample}=track.samples
if#samples<3 then
return nil
end
local collected:{Sample}={}
for index:number=#samples,1,-1 do
local sample:Sample=samples[index]
if sample.at<=reference+1e-4 then
if reference-sample.at>FIT_WINDOW then
break
end
table.insert(collected,1,sample)
end
end
if#collected<3 then
return nil
end
local anchor:number=collected[#collected].at
local sw,st,stt:number=0,0,0
local sx,sy,sz:number=0,0,0
local stx,sty,stz:number=0,0,0
for _,sample:Sample in ipairs(collected)do
local dt:number=sample.at-anchor
local age:number=math.max(0,reference-sample.at)
local weight:number=math.exp(-age/FIT_TAU)
sw+=weight
st+=weight*dt
stt+=weight*dt*dt
sx+=weight*sample.position.X
sy+=weight*sample.position.Y
sz+=weight*sample.position.Z
stx+=weight*dt*sample.position.X
sty+=weight*dt*sample.position.Y
stz+=weight*dt*sample.position.Z
end
if sw<=0 then
return nil
end
local denominator:number=stt-st*st/sw
if denominator<=1e-8 then
return nil
end
local meanT:number=st/sw
local meanX:number=sx/sw
local meanY:number=sy/sw
local meanZ:number=sz/sw
local vx:number=(stx-st*sx/sw)/denominator
local vy:number=(sty-st*sy/sw)/denominator
local vz:number=(stz-st*sz/sw)/denominator
local squared:number=0
for _,sample:Sample in ipairs(collected)do
local dt:number=sample.at-anchor
local dx:number=sample.position.X-(meanX+vx*(dt-meanT))
local dy:number=sample.position.Y-(meanY+vy*(dt-meanT))
local dz:number=sample.position.Z-(meanZ+vz*(dt-meanT))
squared+=dx*dx+dy*dy+dz*dz
end
return{
velocity=Vector3.new(vx,vy,vz),
residual=math.sqrt(squared/#collected),
count=#collected,
span=collected[#collected].at-collected[1].at,
center=anchor+meanT,
}
end
local function measureTurn(
track:Track,
now:number,
fit:Fit
):(number,number,number?,number,number,number)
local newest:Sample?=track.samples[#track.samples]
local fitLag:number=newest
and math.max(0,newest.at-fit.center)
or 0
if fit.count<3 or fit.span<0.06 then
return 0,0,nil,fitLag,0,0
end
local previous:Fit?=fitVelocity(track,now-TURN_SPAN)
if not previous or previous.count<3 or previous.span<0.04 then
return 0,0,nil,fitLag,0,0
end
local worstResidual:number=math.max(fit.residual,previous.residual)
local confidence:number=1
if worstResidual>TURN_RESIDUAL then
if worstResidual>TURN_RESIDUAL*2.25 then
return 0,0,nil,fitLag,0,0
end
confidence=0.5
end
local v0:Vector3=Vector3.new(previous.velocity.X,0,previous.velocity.Z)
local v1:Vector3=Vector3.new(fit.velocity.X,0,fit.velocity.Z)
if v0.Magnitude<TURN_MIN_SPEED or v1.Magnitude<TURN_MIN_SPEED then
return 0,0,nil,fitLag,0,0
end
local dt:number=math.max(0.04,fit.center-previous.center)
local cross:number=v0.Z*v1.X-v0.X*v1.Z
local dot:number=v0.X*v1.X+v0.Z*v1.Z
local rate:number=math.clamp(
math.atan2(cross,dot)/dt,
-TURN_MAX_RATE,
TURN_MAX_RATE
)
if math.abs(rate)<TURN_MIN_RATE then
rate=0
end
local ax:number=(v1.X-v0.X)/dt*confidence
local az:number=(v1.Z-v0.Z)/dt*confidence
rate*=confidence
if math.abs(rate)<TURN_MIN_RATE then
rate=0
end
local speed:number=v1.Magnitude
local along:number=math.clamp(
(ax*v1.X+az*v1.Z)/speed,
-BRAKE_MAX_ACCEL,
BRAKE_MAX_ACCEL
)
local brakeSeconds:number?=nil
if along<=-BRAKE_MIN_ACCEL then
local currentSpeed:number=math.max(0,speed+along*fitLag)
brakeSeconds=currentSpeed/-along
end
local accelMagnitude:number=math.sqrt(ax*ax+az*az)
if accelMagnitude>BRAKE_MAX_ACCEL then
local scale:number=BRAKE_MAX_ACCEL/accelMagnitude
ax*=scale
az*=scale
end
return rate,along,brakeSeconds,fitLag,ax,az
end
local function estimateMotion(track:Track,now:number):Motion?
local samples:{Sample}=track.samples
local last:Sample?=samples[#samples]
if not last then
return nil
end
local age:number=math.max(0,now-last.at)
if age>MAX_SAMPLE_AGE then
return nil
end
local fit:Fit?=fitVelocity(track,now)
local reported:Vector3=last.velocity
local horizontal:Vector3
local source:string
local turnRate:number=0
local longitudinalAccel:number=0
local accelX:number=0
local accelZ:number=0
local brakeSeconds:number?=nil
local fitLag:number=0
local verticalLead:boolean=false
if fit and fit.count>=3 and fit.span>=0.04 and fit.residual<=1.5 then
horizontal=Vector3.new(fit.velocity.X,0,fit.velocity.Z)
source="history"
else
horizontal=Vector3.new(reported.X,0,reported.Z)
source="replicated"
end
if not finiteVector(horizontal)then
horizontal=Vector3.zero
elseif horizontal.Magnitude>MAX_HORIZONTAL_SPEED then
horizontal=horizontal.Unit*MAX_HORIZONTAL_SPEED
end
local airborne:boolean=last.state=="Jumping"
or last.state=="Freefall"
or math.abs(reported.Y)>=15
local vertical:number=0
if airborne then
vertical=reported.Y
if not finiteNumber(vertical)then
vertical=0
elseif math.abs(vertical)<0.5 and fit then
vertical=fit.velocity.Y
end
vertical=math.clamp(vertical,-120,120)
end
if fit then
turnRate,longitudinalAccel,brakeSeconds,fitLag,accelX,accelZ=
measureTurn(track,now,fit)
end
if airborne and math.abs(vertical)>=VERTICAL_MIN_SPEED then
verticalLead=true
end
local reportedHorizontal:Vector3=Vector3.new(reported.X,0,reported.Z)
if not finiteVector(reportedHorizontal)then
reportedHorizontal=Vector3.zero
elseif reportedHorizontal.Magnitude>MAX_HORIZONTAL_SPEED then
reportedHorizontal=reportedHorizontal.Unit*MAX_HORIZONTAL_SPEED
end
local moveDir:Vector3=last.moveDirection or Vector3.zero
local moveHorizontal:Vector3=Vector3.new(moveDir.X,0,moveDir.Z)
if not finiteVector(moveHorizontal)then
moveHorizontal=Vector3.zero
end
local hasInputHistory:boolean=moveHorizontal.Magnitude>0.08
if not hasInputHistory then
for idx:number=math.max(1,#samples-15),#samples do
local s:Sample=samples[idx]
local md:Vector3=s.moveDirection or Vector3.zero
if math.sqrt(md.X*md.X+md.Z*md.Z)>0.08 then
hasInputHistory=true
break
end
end
end
return{
velocity=Vector3.new(horizontal.X,vertical,horizontal.Z),
horizontal=horizontal,
vertical=vertical,
airborne=airborne,
sampleAge=age,
samples=#samples,
residual=fit and fit.residual or math.huge,
source=source,
turnRate=turnRate,
longitudinalAccel=longitudinalAccel,
accelX=accelX,
accelZ=accelZ,
brakeSeconds=brakeSeconds,
fitLag=fitLag,
verticalLead=verticalLead,
moveDirection=moveHorizontal,
reportedHorizontal=reportedHorizontal,
hasInputHistory=hasInputHistory,
}
end
local function leadHorizontal(motion:Motion,horizon:number):Vector3
if horizon<=0 then
return Vector3.zero
end
local moveDir:Vector3?=motion.moveDirection
local moveMag:number=moveDir and moveDir.Magnitude or 0
if motion.hasInputHistory then
local repH:Vector3=motion.reportedHorizontal or motion.horizontal
local fitH:Vector3=motion.horizontal
local v0:Vector3=if repH.Magnitude>0.05
then repH*0.65+fitH*0.35
else fitH
local v0Mag:number=v0.Magnitude
local effHorizon:number=horizon*0.88
local tau:number=motion.airborne and 0.17 or 0.11
if moveMag>0.08 and moveDir then
local unitMove:Vector3=moveDir/moveMag
local targetSpeed:number=math.clamp(math.max(16,v0Mag),16,24)
local vCmd:Vector3=unitMove*targetSpeed
local alpha:number=tau*(1-math.exp(-effHorizon/tau))
return vCmd*effHorizon+(v0-vCmd)*alpha
else
local stopTau:number=0.10
local alpha:number=stopTau*(1-math.exp(-effHorizon/stopTau))
return v0*alpha
end
end
local speed:number=motion.horizontal.Magnitude
if speed<1e-4 then
return Vector3.zero
end
local direction:Vector3=motion.horizontal.Unit
local accelMagnitude:number=math.sqrt(
motion.accelX*motion.accelX+motion.accelZ*motion.accelZ
)
if accelMagnitude>=4 then
local applied:Vector3=Vector3.new(
motion.accelX,
0,
motion.accelZ
)
if accelMagnitude>60 then
applied*=60/accelMagnitude
end
local displacement:Vector3=motion.horizontal*horizon
+applied*(motion.fitLag*horizon
+0.5*horizon*horizon)
if motion.brakeSeconds and horizon>motion.brakeSeconds then
local alongDistance:number=displacement:Dot(direction)
local along:number=math.min(
0,
motion.longitudinalAccel
)
local speedNow:number=math.max(
0,
speed+along*motion.fitLag
)
local stopDistance:number=speedNow
*motion.brakeSeconds*0.5
if alongDistance>stopDistance then
displacement-=direction
*(alongDistance-stopDistance)
end
end
return displacement
end
if math.abs(motion.turnRate)>1e-3 then
local angle:number=math.clamp(
motion.turnRate*(horizon*0.5+motion.fitLag),
-TURN_MAX_ANGLE,
TURN_MAX_ANGLE
)
local cosine:number=math.cos(angle)
local sine:number=math.sin(angle)
direction=Vector3.new(
direction.X*cosine+direction.Z*sine,
0,
-direction.X*sine+direction.Z*cosine
)
end
local along:number=math.min(0,motion.longitudinalAccel)
local speedNow:number=math.max(0,speed+along*motion.fitLag)
local length:number=speedNow*horizon
+0.5*along*horizon*horizon
length=math.max(0,length)
if motion.brakeSeconds and horizon>motion.brakeSeconds then
length=math.min(
length,
speedNow*motion.brakeSeconds*0.5
)
end
return direction*length
end
local function predictedRoot(
track:Track,
motion:Motion,
horizon:number
):Vector3
local last:Sample=track.samples[#track.samples]
local lead:Vector3=leadHorizontal(motion,horizon)
local x:number=last.position.X+lead.X
local z:number=last.position.Z+lead.Z
local y:number=last.position.Y
if motion.verticalLead then
y+=motion.vertical*horizon
-0.5*workspace.Gravity*horizon*horizon
if y<last.position.Y then
for idx:number=#track.samples,1,-1 do
local s:Sample=track.samples[idx]
if s.grounded then
if s.position.Y-y<=MAX_FLOOR_LIFT_STUDS then
y=math.max(y,s.position.Y)
end
break
end
end
end
end
return Vector3.new(x,y,z)
end
local function currentRtt():number
local latency:any=nil
if type(core.getLatencyReadings)=="function"then
local ok:boolean,engine:any,data:any=
pcall(core.getLatencyReadings)
if ok and finiteNumber(data)then
latency=data
elseif ok and finiteNumber(engine)then
latency=engine
end
end
if not finiteNumber(latency)
and type(core.getEstimatedLatency)=="function"then
local ok:boolean,value:any=pcall(core.getEstimatedLatency)
if ok then
latency=value
end
end
if not finiteNumber(latency)then
latency=0.08
end
return math.clamp(latency,0.02,0.6)
end
local rttSamples:{number}={}
local lastRttSampleAt:number=-math.huge
local function robustRtt():number
local now:number=os.clock()
if now-lastRttSampleAt>=RTT_SAMPLE_INTERVAL then
lastRttSampleAt=now
table.insert(rttSamples,currentRtt())
while#rttSamples>RTT_SAMPLES do
table.remove(rttSamples,1)
end
end
local sorted:{number}=table.clone(rttSamples)
table.insert(sorted,currentRtt())
table.sort(sorted)
return sorted[math.floor((#sorted+1)/2)]
end
local function pruneEchoes(now:number):()
if not finiteNumber(now)then
return
end
while#echoes>0 and now-echoTimes[1]>ECHO_MAX_AGE do
table.remove(echoes,1)
table.remove(echoTimes,1)
end
end
local function echoMedian():number?
pruneEchoes(os.clock())
local count:number=#echoes
if count<MIN_ECHO_SAMPLES then
return nil
end
local sorted:{number}=table.clone(echoes)
table.sort(sorted)
local middle:number=(count+1)/2
local lower:number=math.floor(middle)
local upper:number=math.ceil(middle)
return(sorted[lower]+sorted[upper])/2
end
local function noteEcho(sentAt:number,receivedAt:number):()
if not finiteNumber(sentAt)or not finiteNumber(receivedAt)then
return
end
local delay:number=receivedAt-sentAt
if delay<MIN_ECHO_DELAY or delay>MAX_ECHO_DELAY then
return
end
local plausible:number=math.clamp(
currentRtt()*2.5+0.05,0.3,MAX_ECHO_DELAY)
if delay>plausible then
return
end
pruneEchoes(receivedAt)
table.insert(echoes,delay)
table.insert(echoTimes,receivedAt)
while#echoes>MAX_ECHOES do
table.remove(echoes,1)
table.remove(echoTimes,1)
end
local median:number?=echoMedian()
if median and median>currentRtt()*ECHO_DIVERGENCE_RATIO then
table.clear(echoes)
table.clear(echoTimes)
end
end
local function manualLead():number
return settings.autoLead and 0 or settings.extraLead
end
local leadErrors:{[string]:{number}}={}
local leadApplied:{[string]:number}={}
local function modeKey(mode:any):string
local text:string=type(mode)=="string"and mode or""
local lower:string=string.lower(text)
if lower=="normal"or lower=="bullet"then
return"Bullet"
end
if lower=="silent"or lower=="cframe"or lower=="silent aim"then
return"CFrame"
end
if lower=="knife"or lower=="blade"then
return"Knife"
end
return"default"
end
local function leadBucket(mode:any):{number}
local key:string=modeKey(mode)
local bucket:{number}?=leadErrors[key]
if not bucket then
bucket={}
leadErrors[key]=bucket
end
return bucket::{number}
end
local function medianOf(values:{number}):number?
local count:number=#values
if count==0 then
return nil
end
local sorted:{number}=table.clone(values)
table.sort(sorted)
if count%2==1 then
return sorted[(count+1)//2]
end
return(sorted[count//2]+sorted[count//2+1])*0.5
end
local function noteLeadError(seconds:any,mode:any):()
if not finiteNumber(seconds)then
return
end
if math.abs(seconds)>LEAD_FEEDBACK_SAMPLE_LIMIT then
return
end
local key:string=modeKey(mode)
local bucket:{number}=leadBucket(key)
table.insert(bucket,seconds)
while#bucket>LEAD_FEEDBACK_MAX_SAMPLES do
table.remove(bucket,1)
end
if#bucket<LEAD_FEEDBACK_MIN_SAMPLES then
return
end
local median:number?=medianOf(bucket)
if not median then
return
end
local target:number=math.clamp(
median,
-MAX_LEAD_CORRECTION,
MAX_LEAD_CORRECTION
)
local current:number=leadApplied[key]or 0
local step:number=math.clamp(
target-current,
-LEAD_SLEW_PER_SAMPLE,
LEAD_SLEW_PER_SAMPLE
)
leadApplied[key]=current+step
end
local function leadCorrection(mode:any):number
local bucket:{number}?=leadErrors[modeKey(mode)]
if not bucket or#bucket<LEAD_FEEDBACK_MIN_SAMPLES then
return 0
end
return leadApplied[modeKey(mode)]or 0
end
local function leadTarget(mode:any):number
local bucket:{number}?=leadErrors[modeKey(mode)]
if not bucket or#bucket<LEAD_FEEDBACK_MIN_SAMPLES then
return 0
end
local median:number?=medianOf(bucket)
if not median then
return 0
end
return math.clamp(median,-MAX_LEAD_CORRECTION,MAX_LEAD_CORRECTION)
end
local function exportLeadState():{[string]:any}
local out:{[string]:any}={}
for key:string,bucket:{number}in pairs(leadErrors)do
out[key]={
samples=#bucket,
median=medianOf(bucket),
applied=leadApplied[key]or 0,
}
end
return out
end
local function seedLeadError(mode:any,seconds:any,samples:any):()
if not finiteNumber(seconds)then
return
end
local count:number=finiteNumber(samples)and math.floor(samples)or 0
if count<LEAD_FEEDBACK_MIN_SAMPLES then
return
end
local key:string=modeKey(mode)
local clamped:number=math.clamp(seconds,-LEAD_FEEDBACK_SAMPLE_LIMIT,LEAD_FEEDBACK_SAMPLE_LIMIT)
local bucket:{number}=leadBucket(key)
table.clear(bucket)
for _index:number=1,math.min(count,LEAD_FEEDBACK_MAX_SAMPLES)do
table.insert(bucket,clamped)
end
leadApplied[key]=math.clamp(clamped,-MAX_LEAD_CORRECTION,MAX_LEAD_CORRECTION)
end
local function leadSamples(mode:any):number
local bucket:{number}?=leadErrors[modeKey(mode)]
return bucket and#bucket or 0
end
local function leadUncertainty(mode:any):number
local bucket:{number}?=leadErrors[modeKey(mode)]
if not bucket or#bucket<LEAD_FEEDBACK_MIN_SAMPLES then
return 0
end
local absolute:{number}={}
for _,seconds:number in ipairs(bucket)do
table.insert(absolute,math.abs(seconds))
end
return medianOf(absolute)or 0
end
local function gunHorizon(mode:any):(number,number,number?)
local rtt:number=robustRtt()
local echo:number?=echoMedian()
local pipeline:number=echo
and(rtt*0.6+echo*0.4)
or rtt
local horizon:number=pipeline
+settings.interpolation
+manualLead()
+leadCorrection(mode)
if settings.autoLead then
horizon=math.min(horizon,MAX_AUTO_LEAD)
end
return math.clamp(horizon,0.005,MAX_HORIZON),rtt,echo
end
local function knifeHorizon():(number,number,number?)
local rtt:number=robustRtt()
local echo:number?=echoMedian()
local pipeline:number=echo
and(rtt*0.6+echo*0.4)
or rtt
local horizon:number=pipeline
+settings.interpolation
+manualLead()
+leadCorrection("knife")
if settings.autoLead then
horizon=math.min(horizon,MAX_AUTO_LEAD)
end
return math.clamp(horizon,0.005,MAX_HORIZON),rtt,echo
end
local function gunAim(
target:Player,
_muzzle:Vector3,
mode:any
):(Vector3?,AimInfo?,string?)
local aimMode:string=modeKey(mode)
local humanoid:any=targetHumanoid(target)
if humanoid~=nil and not bodyIsAlive(humanoid)then
tracks[target]=nil
return nil,nil,"dead target"
end
local track:Track?=tracks[target]
if not track then
samplePlayer(target,os.clock())
track=tracks[target]
end
if not track or#track.samples==0 then
return nil,nil,"no samples"
end
local now:number=os.clock()
local motion:Motion?=estimateMotion(track,now)
if not motion then
return nil,nil,"stale sample"
end
local pipeline:number,rtt:number,echo:number?=gunHorizon(aimMode)
local rttEngine:number?,rttData:number?=nil,nil
if type(core.getLatencyReadings)=="function"then
local ok:boolean,engine:any,data:any=pcall(core.getLatencyReadings)
if ok then
rttEngine=finiteNumber(engine)and engine or nil
rttData=finiteNumber(data)and data or nil
end
end
local horizon:number=math.clamp(
motion.sampleAge+pipeline,
0.005,
MAX_HORIZON
)
local rootPosition:Vector3=predictedRoot(track,motion,horizon)
local last:Sample=track.samples[#track.samples]
local aimVelocity:Vector3=motion.velocity
if motion.hasInputHistory and horizon>1e-4 then
local chord:Vector3=rootPosition-last.position
aimVelocity=Vector3.new(
chord.X/horizon,
motion.velocity.Y,
chord.Z/horizon
)
end
local aimOffset:Vector3=last.aimOffset
local freshAimPart:BasePart?=findAimPart(track.character)
if freshAimPart then
aimOffset=freshAimPart.Position-track.root.Position
end
local endpoint:Vector3=rootPosition+aimOffset
return endpoint,{
endpoint=endpoint,
targetPosition=endpoint,
capturedPosition=last.position+aimOffset,
rootPosition=rootPosition,
velocity=aimVelocity,
leadSeconds=horizon,
sampleAge=motion.sampleAge,
samples=motion.samples,
residual=motion.residual,
motionSource=motion.source,
model="kinematic-v51",
rtt=rtt,
rttEngine=rttEngine,
rttData=rttData,
echoDelay=echo,
echoSamples=#echoes,
turnRate=motion.turnRate,
brakeSeconds=motion.brakeSeconds,
leadCorrection=leadCorrection(aimMode),
leadCorrectionTarget=leadTarget(aimMode),
leadSamples=leadSamples(aimMode),
aimMode=aimMode,
},nil
end
local KNIFE_FLOOR_DEPTH:number=80
local KNIFE_FLOOR_CACHE_SECONDS:number=0.10
local KNIFE_FLOOR_XZ_TOLERANCE:number=0.35
local knifeFloorParams:RaycastParams?=nil
local knifeFloorParamsTried:boolean=false
local knifeFloorFilter:{Instance}={}
local function knifeStandingHeight(track:Track):number?
local humanoid:Humanoid=track.humanoid
local root:BasePart=track.root
if not humanoid or not root then return nil end
local hip:number=humanoid.HipHeight
local size:Vector3=root.Size
if not finiteNumber(hip)or not finiteVector(size)then return nil end
local height:number=hip+size.Y*0.5
local rig:any=humanoid.RigType
if rig==Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6 then
local leg:Instance?=track.character:FindFirstChild("Left Leg")
or track.character:FindFirstChild("Right Leg")
if not leg or not leg:IsA("BasePart")then return nil end
height+=(leg::BasePart).Size.Y
elseif rig~=Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15 then
return nil
end
if not finiteNumber(height)or height<0.5 or height>10 then return nil end
return height
end
local function makeKnifeFloorParams():RaycastParams
local params:RaycastParams=RaycastParams.new()
params.FilterType=Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
params.RespectCanCollide=true
params.IgnoreWater=true
return params
end
local function castKnifeFloor(origin:Vector3,params:RaycastParams):RaycastResult?
return workspace:Raycast(origin,Vector3.new(0,-KNIFE_FLOOR_DEPTH,0),params)
end
local function knifeFloorAt(track:Track,projected:Vector3,now:number):(boolean,number?)
local okHeight:boolean,height:any=pcall(knifeStandingHeight,track)
if not okHeight or not finiteNumber(height)then return false,nil end
local last:Sample=track.samples[#track.samples]
local topY:number=last.position.Y+height
local cache:{any}=track.knifeFloorCache or{}
track.knifeFloorCache=cache
local newest:any=nil
for _,probe:any in ipairs(cache)do
local age:number=now-probe.at
local dx:number=projected.X-probe.x
local dz:number=projected.Z-probe.z
if age>=0 and age<=KNIFE_FLOOR_CACHE_SECONDS
and dx*dx+dz*dz<=KNIFE_FLOOR_XZ_TOLERANCE^2
and math.abs(topY-probe.topY)<=0.25
and math.abs(height-probe.height)<=0.05
and(probe.part==nil or(probe.part.Parent~=nil
and(probe.partFrame==nil or(probe.part.CFrame==probe.partFrame
and probe.part.CanCollide==probe.partCanCollide))
and(probe.partSize==nil or probe.part.Size==probe.partSize)))
and(not newest or probe.serial>newest.serial)then
newest=probe
end
end
if newest then return newest.known,newest.supportY end
if not knifeFloorParamsTried then
knifeFloorParamsTried=true
local okParams:boolean,params:any=pcall(makeKnifeFloorParams)
if okParams then knifeFloorParams=params end
end
local params:RaycastParams?=knifeFloorParams
if not params then return false,nil end
table.clear(knifeFloorFilter)
if LocalPlayer.Character then table.insert(knifeFloorFilter,LocalPlayer.Character)end
table.insert(knifeFloorFilter,track.character)
params.FilterDescendantsInstances=knifeFloorFilter
local from:Vector3=Vector3.new(projected.X,topY,projected.Z)
local okRay:boolean,hit:any=pcall(castKnifeFloor,from,params)
local known:boolean=okRay
local supportY:number?=nil
local part:Instance?=nil
local partFrame:CFrame?=nil
local partSize:Vector3?=nil
local partCanCollide:boolean?=nil
if okRay and hit then
part=hit.Instance
local okHit:boolean,valid:boolean=pcall(function():boolean
if not part or not finiteVector(hit.Position)or not finiteVector(hit.Normal)
or hit.Normal.Y<0.65 then return false end
if part:IsA("BasePart")then
partFrame=(part::BasePart).CFrame
partSize=(part::BasePart).Size
partCanCollide=(part::BasePart).CanCollide
if not partCanCollide then return false end
end
local model:Model?=part:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
if model and model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")then return false end
return true
end)
if not okHit or not valid then
known=false
else
local candidate:number=hit.Position.Y+height
if candidate>last.position.Y+0.75 then
known=false
else
supportY=math.min(candidate,last.position.Y)
end
end
end
local index:number=track.knifeFloorNext or 1
local probe:any=cache[index]or{}
track.knifeFloorSerial=(track.knifeFloorSerial or 0)+1
probe.serial=track.knifeFloorSerial
probe.at,probe.x,probe.z,probe.topY=now,projected.X,projected.Z,topY
probe.height,probe.known,probe.supportY,probe.part=height,known,supportY,part
probe.partFrame,probe.partSize,probe.partCanCollide=partFrame,partSize,partCanCollide
cache[index]=probe
track.knifeFloorNext=index==1 and 2 or 1
return known,supportY
end
local function knifeRootAt(
track:Track,motion:Motion,span:number,floorKnown:boolean,floorY:number?
):Vector3
if floorKnown and motion.verticalLead then
local last:Sample=track.samples[#track.samples]
local lead:Vector3=leadHorizontal(motion,span)
local y:number=last.position.Y+motion.vertical*span
-0.5*workspace.Gravity*span*span
if floorY then y=math.max(y,floorY)end
return Vector3.new(last.position.X+lead.X,y,last.position.Z+lead.Z)
end
return predictedRoot(track,motion,span)
end
local function knifeFloorIntercept(
track:Track,motion:Motion,horizon:number,flight:number,
origin:Vector3,speed:number,floorY:number
):(Vector3,number)
local last:Sample=track.samples[#track.samples]
local root:Vector3=knifeRootAt(track,motion,horizon+flight,true,floorY)
for _=1,5 do
local revised:number=(root+last.aimOffset-origin).Magnitude/speed
local delta:number=math.abs(revised-flight)
flight=revised
root=knifeRootAt(track,motion,horizon+flight,true,floorY)
if delta<=1e-4 then break end
end
return root,(root+last.aimOffset-origin).Magnitude/speed
end
local function knifeAirborneIntercept(
track:Track,motion:Motion,horizon:number,origin:Vector3,
speed:number,floorKnown:boolean
):(Vector3?,number?)
local last:Sample=track.samples[#track.samples]
local lowTime:number=0
local highTime:number=5
if motion.verticalLead and workspace.Gravity>0 then
local gravity:number=workspace.Gravity
local allowance:number=floorKnown and 0 or MAX_FLOOR_LIFT_STUDS
local c:number=last.position.Y+last.aimOffset.Y-origin.Y
+motion.vertical*horizon-0.5*gravity*horizon*horizon+allowance
local b:number=motion.vertical-gravity*horizon+speed
local discriminant:number=b*b+2*gravity*c
if not finiteNumber(discriminant)or discriminant<0 then return nil,nil end
local root:number=math.sqrt(discriminant)
lowTime=math.max(0,(b-root)/gravity)
highTime=math.min(highTime,(b+root)/gravity)
end
if highTime<lowTime or highTime<=0 then return nil,nil end
local segments:number=math.clamp(math.ceil((highTime-lowTime)/0.025),1,64)
local previous:number=lowTime
local previousRoot:Vector3=knifeRootAt(track,motion,horizon+previous,floorKnown,nil)
local previousGap:number=(previousRoot+last.aimOffset-origin).Magnitude-speed*previous
if previousGap<=1e-6 then
local actual:number=(previousRoot+last.aimOffset-origin).Magnitude/speed
if math.abs(actual-previous)>0.002 then return nil,nil end
return previousRoot,actual
end
for index:number=1,segments do
local time:number=lowTime+(highTime-lowTime)*index/segments
local root:Vector3=knifeRootAt(track,motion,horizon+time,floorKnown,nil)
local gap:number=(root+last.aimOffset-origin).Magnitude-speed*time
if gap<=0 then
local left:number,right:number=previous,time
for _=1,12 do
local middle:number=(left+right)*0.5
local midRoot:Vector3=knifeRootAt(track,motion,horizon+middle,floorKnown,nil)
local midGap:number=(midRoot+last.aimOffset-origin).Magnitude-speed*middle
if midGap<=0 then right=middle else left=middle end
end
local solved:Vector3=knifeRootAt(track,motion,horizon+right,floorKnown,nil)
local solvedFlight:number=(solved+last.aimOffset-origin).Magnitude/speed
if math.abs(solvedFlight-right)>0.002 then return nil,nil end
return solved,solvedFlight
end
previous=time
end
return nil,nil
end
local function knifeAim(
target:Player,
origin:Vector3,
speed:number?
):(Vector3?,AimInfo?,string?)
local humanoid:any=targetHumanoid(target)
if humanoid~=nil and not bodyIsAlive(humanoid)then
tracks[target]=nil
return nil,nil,"dead target"
end
local track:Track?=tracks[target]
if track and track.character~=target.Character then
tracks[target]=nil
track=nil
end
if not track then
samplePlayer(target,os.clock())
track=tracks[target]
end
if not track or#track.samples==0 then
return nil,nil,"no samples"
end
local now:number=os.clock()
local motion:Motion?=estimateMotion(track,now)
if not motion then
return nil,nil,"stale sample"
end
local resolvedSpeed:number=tonumber(speed)or DEFAULT_KNIFE_SPEED
if resolvedSpeed<24 or resolvedSpeed>300 then
resolvedSpeed=DEFAULT_KNIFE_SPEED
end
local pipeline:number,rtt:number,echo:number?=knifeHorizon()
local rttEngine:number?,rttData:number?=nil,nil
if type(core.getLatencyReadings)=="function"then
local ok:boolean,engine:any,data:any=pcall(core.getLatencyReadings)
if ok then
rttEngine=finiteNumber(engine)and engine or nil
rttData=finiteNumber(data)and data or nil
end
end
local horizon:number=math.clamp(
motion.sampleAge+pipeline,
0.005,
MAX_HORIZON
)
local last:Sample=track.samples[#track.samples]
local aimStart:Vector3=last.position+last.aimOffset
local initial:Vector3=motion.velocity
local flight:number?=predictionMath.interceptTime(
aimStart+initial*horizon-origin,
initial,
resolvedSpeed
)
local horizontalSeed:boolean=false
if not flight and motion.airborne and motion.vertical<0 then
local lateral:Vector3=Vector3.new(initial.X,0,initial.Z)
flight=predictionMath.interceptTime(
aimStart+lateral*horizon-origin,lateral,resolvedSpeed
)
horizontalSeed=flight~=nil
end
if not flight then
return nil,nil,"unreachable"
end
local span:number=horizon+flight
local lead:Vector3=leadHorizontal(motion,span)
local vertical:number=motion.verticalLead and motion.vertical or 0
if motion.verticalLead then
vertical-=0.5*workspace.Gravity*span
end
local effective:Vector3=Vector3.new(
lead.X,
vertical*span,
lead.Z
)/span
for _iteration:number=1,4 do
local revised:number?=predictionMath.interceptTime(
aimStart+effective*horizon-origin,
effective,
resolvedSpeed
)
if not revised then
break
end
local delta:number=math.abs(revised-flight)
flight=revised
if delta<=1e-4 then
break
end
span=horizon+flight
lead=leadHorizontal(motion,span)
vertical=motion.verticalLead and motion.vertical or 0
if motion.verticalLead then
vertical-=0.5*workspace.Gravity*span
end
effective=Vector3.new(lead.X,vertical*span,lead.Z)/span
end
local endpoint:Vector3=aimStart+effective*(horizon+flight)
if motion.airborne then
local lateral:Vector3=Vector3.new(initial.X,0,initial.Z)
local lateralFlight:number?=predictionMath.interceptTime(
aimStart+lateral*horizon-origin,lateral,resolvedSpeed
)
local probeFlight:number=lateralFlight or flight
local spanAtProbe:number=horizon+probeFlight
local probePoint:Vector3=predictedRoot(track,motion,spanAtProbe)
local possibleSpan:number=horizon+math.max(probeFlight,flight)
local rawY:number=last.position.Y+motion.vertical*possibleSpan
-0.5*workspace.Gravity*possibleSpan*possibleSpan
local known:boolean,floorY:number?=false,nil
local probes:number=0
if motion.verticalLead and rawY<last.position.Y-0.25 then
known,floorY=knifeFloorAt(track,probePoint,now)
probes=1
end
local solvedRoot:Vector3?=nil
local solvedFlight:number?=nil
if known and floorY then
solvedRoot,solvedFlight=knifeFloorIntercept(
track,motion,horizon,probeFlight,origin,resolvedSpeed,floorY
)
local dx:number=(solvedRoot::Vector3).X-probePoint.X
local dz:number=(solvedRoot::Vector3).Z-probePoint.Z
if dx*dx+dz*dz>KNIFE_FLOOR_XZ_TOLERANCE^2 then
probePoint=solvedRoot::Vector3
known,floorY=knifeFloorAt(track,probePoint,now)
probes+=1
if known and floorY then
solvedRoot,solvedFlight=knifeFloorIntercept(
track,motion,horizon,solvedFlight::number,
origin,resolvedSpeed,floorY
)
dx=(solvedRoot::Vector3).X-probePoint.X
dz=(solvedRoot::Vector3).Z-probePoint.Z
if dx*dx+dz*dz>KNIFE_FLOOR_XZ_TOLERANCE^2 then
return nil,nil,"uncertain landing"
end
else
solvedRoot,solvedFlight=nil,nil
end
end
end
if not solvedRoot then
if horizontalSeed then return nil,nil,"unreachable"end
local first:Vector3=knifeRootAt(track,motion,horizon+flight,known,nil)
local actual:number=(first+last.aimOffset-origin).Magnitude/resolvedSpeed
if finiteNumber(actual)and actual<=5 and math.abs(actual-flight)<=0.002 then
solvedRoot,solvedFlight=first,actual
else
solvedRoot,solvedFlight=knifeAirborneIntercept(
track,motion,horizon,origin,resolvedSpeed,known
)
end
if not solvedRoot or not solvedFlight then return nil,nil,"unreachable"end
if probes>0 and known then
local dx:number=solvedRoot.X-probePoint.X
local dz:number=solvedRoot.Z-probePoint.Z
if dx*dx+dz*dz>KNIFE_FLOOR_XZ_TOLERANCE^2 then
if probes>=2 then return nil,nil,"uncertain landing"end
probePoint=solvedRoot
known,floorY=knifeFloorAt(track,probePoint,now)
probes+=1
if not known then return nil,nil,"uncertain landing"end
if floorY then
solvedRoot,solvedFlight=knifeFloorIntercept(
track,motion,horizon,solvedFlight,origin,resolvedSpeed,floorY
)
dx=solvedRoot.X-probePoint.X
dz=solvedRoot.Z-probePoint.Z
if dx*dx+dz*dz>KNIFE_FLOOR_XZ_TOLERANCE^2 then
return nil,nil,"uncertain landing"
end
end
end
end
end
if not solvedRoot or not solvedFlight or not finiteVector(solvedRoot)
or not finiteNumber(solvedFlight)or solvedFlight>5 then
return nil,nil,"unreachable"
end
local consistentRoot:Vector3=knifeRootAt(
track,motion,horizon+solvedFlight,known,floorY
)
if(consistentRoot-solvedRoot).Magnitude>0.25 then
return nil,nil,"uncertain intercept"
end
if horizontalSeed then
local correctedSpan:number=horizon+solvedFlight
local unboundedY:number=last.position.Y+motion.vertical*correctedSpan
-0.5*workspace.Gravity*correctedSpan*correctedSpan
if not floorY or unboundedY>floorY then return nil,nil,"unreachable"end
end
endpoint=solvedRoot+last.aimOffset
flight=(endpoint-origin).Magnitude/resolvedSpeed
end
return endpoint,{
endpoint=endpoint,
targetPosition=endpoint,
capturedPosition=aimStart,
rootPosition=endpoint-last.aimOffset,
velocity=motion.velocity,
leadSeconds=horizon,
flightSeconds=flight,
sampleAge=motion.sampleAge,
samples=motion.samples,
residual=motion.residual,
motionSource=motion.source,
model="kinematic-v164-knife",
rtt=rtt,
rttEngine=rttEngine,
rttData=rttData,
echoDelay=echo,
echoSamples=#echoes,
turnRate=motion.turnRate,
brakeSeconds=motion.brakeSeconds,
leadCorrection=0,
},nil
end
local function playerStatus(target:Player?):string
local rtt:number=robustRtt()
local echo:number?=echoMedian()
local parts:{string}={
string.format("RTT %.0f ms",rtt*1000),
}
if echo then
table.insert(
parts,
string.format("echo %.0f ms (%d)",echo*1000,#echoes)
)
end
table.insert(
parts,
string.format("interp %.0f ms",settings.interpolation*1000)
)
if settings.autoLead then
table.insert(
parts,
echo and"auto lead (echo)"or"auto lead (ping)"
)
elseif settings.extraLead~=0 then
table.insert(
parts,
string.format("manual %+.0f ms",settings.extraLead*1000)
)
end
local horizon:number=(echo or rtt)
+settings.interpolation
+manualLead()
if target then
local track:Track?=tracks[target]
local preview:Motion?=track and estimateMotion(track,os.clock())
if preview then
horizon+=preview.sampleAge
end
end
local capped:boolean=false
if settings.autoLead and horizon>MAX_AUTO_LEAD then
horizon=MAX_AUTO_LEAD
capped=true
end
if horizon>MAX_HORIZON then
horizon=MAX_HORIZON
capped=true
end
table.insert(
parts,
string.format(
"lead %.0f ms%s",
horizon*1000,
capped and" (capped)"or""
)
)
if target then
local track:Track?=tracks[target]
local motion:Motion?=track and estimateMotion(track,os.clock())
if motion then
table.insert(parts,string.format("%.1f studs/s",motion.horizontal.Magnitude))
table.insert(parts,string.format("%d samples",motion.samples))
table.insert(parts,string.format("age %.0f ms",motion.sampleAge*1000))
if motion.airborne then
table.insert(parts,"airborne")
end
if math.abs(motion.turnRate)>0.05 then
table.insert(
parts,
string.format("turn %+.0f deg/s",math.deg(motion.turnRate))
)
end
if motion.brakeSeconds then
table.insert(
parts,
string.format("brake %.2f s",motion.brakeSeconds)
)
end
else
table.insert(parts,"no fresh samples")
end
end
return table.concat(parts," · ")
end
local controller:any={
settings=settings,
noteEcho=noteEcho,
getHistory=function(
player:Player,
seconds:number?
):({any}?,number?)
local track:Track?=tracks[player]
if not track or#track.samples<2 then
return nil,0
end
local span:number=math.clamp(
seconds or HISTORY_SECONDS,
0.05,
HISTORY_SECONDS
)
local newest:Sample=track.samples[#track.samples]
local age:number=math.max(0,os.clock()-newest.at)
local points:{any}={}
for _,sample:Sample in ipairs(track.samples)do
local at:number=sample.at-newest.at
if at>=-span-1e-6 then
table.insert(points,{
at=at,
position=sample.position,
velocity=sample.velocity,
state=sample.state,
grounded=sample.grounded,
moveDirection=sample.moveDirection,
aimOffset=sample.aimOffset,
aimPart=sample.aimPart,
})
end
end
if#points<2 then
return nil,age
end
return points,age
end,
gunAim=gunAim,
knifeAim=knifeAim,
status=playerStatus,
getRtt=currentRtt,
getEchoDelay=echoMedian,
predict=function(player:Player,horizon:number):Vector3?
local track:Track?=tracks[player]
if not track or#track.samples==0 or not finiteNumber(horizon)then
return nil
end
local motion:Motion?=estimateMotion(track,os.clock())
if not motion then
return nil
end
return predictedRoot(track,motion,math.clamp(horizon,0,2))
end,
getMotion=function(player:Player):Motion?
local track:Track?=tracks[player]
if not track then
return nil
end
return estimateMotion(track,os.clock())
end,
noteLeadError=noteLeadError,
exportLeadState=exportLeadState,
seedLeadError=seedLeadError,
getLeadCorrection=leadCorrection,
getLeadCorrectionTarget=leadTarget,
getLeadUncertainty=leadUncertainty,
getLeadSamples=leadSamples,
reset=function():()
table.clear(echoes)
table.clear(echoTimes)
table.clear(tracks)
table.clear(rttSamples)
for _,bucket:{number}in pairs(leadErrors)do
table.clear(bucket)
end
table.clear(leadErrors)
table.clear(leadApplied)
lastRttSampleAt=-math.huge
end,
}
local roster:{Player}={}
local rosterAt:number=-math.huge
local function tick(deltaTime:number):()
elapsed+=math.max(0,deltaTime or 0)
local interval:number=1/SAMPLE_HZ
if elapsed+1e-9<interval then
return
end
elapsed=elapsed%interval
local now:number=os.clock()
if now-rosterAt>=0.25 or#roster==0 then
rosterAt=now
roster=Players:GetPlayers()
end
for _,player:Player in ipairs(roster)do
if player~=LocalPlayer then
samplePlayer(player,now)
end
end
end
state.mm2Prediction=controller
featureConnections.MM2PredictionSampler=
TaskManager:Connect(tick,nil,"Prediction sampler")
activeCleanup=function():()
disconnectFeatureConnection("MM2PredictionSampler")
if state.mm2Prediction==controller then
state.mm2Prediction=nil
end
table.clear(echoes)
table.clear(echoTimes)
table.clear(tracks)
end
Module.Events=featureConnections
Module.Initialized=true
return Module
end
function Module.destroy():()
if not Module.Initialized then
return
end
Module.Initialized=false
pcall(activeCleanup)
activeCleanup=function():()end
Module.Events={}
Module.Runtime=nil
end
return Module
-- FIN src/games/MM2/Prediction.lua

-- INICIO src/games/MM2/Render/CoinEsp.lua
local Module={
Name="MM2 Coin ESP",
Events={}::{[string]:any},
Initialized=false,
Runtime=nil::any,
}
local activeCleanup:()->()=function():()end
local COIN_TAG:string="CoinVisual"
local VISUAL_NAME:string="CoinVisual"
local SERVER_NAME:string="Coin_Server"
local FAMILY_COIN:string="Coin"
local FAMILY_CANDY:string="Candy"
local FAMILY_SNOW:string="SnowToken"
local FAMILY_BEACH:string="BeachBall"
local FAMILY_EGG:string="Egg"
local FAMILY_HEART:string="Heart"
local FAMILY_ALL:string="All"
local FAMILY_AUTO:string="Auto (server)"
local FAMILY_ORDER:{string}={
FAMILY_EGG,
FAMILY_BEACH,
FAMILY_SNOW,
FAMILY_CANDY,
FAMILY_HEART,
FAMILY_COIN,
}
local COUNT_ORDER:{string}={
FAMILY_COIN,
FAMILY_CANDY,
FAMILY_SNOW,
FAMILY_BEACH,
FAMILY_EGG,
FAMILY_HEART,
}
local FAMILY_NOUNS:{[string]:{string}}={
[FAMILY_COIN]={"coin","coins"},
[FAMILY_CANDY]={"candy","candies"},
[FAMILY_SNOW]={"snowtoken","snowtokens"},
[FAMILY_BEACH]={"beachball","beachballs"},
[FAMILY_EGG]={"egg","eggs"},
[FAMILY_HEART]={"heart","hearts"},
}
local FAMILY_CHOICES:{string}={
FAMILY_AUTO,
FAMILY_COIN,
FAMILY_CANDY,
FAMILY_SNOW,
FAMILY_BEACH,
FAMILY_EGG,
FAMILY_HEART,
FAMILY_ALL,
}
local BAG_TO_FAMILY:{[string]:string}={
Coin=FAMILY_COIN,
Candy=FAMILY_CANDY,
SnowToken=FAMILY_SNOW,
BeachBall=FAMILY_BEACH,
Egg=FAMILY_EGG,
}
local FAMILY_PHRASES:{[string]:{string}}={
[FAMILY_EGG]={"egg","eggs"},
[FAMILY_BEACH]={"beachball","beach ball","beachballs","beach balls"},
[FAMILY_SNOW]={"snowtoken","snow token","snowtokens","snow tokens","snowball","snow ball"},
[FAMILY_CANDY]={"candy","candies","candied","candycorn","candy corn"},
[FAMILY_HEART]={"heart","hearts"},
[FAMILY_COIN]={"maincoin","coinold","decalpart"},
}
local RESCAN_PERIOD:number=2
local function lower(value:string):string
return string.lower(value)
end
local function contains(value:string,needle:string):boolean
return string.find(value,needle,1,true)~=nil
end
function Module.init(runtime:any):any
if Module.Initialized then
return Module
end
local core:any=state.mm2Core
assert(type(core)=="table","MM2 Coin ESP requires the MM2 core module")
Module.Runtime=runtime
local CoinEffects:any=core.CoinEffects
local clearEffects:any=core.clearEffects
local mm2Settings:any=core.mm2Settings
local collectionService:any=game:GetService("CollectionService")
local settings={
family=FAMILY_AUTO,
}
local serverFamily:string?=nil
local lastRaw={tagged=0,coinId=0,named=0}
local coinTags:{[Instance]:{box:Instance}}={}
local lastFamilies:{[Instance]:string}={}
state.mm2CoinTagParts={}
local function clearTags():()
for part:Instance,entry in pairs(coinTags)do
coinTags[part]=nil
pcall(function():()
entry.box:Destroy()
end)
end
table.clear(lastFamilies)
state.mm2CoinTagParts={}
end
local function attribute(object:Instance,name:string):any
return object:GetAttribute(name)
end
local function hasTag(object:Instance):boolean
return collectionService:HasTag(object,COIN_TAG)
end
local function visualOf(part:Instance):Instance?
if part.Name==SERVER_NAME then
local child:Instance?=part:FindFirstChild(VISUAL_NAME)
if child and child:IsA("BasePart")then
return child
end
return nil
end
return part
end
local function passesFilters(part:Instance):boolean
if not part:IsA("BasePart")or not part:IsDescendantOf(workspace)then
return false
end
local current:Instance?=part.Parent
while current and current~=workspace do
if current:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")then
return false
end
current=current.Parent
end
return true
end
local function familyFromWorkspace():string?
local eventName:string=lower(tostring(attribute(workspace,"CurrentEvent")or""))
if contains(eventName,"halloween")then
return FAMILY_CANDY
elseif contains(eventName,"christmas")then
return FAMILY_SNOW
elseif contains(eventName,"summer")then
return FAMILY_BEACH
elseif contains(eventName,"easter")then
return FAMILY_EGG
elseif contains(eventName,"valentine")then
return FAMILY_HEART
end
for attrName:string,family:string in pairs({
isValentines=FAMILY_HEART,
isChristmas=FAMILY_SNOW,
isChristmasEnding=FAMILY_SNOW,
IsSummer=FAMILY_BEACH,
IsEaster=FAMILY_EGG,
})do
if attribute(workspace,attrName)==true then
return family
end
end
return nil
end
local function activeFamily():string?
return serverFamily or familyFromWorkspace()
end
local familyCache:{[Instance]:string}={}
local function familyOf(part:Instance):string
local cached:string?=familyCache[part]
if cached then
return cached
end
local names:{string}={}
for _,object:Instance in ipairs(part:GetDescendants())do
table.insert(names,object.Name)
end
local haystack:string=lower(table.concat(names," "))
local active:string?=activeFamily()
for _,family:string in ipairs(FAMILY_ORDER)do
if family~=FAMILY_COIN or active==nil or active==FAMILY_COIN then
for _,phrase:string in ipairs(FAMILY_PHRASES[family])do
if contains(haystack,phrase)then
if family~=FAMILY_COIN then
familyCache[part]=family
end
return family
end
end
end
end
return active or FAMILY_COIN
end
local filterCache:{[Instance]:boolean}={}
local function collectAll():({[Instance]:string},{[string]:number})
local found:{[Instance]:string}={}
local counts:{[string]:number}={}
lastRaw={tagged=0,coinId=0,named=0}
local function passesFiltersCached(part:Instance):boolean
if attribute(part,"Collected")==true
or attribute(part,"Delete")==true then
return false
end
local cached:boolean?=filterCache[part]
if cached~=nil then
return cached
end
local result:boolean=passesFilters(part)
filterCache[part]=result
return result
end
local function consider(part:Instance?):()
if not part then
return
end
local visual:Instance?=visualOf(part)
if not visual or found[visual]then
return
end
if not passesFiltersCached(visual)then
return
end
local family:string=familyOf(visual)
found[visual]=family
counts[family]=(counts[family]or 0)+1
end
local tagged:{Instance}=collectionService:GetTagged(COIN_TAG)
lastRaw.tagged=#tagged
for _,object:Instance in ipairs(tagged)do
if object:IsA("BasePart")then consider(object)end
end
for _,object:Instance in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants())do
if object:IsA("BasePart")then
if attribute(object,"CoinID")~=nil then
lastRaw.coinId+=1
consider(object)
elseif object.Name==VISUAL_NAME or object.Name==SERVER_NAME then
lastRaw.named+=1
consider(object)
end
end
end
return found,counts
end
local function wanted(family:string):boolean
local choice:string=settings.family
if choice==FAMILY_ALL then
return true
end
if choice==FAMILY_AUTO then
local active:string?=activeFamily()
return active==nil or family==active
end
return family==choice
end
local function describe(counts:{[string]:number}):string
local parts:{string}={}
for _,family:string in ipairs(COUNT_ORDER)do
local amount:number=counts[family]or 0
if amount>0 then
local nouns:{string}=FAMILY_NOUNS[family]
table.insert(parts,tostring(amount).." "..(amount==1 and nouns[1]or nouns[2]))
end
end
if#parts==0 then
return"no coins yet"
end
return table.concat(parts," · ")
end
local pickupCache:{at:number,list:{Instance}}={at=-math.huge,list={}}
local function refreshPickupCache(found:{[Instance]:string}):()
local list:{Instance}={}
for part:Instance,family:string in pairs(found)do
if wanted(family)then
table.insert(list,part)
end
end
pickupCache={at=os.clock(),list=list}
end
state.mm2CoinPickups=function():{Instance}
if os.clock()-pickupCache.at<0.5 then
return pickupCache.list
end
local found:{[Instance]:string}=collectAll()
refreshPickupCache(found)
return pickupCache.list
end
local function toggleCoinEsp(enabled:boolean):()
disconnectFeatureConnection("MM2CoinRefresh")
disconnectFeatureConnection("MM2CoinCollected")
disconnectFeatureConnection("MM2CoinsStarted")
disconnectFeatureConnection("MM2CoinTagAdded")
disconnectFeatureConnection("MM2CoinTagRemoved")
disconnectFeatureConnection("MM2CoinAdded")
clearEffects(CoinEffects)
clearTags()
if not enabled then
local card:any=state.mm2CoinFeature
if card then
card:SetStatus(nil)
end
return
end
local elapsed:number=0
local lastStatus:string?=nil
local dirty:boolean=true
local function setStatus(text:string):()
if text==lastStatus then
return
end
lastStatus=text
local card:any=state.mm2CoinFeature
if card then
card:SetStatus(text)
end
end
local function visualOf(part:Instance):BasePart
local main:Instance?=part:FindFirstChild("MainCoin",true)
if main and main:IsA("BasePart")then return main::BasePart end
local visual:Instance?=part:FindFirstChild("CoinVisual",true)
if visual and visual:IsA("BasePart")then return visual::BasePart end
return part::BasePart
end
local tagsDirty:boolean=true
local function markerTransparency():number
return math.clamp(mm2Settings.coinTransparency,0,0.6)
end
local function makeTag(part:Instance):any
local visual:BasePart=visualOf(part)
local box:BoxHandleAdornment=Instance.new("BoxHandleAdornment")
box.Name="WurstCoinBox"
box.AlwaysOnTop=true
box.ZIndex=5
box.Adornee=visual
box.Color3=mm2Settings.coinColor
box.Transparency=markerTransparency()
box.Size=visual.Size*1.3
box.Parent=part
return{box=box}
end
local function syncTags():()
for part:Instance in pairs(lastFamilies)do
if part.Parent and not coinTags[part]then
coinTags[part]=makeTag(part)
tagsDirty=true
end
end
for part:Instance,entry:any in pairs(coinTags)do
if not part.Parent or not lastFamilies[part]then
coinTags[part]=nil
pcall(function():()
entry.box:Destroy()
end)
tagsDirty=true
end
end
if tagsDirty then
tagsDirty=false
local published:{[Instance]:boolean}={}
for part:Instance in pairs(coinTags)do
published[part]=true
end
state.mm2CoinTagParts=published
end
end
local lastTagColourAt:number=-math.huge
local function drawOverlay():()
local now:number=os.clock()
if now-lastTagColourAt<0.5 then
return
end
lastTagColourAt=now
for part:Instance,entry:any in pairs(coinTags)do
if not part.Parent then
coinTags[part]=nil
entry.box:Destroy()
tagsDirty=true
else
pcall(function():()
entry.box.Color3=mm2Settings.coinColor
entry.box.Transparency=markerTransparency()
end)
end
end
if tagsDirty then
tagsDirty=false
local published:{[Instance]:boolean}={}
for part:Instance in pairs(coinTags)do
published[part]=true
end
state.mm2CoinTagParts=published
end
end
local function refresh():()
local found:{[Instance]:string},counts:{[string]:number}=collectAll()
local visible:{[Instance]:boolean}={}
table.clear(lastFamilies)
for object:Instance,family:string in pairs(found)do
if wanted(family)then
visible[object]=true
lastFamilies[object]=family
end
end
if settings.family==FAMILY_AUTO and activeFamily()~=nil then
local anyWanted:boolean=false
for object:Instance in pairs(visible)do
anyWanted=true
break
end
if not anyWanted then
for object:Instance,family:string in pairs(found)do
visible[object]=true
lastFamilies[object]=family
end
end
end
refreshPickupCache(found)
syncTags()
local shown:number=0
for _ in pairs(visible)do
shown+=1
end
local foundTotal:number=0
for _ in pairs(found)do
foundTotal+=1
end
if foundTotal==0 then
setStatus(string.format(
"no coins yet (tag %d · id %d · named %d)",
lastRaw.tagged,lastRaw.coinId,lastRaw.named))
else
setStatus(describe(counts).." · "..shown.." shown")
end
dirty=false
end
featureConnections.MM2CoinTagAdded=
collectionService:GetInstanceAddedSignal(COIN_TAG):Connect(function():()
dirty=true
end)
featureConnections.MM2CoinTagRemoved=
collectionService:GetInstanceRemovedSignal(COIN_TAG):Connect(function():()
dirty=true
end)
featureConnections.MM2CoinAdded=workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(object:Instance):()
if object:IsA("BasePart")
and(object.Name==VISUAL_NAME or object.Name==SERVER_NAME
or attribute(object,"CoinID")~=nil or hasTag(object))
then
dirty=true
end
end)
local started:Instance?=core.mm2GameplayRemotes
and core.mm2GameplayRemotes:FindFirstChild("CoinsStarted")
if started and started:IsA("RemoteEvent")then
featureConnections.MM2CoinsStarted=
(started::any).OnClientEvent:Connect(function(bagTable:any):()
if type(bagTable)=="table"then
for bag:any in pairs(bagTable)do
local family:string?=BAG_TO_FAMILY[tostring(bag)]
if family then
serverFamily=family
end
end
end
dirty=true
end)
end
local remotes:any=core.mm2GameplayRemotes
local collected:Instance?=remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("CoinCollected")
if collected and collected:IsA("RemoteEvent")then
featureConnections.MM2CoinCollected=
(collected::any).OnClientEvent:Connect(function():()
dirty=true
end)
end
refresh()
featureConnections.MM2CoinRefresh=TaskManager:Connect(
function(deltaTime:number):()
drawOverlay()
elapsed+=deltaTime
if not dirty and elapsed<RESCAN_PERIOD then
return
end
elapsed=0
refresh()
end
,nil,"Coin ESP")
end
local CoinFeature:any=createUniversalFeature(
"Coin ESP",
"Draws one small box, exactly the size of the coin, candy, snow token, beach ball or egg mesh, on every real pickup and reports how many of each family exist.",
3,
toggleCoinEsp,
{
categoryName="Render",
parent=MM2Scroll,
registry=mm2Features,
}
)
state.mm2CoinFeature=CoinFeature
addCycleOption(
CoinFeature,
"Coin in use",
FAMILY_CHOICES,
1,
function(value:string):()
settings.family=value
if CoinFeature.enabled then
toggleCoinEsp(true)
end
end,
"Auto follows the coin family the server announces through CoinsStarted."
)
addColorOption(
CoinFeature,
"Coin colour",
mm2Settings.coinColor,
function(value:Color3):()
mm2Settings.coinColor=value
if CoinFeature.enabled then
toggleCoinEsp(true)
end
end
)
addNumberOption(
CoinFeature,
"Coin transparency",
mm2Settings.coinTransparency,
0,
1,
function(value:number):()
mm2Settings.coinTransparency=value
if CoinFeature.enabled then
toggleCoinEsp(true)
end
end
)
addInformationOption(
CoinFeature,
"Pickups are recognised by the game's own signatures only: the CoinVisual tag, the CoinID attribute and the exact server/visual part pair. Each pickup gets a single BoxHandleAdornment sized exactly to its visible mesh (no labels), so a hundred coins cost no per-frame Lua. Auto follows the family the server announces on CoinsStarted."
)
activeCleanup=function():()
state.mm2CoinFeature=nil
toggleCoinEsp(false)
end
Module.Events=featureConnections
Module.Initialized=true
return Module
end
function Module.destroy():()
if not Module.Initialized then
return
end
Module.Initialized=false
pcall(activeCleanup)
activeCleanup=function():()
end
Module.Events={}
Module.Runtime=nil
end
return Module

local Module={
Name="MM2",
Events={}::{[string]:any},

Runtime=nil::any,
}
local moduleCleanup:()->()=function():()end
local function buildMM2Features()
local host:any=getfenv()
local trajectoryLogPrefix:string="["..tostring(host.PRODUCT.logPrefix)
..":MM2:Trajectory]"
local MM2Effects=create("Folder",{
Parent=ScreenGui,
Name="MM2Effects",
})
local GunEffects=create("Folder",{
Parent=MM2Effects,
Name="GunESP",
})
local TrapEffects=create("Folder",{
Parent=MM2Effects,
Name="TrapESP",
})
local CoinEffects=create("Folder",{
Parent=MM2Effects,
Name="CoinChams",
})
local mm2RoundData:{[any]:any}={}
local mm2GameplayRemotes:Instance?=nil
local mm2RoleRemote:RemoteFunction?=nil
local roleRefreshRunning:boolean=false
local lastRoleRefreshAt:number=-math.huge
local roundLifecycleActive:boolean?=nil
local roleRefreshGeneration:number=0
pcall(function()
local replicatedStorage:ReplicatedStorage=
game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local remotes:Instance?=replicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes",5)
local gameplay:Instance?=remotes and remotes:WaitForChild("Gameplay",5)
if not gameplay then
return
end
mm2GameplayRemotes=gameplay
local getCurrentPlayerData:Instance?=gameplay:FindFirstChild(
"GetCurrentPlayerData"
)
if getCurrentPlayerData and getCurrentPlayerData:IsA("RemoteFunction")then
mm2RoleRemote=getCurrentPlayerData
end
end)
