local beforeAt:number=pending.activatedAt+(newest.dt or 0)
local afterAt:number=pending.confirmedAt
and pending.confirmedAt
or(pending.activatedAt+latencyMs/1000)
if not predictionMath.finite(afterAt)or afterAt<=beforeAt then
return nil
end
local scoringAt:number=pending.activatedAt+latencyMs/1000
local alpha:number=math.clamp(
(scoringAt-beforeAt)/(afterAt-beforeAt),
0,
1
)
local p0:{number}?=newest.position
local p1:{number}?=confirmation.position
if type(p0)~="table"or type(p1)~="table"
or#p0<3 or#p1<3 then
return nil
end
local rootX:number=p0[1]+(p1[1]-p0[1])*alpha
local rootY:number=p0[2]+(p1[2]-p0[2])*alpha
local rootZ:number=p0[3]+(p1[3]-p0[3])*alpha
local offset:{number}?=newest.aimOffset
if type(offset)=="table"and#offset>=3 then
rootX+=offset[1]
rootY+=offset[2]
rootZ+=offset[3]
end
local velocity:{number}?=newest.velocity
if type(velocity)~="table"or#velocity<3 then
return nil
end
local vx:number=velocity[1]
local vz:number=velocity[3]
local speed:number=math.sqrt(vx*vx+vz*vz)
if speed<3 then
return nil
end
local along:number=((rootX-aim.X)*vx+(rootZ-aim.Z)*vz)/speed
if math.abs(along)>6 then
return nil
end
return along/speed
end
local function measureEndpointErrorStuds(pending:PendingGun):number?
local history:{HistoryPoint}=pending.targetHistory or{}
local newest:HistoryPoint?=history[#history]
local aim:Vector3?=pending.requestedAim
local latencyMs:any=pending.latencyMs
local confirmation:any=pending.rootAtConfirmation
if not newest or not aim or not confirmation then
return nil
end
if not predictionMath.finite(latencyMs)or latencyMs<=0
or latencyMs>1200 then
return nil
end
if not predictionMath.finite(pending.activatedAt)then
return nil
end
local beforeAt:number=pending.activatedAt+(newest.dt or 0)
local afterAt:number=pending.confirmedAt
and pending.confirmedAt
or(pending.activatedAt+latencyMs/1000)
if not predictionMath.finite(afterAt)or afterAt<=beforeAt then
return nil
end
local scoringAt:number=pending.activatedAt+latencyMs/1000
local alpha:number=math.clamp(
(scoringAt-beforeAt)/(afterAt-beforeAt),
0,
1
)
local p0:{number}?=newest.position
local p1:{number}?=confirmation.position
if type(p0)~="table"or type(p1)~="table"
or#p0<3 or#p1<3 then
return nil
end
local rootX:number=p0[1]+(p1[1]-p0[1])*alpha
local rootY:number=p0[2]+(p1[2]-p0[2])*alpha
local rootZ:number=p0[3]+(p1[3]-p0[3])*alpha
local offset:{number}?=newest.aimOffset
if type(offset)=="table"and#offset>=3 then
rootX+=offset[1]
rootY+=offset[2]
rootZ+=offset[3]
end
local dx:number=rootX-aim.X
local dy:number=rootY-aim.Y
local dz:number=rootZ-aim.Z
local distance:number=math.sqrt(dx*dx+dy*dy+dz*dz)
if not predictionMath.finite(distance)or distance>30 then
return nil
end
return distance
end
finalizeGunCapture=function(pending:PendingGun):()
if pending.targetDiedAt then
runtime.sessionGunKills+=1
end
if pending.healthConnection then
pcall(function():()
(pending.healthConnection::RBXScriptConnection):Disconnect()
end)
pending.healthConnection=nil
end
if state.mm2Prediction and type(state.mm2Prediction.noteEcho)=="function"
and predictionMath.finite(pending.latencyMs)
and predictionMath.finite(pending.activatedAt)then
state.mm2Prediction.noteEcho(
pending.activatedAt,
pending.activatedAt+pending.latencyMs/1000
)
end
local leadError:number?=measureLeadErrorSeconds(pending)
if leadError and state.mm2Prediction
and type(state.mm2Prediction.noteLeadError)=="function"then
pcall(state.mm2Prediction.noteLeadError,leadError,pending.shotMode)
end
local leadCorrection:number?=nil
local leadCorrectionTarget:number?=nil
local leadFeedbackSamples:number?=nil
if state.mm2Prediction then
if type(state.mm2Prediction.getLeadCorrection)=="function"then
local ok:boolean,value:any=pcall(
state.mm2Prediction.getLeadCorrection,
pending.shotMode
)
if ok and predictionMath.finite(value)then
leadCorrection=value
end
end
if type(state.mm2Prediction.getLeadCorrectionTarget)=="function"then
local ok:boolean,value:any=pcall(
state.mm2Prediction.getLeadCorrectionTarget,
pending.shotMode
)
if ok and predictionMath.finite(value)then
leadCorrectionTarget=value
end
end
if type(state.mm2Prediction.getLeadSamples)=="function"then
local ok:boolean,value:any=pcall(
state.mm2Prediction.getLeadSamples,
pending.shotMode
)
if ok and type(value)=="number"then
leadFeedbackSamples=value
end
end
end
local endpointError:number?=measureEndpointErrorStuds(pending)
local analysis:{[string]:any}=analyzeTargetTrajectory(
pending.targetTrajectory,
pending.pingMs,
"gun",
(pending.latencyMs or 0)/1000
)
appendEvent({
shotId=pending.shotId,
phase="confirmation",
kind="gun",
serverTime=workspace:GetServerTimeNow(),
clock=os.clock(),
pingMs=pending.pingMs,
latencyMs=pending.latencyMs,
targetUserId=pending.targetUserId,
targetName=pending.targetName,
targetState=analysis.initialState,
targetJumped=analysis.jumped,
targetTrajectory=pending.targetTrajectory,
targetHistory=#(pending.targetHistory or{})>0
and pending.targetHistory
or nil,
observedDisplacement=analysis.displacement,
constantVelocityResidual=analysis.constantVelocityResidual,
horizontalLeadSeconds=analysis.horizontalLeadSeconds,
verticalLeadSeconds=analysis.verticalLeadSeconds,
endpoint=pending.confirmedEndpoint
and vectorArray(pending.confirmedEndpoint)
or nil,
hitTarget=pending.confirmedHitTarget,
shotMode=pending.shotMode,
silentSpawn=pending.silentSpawn,
serverEchoMs=(pending.serverConfirmedAt
and pending.serverActivatedAt)
and(pending.serverConfirmedAt-pending.serverActivatedAt)
*1000
or nil,
serverActivatedAt=pending.serverActivatedAt,
serverConfirmedAt=pending.serverConfirmedAt,
serverOrigin=pending.confirmedServerOrigin
and vectorArray(pending.confirmedServerOrigin)
or nil,
aimOffsetAtFire=(function():{number}?
local history:{HistoryPoint}=pending.targetHistory or{}
local newest:HistoryPoint?=history[#history]
return newest and newest.aimOffset or nil
end)(),
leadErrorSeconds=leadError,
leadCorrectionSeconds=leadCorrection,
leadCorrectionTargetSeconds=leadCorrectionTarget,
leadFeedbackSamples=leadFeedbackSamples,
aimMode=pending.shotMode,
endpointErrorStuds=endpointError,
trajectoryPoints=#pending.targetTrajectory,
trajectorySpanSeconds=(
#pending.targetTrajectory>1
and(pending.targetTrajectory[#pending.targetTrajectory].dt
-pending.targetTrajectory[1].dt)
)
or 0,
trajectoryCapped=pending.trajectoryCapped==true,
samplingStoppedReason=pending.samplingStoppedReason,
samplingStoppedAt=pending.samplingStoppedAt,
rttEngineAtConfirmation=pending.rttEngineAtConfirmation,
rttDataAtConfirmation=pending.rttDataAtConfirmation,
aim=pending.requestedAim and vectorArray(pending.requestedAim)or nil,
origin=pending.origin and vectorArray(pending.origin)or nil,
hitPart=pending.confirmedHitPart,
hitPartPosition=pending.confirmedHitPartPosition
and vectorArray(pending.confirmedHitPartPosition)
or nil,
hitPartSize=pending.confirmedHitPartSize
and vectorArray(pending.confirmedHitPartSize)
or nil,
rootAtConfirmation=pending.rootAtConfirmation,
lastSeenPosition=pending.lastSeenPosition
and vectorArray(pending.lastSeenPosition)
or nil,
healthAtAim=pending.targetHealthAtAim,
healthAtConfirmation=pending.targetHealthAtConfirmation,
healthAfter=pending.targetHealthAfter,
healthLatest=pending.targetHealthLatest,
healthEvidenceScope="client-observed health; not proof this shot caused a change",
healthDropAt=pending.targetHealthDropAt,
healthDropServerTime=pending.targetHealthDropServerTime,
diedAt=pending.targetDiedAt,
diedServerTime=pending.targetDiedServerTime,
died=pending.targetDiedAt~=nil,
pingBucket=analysis.pingBucket,
bestMotionModel=analysis.bestMotionModel,
motionModelErrors=analysis.motionModelErrors,
evaluationSeconds=analysis.evaluationSeconds,
missReason=missReason,
})
end
local function handleGunFired(
eventGun:any,
eventOriginValue:any,
endpointValue:any,
hitPartValue:any
):()
if not runtime.active then
return
end
local now:number=os.clock()
compactPending(runtime.pendingGuns,now)
local eventTool:Tool?=normalizeGun(eventGun)
local eventOrigin:Vector3?=worldPosition(eventOriginValue)
local selectedIndex:number?=nil
local fallbackIndex:number?=nil
for index:number=1,#runtime.pendingGuns do
local pending:PendingGun=runtime.pendingGuns[index]
if not pending.confirmedAt then
local toolMatches:boolean=eventTool~=nil
and eventTool==pending.tool
local originMatches:boolean=eventOrigin~=nil
and pending.origin~=nil
and(eventOrigin-pending.origin).Magnitude<=8
if toolMatches and originMatches then
selectedIndex=index
break
end
if fallbackIndex==nil then
if toolMatches
and(eventOrigin==nil or pending.origin==nil)then
fallbackIndex=index
elseif eventTool==nil and originMatches then
fallbackIndex=index
end
end
end
end
if not selectedIndex then
selectedIndex=fallbackIndex
end
if not selectedIndex then
local character:Model?=LocalPlayer.Character
local root:BasePart?=character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")::BasePart?
local isLocalShooter:boolean=false
if eventTool and eventTool.Parent==character then
isLocalShooter=true
elseif root and eventOrigin and(eventOrigin-root.Position).Magnitude<=20 then
isLocalShooter=true
end
if isLocalShooter then
runtime.unmatchedGunSignals+=1
appendEvent({kind="gun",phase="unmatched-signal",
serverTime=workspace:GetServerTimeNow(),pingMs=getPingMilliseconds()})
end
return
end
local pending:PendingGun=runtime.pendingGuns[selectedIndex]
local latencyMs:number=math.max(0,(now-pending.activatedAt)*1000)
local target:Player?=pending.targetUserId
and Players:GetPlayerByUserId(pending.targetUserId)
or nil
if target then
local finalPoint:TrajectoryPoint?=captureTrajectoryPoint(
target,
pending.activatedAt,
pending.pingMs
)
if finalPoint then
table.insert(pending.targetTrajectory,finalPoint)
end
end
local endpoint:Vector3?=worldPosition(endpointValue)
local targetCharacter:Model?=target and target.Character
local hitTarget:boolean=typeof(hitPartValue)=="Instance"
and targetCharacter~=nil
and hitPartValue:IsDescendantOf(targetCharacter)
pending.serverConfirmedAt=workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
pending.confirmedServerOrigin=eventOrigin
local engineNow:number?,dataNow:number?=readLatencyReadings()
pending.rttEngineAtConfirmation=engineNow
pending.rttDataAtConfirmation=dataNow
if typeof(hitPartValue)=="Instance"then
pcall(function():()
pending.confirmedHitPart=string.sub(hitPartValue.Name,1,80)
if hitPartValue:IsA("BasePart")then
pending.confirmedHitPartPosition=hitPartValue.Position
pending.confirmedHitPartSize=hitPartValue.Size
end
end)
end
if target then
local root:BasePart?=getTargetRoot(target)
local humanoid:Humanoid?=getTargetHumanoid(target)
if root then
local stateName:string?=nil
if humanoid then
pcall(function():()
stateName=humanoid:GetState().Name
end)
end
pending.rootAtConfirmation={
position=vectorArray(root.Position),
velocity=vectorArray(root.AssemblyLinearVelocity),
state=stateName,
}
end
if humanoid then
pending.targetHealthAtConfirmation=humanoid.Health
end
end
if latencyMs>=30 and latencyMs<=450 then
table.insert(runtime.freshGunLatency,{at=now,ms=latencyMs})
if#runtime.freshGunLatency>24 then table.remove(runtime.freshGunLatency,1)end
estimatesAt=-math.huge
end
addSample(runtime.gunAcceptance,latencyMs)
runtime.sessionGunConfirmed+=1
if hitTarget then
runtime.sessionGunHitTarget+=1
end
local missReason:string?=nil
if hitTarget then
missReason="hit"
elseif typeof(hitPartValue)=="Instance"then
local otherOwner:any=nil
pcall(function():()
otherOwner=Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(
hitPartValue:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
)
end)
if otherOwner and otherOwner~=target then
missReason="other-player"
else
missReason="geometry"
end
else
missReason="no-hit-part"
end
pending.missReason=missReason
if missReason~="hit"then
runtime.sessionGunMisses=(runtime.sessionGunMisses or 0)+1
runtime.missReasons=runtime.missReasons or{}
runtime.missReasons[missReason]=
(runtime.missReasons[missReason]or 0)+1
end
pending.confirmedAt=now
pending.latencyMs=latencyMs
pending.confirmedEndpoint=endpoint
pending.confirmedHitTarget=hitTarget
emitShot("shotConfirmation",{
shotId=pending.shotId,kind="gun",hitTarget=hitTarget,
targetName=pending.targetName,latencyMs=latencyMs,
endpoint=endpoint,evidence="GunFired.hitPart",
})
ensureTrajectorySampler()
end
local KNIFE_FEEDBACK_MIN_SPEED:number=2.0
local KNIFE_FEEDBACK_MAX_MISS_STUDS:number=6.0
local KNIFE_FEEDBACK_SAMPLE_LIMIT:number=0.15
local function knifeLeadErrorSeconds(track:KnifeTrack):number?
local aimRoot:Vector3?=track.requestedRoot
local approach:Vector3?=track.approachRoot
if not aimRoot or not approach then
return nil
end
local reference:Vector3?=track.aimVelocity
if not reference then
reference=track.approachVelocity
end
if not reference then
return nil
end
local horizontal:Vector3=Vector3.new(reference.X,0,reference.Z)
local speed:number=horizontal.Magnitude
if speed<KNIFE_FEEDBACK_MIN_SPEED then
return nil
end
local offset:Vector3=approach-aimRoot
local flat:Vector3=Vector3.new(offset.X,0,offset.Z)
if flat.Magnitude>KNIFE_FEEDBACK_MAX_MISS_STUDS then
return nil
end
local seconds:number=flat:Dot(horizontal)/(speed*speed)
if not predictionMath.finite(seconds)
or math.abs(seconds)>KNIFE_FEEDBACK_SAMPLE_LIMIT then
return nil
end
return seconds
end
local finishKnifeTrack:(projectile:Instance,reason:string)->()
local function stopKnifeSamplerIfIdle():()
if next(runtime.tracks)==nil then
disconnectFeatureConnection("MM2KnifeSampler")
end
end
finishKnifeTrack=function(projectile:Instance,reason:string):()
local track:any=runtime.tracks[projectile]
if not track then return end
runtime.tracks[projectile]=nil
if track.destroyConnection then track.destroyConnection:Disconnect()end
local pending:any=track.pending
if pending and pending.healthConnection then
pending.healthConnection:Disconnect()
pending.healthConnection=nil
end
local measuredSpeed:number?=median(track.speeds)
local resolvedSpeed:number?=track.rawSpeed
if not resolvedSpeed or resolvedSpeed<24 or resolvedSpeed>300 then
resolvedSpeed=measuredSpeed
end
local validSpeed:boolean=resolvedSpeed~=nil
and resolvedSpeed>=24 and resolvedSpeed<=300
local leadErrorSeconds:number?=track.localOwner and knifeLeadErrorSeconds(track)or nil
if leadErrorSeconds and type(state.mm2Prediction)=="table"
and type(state.mm2Prediction.noteLeadError)=="function"then
pcall(state.mm2Prediction.noteLeadError,leadErrorSeconds,"knife")
end
if track.localOwner and validSpeed then
addSample(runtime.knifeSpeed,resolvedSpeed::number)
runtime.sessionKnifeConfirmed+=1
end
local analysis:{[string]:any}=analyzeTargetTrajectory(
track.targetTrajectory,track.pingMs,"knife",nil
)
appendEvent({
shotId=track.shotId,
projectileId=track.projectileId,
phase="projectile-observed",
kind="knife",
serverTime=workspace:GetServerTimeNow(),
pingMs=track.pingMs,
latencyMs=track.spawnDelayMs,
speed=validSpeed and resolvedSpeed or nil,
rawSpeed=track.rawSpeed,
samples=#track.speeds,
reason=reason,
ownerName=track.ownerName,
ownerUserId=track.ownerUserId,
localOwner=track.localOwner,
source=track.source,
direction=track.direction,
projectileTrajectory=track.projectileTrajectory,
projectileSamplingSeconds=os.clock()-track.startedAt,
targetUserId=track.targetUserId,
targetName=track.targetName,
targetState=analysis.initialState,
targetJumped=analysis.jumped,
targetTrajectory=track.targetTrajectory,
targetHistory=#track.targetHistory>0 and track.targetHistory or nil,
observedDisplacement=analysis.displacement,
constantVelocityResidual=analysis.constantVelocityResidual,
horizontalLeadSeconds=analysis.horizontalLeadSeconds,
verticalLeadSeconds=analysis.verticalLeadSeconds,
pingBucket=analysis.pingBucket,
bestMotionModel=analysis.bestMotionModel,
motionModelErrors=analysis.motionModelErrors,
evaluationSeconds=analysis.evaluationSeconds,
closestApproachStuds=track.closestApproach,
closestApproachAtMs=track.closestApproachAt,
bodyRadiusStuds=track.bodyRadius,
leadErrorSeconds=leadErrorSeconds,
predictedRoot=track.requestedRoot and vectorArray(track.requestedRoot)or nil,
approachRoot=track.approachRoot and vectorArray(track.approachRoot)or nil,
approachAtMs=track.approachAt,
aimLeadSeconds=track.aimLeadSeconds,
targetHealthAtAim=pending and pending.targetHealthAtAim or nil,
targetHealthAfter=pending and pending.targetHealthAfter or nil,
targetHealthLatest=pending and pending.targetHealthLatest or nil,
healthEvidenceScope="client-observed health; not proof this projectile caused a change",
passedThroughBody=track.closestApproach~=nil and track.bodyRadius~=nil
and track.closestApproach<=track.bodyRadius or nil,
geometryScope="client-estimated closest approach, not server-confirmed impact",
})
stopKnifeSamplerIfIdle()
end
local function ensureKnifeSampler():()
if featureConnections.MM2KnifeSampler then
return
end
featureConnections.MM2KnifeSampler=TaskManager:Connect(function(
_deltaTime:number
):()
local now:number=os.clock()
local completed:{Instance}={}
for projectile:Instance,track:any in pairs(runtime.tracks)do
if not projectile.Parent or now-track.startedAt>=5 then
table.insert(completed,projectile)
continue
end
local position:Vector3?=projectilePosition(projectile)
if position and track.previousPosition and track.previousAt then
local sampleDelta:number=now-track.previousAt
local displacement:number=(position-track.previousPosition).Magnitude
if sampleDelta>=1/240 and displacement>=0.01 then
local speed:number=displacement/sampleDelta
if speed>=24 and speed<=300 then
table.insert(track.speeds,speed)
end
end
end
if position then
track.previousPosition=position
track.previousAt=now
if now-track.lastProjectileSampleAt>=1/30
and#track.projectileTrajectory<160 then
table.insert(track.projectileTrajectory,{
dt=now-track.startedAt,position=vectorArray(position),
})
track.lastProjectileSampleAt=now
end
if track.targetUserId then
local target:Player?=Players:GetPlayerByUserId(
track.targetUserId
)
local body:BasePart?=target
and getTargetRoot(target)
or nil
if body then
local distance:number=
(position-body.Position).Magnitude
if not track.closestApproach
or distance<track.closestApproach then
track.closestApproach=distance
track.closestApproachAt=
(now-track.startedAt)*1000
track.approachRoot=body.Position
track.approachVelocity=
body.AssemblyLinearVelocity
track.approachAt=
(now-track.startedAt)*1000
local size:Vector3=body.Size
track.bodyRadius=0.5*math.max(
size.X,
math.max(size.Y,size.Z)
)
end
end
end
end
if track.targetUserId
and now-track.lastTrajectorySampleAt>=1/30 then
local target:Player?=Players:GetPlayerByUserId(
track.targetUserId
)
local point:TrajectoryPoint?=target
and captureTrajectoryPoint(
target,
track.startedAt-track.spawnDelayMs/1000,
track.pingMs
)
or nil
if point and#track.targetTrajectory<120 then
table.insert(track.targetTrajectory,point)
end
track.lastTrajectorySampleAt=now
end
end
for _,projectile:Instance in ipairs(completed)do
finishKnifeTrack(projectile,"sample-window-complete")
end
if next(runtime.tracks)==nil then
disconnectFeatureConnection("MM2KnifeSampler")
end
end,nil,"MM2 knife sampler")
end
local function resolveKnifeProjectile(projectile:Instance):()
if not runtime.active or runtime.tracks[projectile]then return end
local sessionId:string=runtime.sessionId
local now:number=os.clock()
local projectileId:string=nextShotId()
local position:Vector3?=projectilePosition(projectile)
local rawSpeed:number?=nil
local direction:any=nil
pcall(function():()
rawSpeed=tonumber(projectile:GetAttribute("ThrowSpeed"))
direction=projectile:GetAttribute("Direction")
end)
if rawSpeed and not finite(rawSpeed)then rawSpeed=nil end
local directionArray:any=nil
if typeof(direction)=="Vector3"and finite(direction.X)
and finite(direction.Y)and finite(direction.Z)then
directionArray=vectorArray(direction)
end
local track:any={
shotId=projectileId,
projectileId=projectileId,
startedAt=now,
previousAt=now,
previousPosition=position,
lastProjectileSampleAt=now,
projectileTrajectory=position and{{dt=0,position=vectorArray(position)}}or{},
speeds={},
rawSpeed=rawSpeed,
direction=directionArray,
spawnDelayMs=0,
pingMs=getPingMilliseconds(),
targetTrajectory={},
targetHistory={},
lastTrajectorySampleAt=now,
}
runtime.sessionKnifeObserved+=1
runtime.tracks[projectile]=track
appendEvent({kind="knife",phase="projectile-spawn",shotId=projectileId,
projectileId=projectileId,serverTime=workspace:GetServerTimeNow(),
pingMs=track.pingMs,origin=position and vectorArray(position)or nil,
direction=directionArray,rawSpeed=rawSpeed,
evidence="client-observed ThrowingKnife tag"})
track.destroyConnection=projectile.Destroying:Connect(function():()
finishKnifeTrack(projectile,"destroyed")
end)
ensureKnifeSampler()
task.spawn(function():()
local linkedHandle:Instance?=nil
for _attempt:number=1,20 do
if not runtime.active or runtime.sessionId~=sessionId
or runtime.tracks[projectile]~=track or not projectile.Parent then return end
local link:Instance?=projectile:FindFirstChild("HandleLink",true)
if link and link:IsA("ObjectValue")and link.Value then
linkedHandle=link.Value
break
end
task.wait(0.05)
end
if not linkedHandle or runtime.tracks[projectile]~=track then return end
local owner:Player?=nil
local linkedTool:Tool?=nil
pcall(function():()
linkedTool=linkedHandle:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Tool")::Tool?
local character:Model?=linkedHandle:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")::Model?
if character then owner=Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(character)end
end)
track.ownerName=owner and owner.Name or nil
track.ownerUserId=owner and owner.UserId or nil
track.localOwner=owner==LocalPlayer
or(linkedTool~=nil and runtime.observedTools[linkedTool]==true)
compactPending(runtime.pendingKnives,os.clock())
local selectedIndex:number?=nil
local fallbackIndex:number?=nil
for index:number=1,#runtime.pendingKnives do
local pending:PendingKnife=runtime.pendingKnives[index]
local tool:Tool?=pending.tool
if tool then
local handle:Instance?=tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
if linkedHandle==handle or linkedHandle:IsDescendantOf(tool)then
if pending.attemptCounted then selectedIndex=index;break end
fallbackIndex=fallbackIndex or index
end
end
end
selectedIndex=selectedIndex or fallbackIndex
if selectedIndex then
local pending:any=table.remove(runtime.pendingKnives,selectedIndex)
track.pending=pending
track.shotId=pending.shotId
track.localOwner=true
track.ownerName=LocalPlayer.Name
track.ownerUserId=LocalPlayer.UserId
track.source=pending.source
track.spawnDelayMs=math.max(0,(now-pending.activatedAt)*1000)
track.pingMs=pending.pingMs
track.targetUserId=pending.targetUserId
track.targetName=pending.targetName
track.requestedAim=pending.requestedAim
track.requestedRoot=pending.requestedRoot
track.aimVelocity=pending.aimVelocity
track.aimLeadSeconds=pending.aimLeadSeconds
track.activatedAt=pending.activatedAt
track.targetTrajectory=pending.targetTrajectory
track.targetHistory=pending.targetHistory or{}
addSample(runtime.knifeSpawnDelay,track.spawnDelayMs)
if not pending.attemptCounted then
runtime.sessionKnifeAttempts+=1
emitShot("authoringShot",{shotId=pending.shotId,kind="knife",
toolName=pending.tool.Name,originPos=pending.origin,
aimPos=pending.requestedAim,targetName=pending.targetName,
eventSource="observed-projectile"})
end
emitShot("shotConfirmation",{shotId=pending.shotId,kind="knife",
evidence="projectile-observed",targetName=pending.targetName})
end
appendEvent({kind="knife",phase="projectile-linked",shotId=track.shotId,
projectileId=projectileId,serverTime=workspace:GetServerTimeNow(),
pingMs=track.pingMs,ownerName=track.ownerName,
ownerUserId=track.ownerUserId,localOwner=track.localOwner,
targetName=track.targetName,source=track.source})
end)
end
local function isGun(tool:Tool):boolean
return tool.Name=="Gun"
or CollectionService:HasTag(tool,"Weapon_Gun")
or tool:FindFirstChild("Shoot")~=nil
end
local function isKnife(tool:Tool):boolean
return tool.Name=="Knife"
or CollectionService:HasTag(tool,"Weapon_Knife")
or(tool:FindFirstChild("Events")~=nil
and tool.Events:FindFirstChild("KnifeThrown")~=nil)
end
local function beginPendingShot(kind:string,entry:any,target:Player?):any
local now:number=entry.activatedAt or os.clock()
entry.activatedAt=now
entry.serverActivatedAt=workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
entry.targetUserId=target and target.UserId or entry.targetUserId
entry.targetName=target and target.Name or entry.targetName
if entry.targetTrajectory==nil then
entry.targetTrajectory={}
if target then
local point:TrajectoryPoint?=captureTrajectoryPoint(
target,
now,
entry.pingMs or 0
)
if point then
table.insert(entry.targetTrajectory,point)
end
end
end
if entry.targetHistory==nil then
entry.targetHistory=captureTargetHistory(target,now)
end
entry.lastTrajectorySampleAt=entry.lastTrajectorySampleAt or now
watchPendingHealth(entry,target)
local queue:{any}=kind=="gun"
and runtime.pendingGuns
or runtime.pendingKnives
table.insert(queue,entry)
compactPending(queue,now)
ensureTrajectorySampler()
return entry
end
local function observeTool(tool:Tool):()
if runtime.observedTools[tool]then
return
end
local gun:boolean=isGun(tool)
local knife:boolean=isKnife(tool)
if not gun and not knife then
return
end
runtime.observedTools[tool]=true
trackConnection(tool.Activated:Connect(function():()
if not runtime.active then
return
end
local now:number=os.clock()
local requestedAim:Vector3?=getRequestedAim()
local pingMs:number=getPingMilliseconds()
local target:Player?=gun
and findMurderer()
or findKnifeAnalysisTarget(requestedAim)
local targetTrajectory:{TrajectoryPoint}={}
if target then
local initialPoint:TrajectoryPoint?=captureTrajectoryPoint(
target,
now,
pingMs
)
if initialPoint then
table.insert(targetTrajectory,initialPoint)
end
end
local shotId:string=nextShotId()
appendEvent({shotId=shotId,kind=gun and"gun"or"knifeActivation",
phase=gun and"attempt"or"activation",
serverTime=workspace:GetServerTimeNow(),pingMs=pingMs,
targetName=target and target.Name or nil})
emitShot("authoringShot",{shotId=shotId,kind=gun and"gun"or"knifeActivation",
toolName=tool.Name,originPos=toolOrigin(tool),aimPos=requestedAim,
targetName=target and target.Name or nil,eventSource="tool-activation"})
if gun then
runtime.sessionGunAttempts+=1
beginPendingShot("gun",{
shotId=shotId,
source="native-activation",
attemptCounted=true,
tool=tool,
activatedAt=now,
origin=toolOrigin(tool),
requestedAim=requestedAim,
pingMs=pingMs,
targetTrajectory=targetTrajectory,
},target)
elseif knife then
runtime.rawKnifeActivations+=1
beginPendingShot("knife",{
shotId=shotId,
attemptCounted=false,
tool=tool,
activatedAt=now,
origin=toolOrigin(tool),
requestedAim=requestedAim,
pingMs=pingMs,
targetTrajectory=targetTrajectory,
},target)
end
end))
end
local function observeContainer(container:Instance?):()
if not container then
return
end
for _,child:Instance in ipairs(container:GetChildren())do
if child:IsA("Tool")then
observeTool(child)
end
end
trackConnection(container.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child:Instance):()
if child:IsA("Tool")then
observeTool(child)
end
end))
end
local function installGunConfirmation(generation:number):()
local function current():boolean
return runtime.active and runtime.observationGeneration==generation
end
task.spawn(function():()
local clientServices:Instance?=nil
local weaponModule:Instance?=nil
for _attempt:number=1,20 do
if not current()then
return
end
clientServices=game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
:FindFirstChild("ClientServices")
weaponModule=clientServices
and clientServices:FindFirstChild("WeaponService")
if weaponModule then
break
end
task.wait(0.25)
end
if not weaponModule then
warn(trajectoryLogPrefix.." WeaponService did not appear; Knife remains active.")
return
end
local loaded:boolean,weaponService:any=pcall(
state.requireModule,
weaponModule
)
if not current()then return end
if not loaded or type(weaponService)~="table"then
warn(
trajectoryLogPrefix.." Could not observe WeaponService: "
..tostring(weaponService)
)
return
end
pcall(function():()
local signal:any=weaponService.KnifeThrown and weaponService.KnifeThrown.Event
if not signal or type(signal.Connect)~="function"then return end
trackConnection(signal:Connect(function():()
if not current()then return end
local knife:Tool?=getPlayerWeapon(LocalPlayer,"Knife",true)
if not knife or knife.Parent~=LocalPlayer.Character then return end
local aim:Vector3?=getRequestedAim()
controller:noteAuthoredShot({
kind="knife",tool=knife,toolName=knife.Name,
originPos=toolOrigin(knife),aimPos=aim,
target=findKnifeAnalysisTarget(aim),source="native-throw-event",
settings={mode="Manual",aimScope="mouse at input; actual redirected aim may differ"},
})
end))
end)
local connected:boolean,connection:any=pcall(function():any
return weaponService.GunFired.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(...:any):()
if current()then handleGunFired(...)end
end)
end)
if connected and connection then
trackConnection(connection::RBXScriptConnection)
print(trajectoryLogPrefix.." GunFired confirmation connected.")
else
warn(
trajectoryLogPrefix.." GunFired no es compatible: "
..tostring(connection)
)
end
end)
end
controller={}::Controller
function controller:prepare():()
if not runtime.historyLoaded then
runtime.historyLoaded=true
loadSavedHistory()
end
end
function controller:start():()
local states:any=type(configData)=="table"and configData.states or nil
if not states or states["MM2.Calibration.Consent"]~=true then
return
end
if runtime.active then
return
end
if not GAME_CHECK.MM2Active then
warn(trajectoryLogPrefix.." Inicio omitido fuera de MM2.")
return
end
runtime.active=true
runtime.observationGeneration+=1
local generation:number=runtime.observationGeneration
runtime.startedAt=os.clock()
runtime.startedAtUnix=os.time()
self:prepare()
if type(task)=="table"and type(task.spawn)=="function"then
task.spawn(function():()
for _attempt:number=1,20 do
if not runtime.active or runtime.observationGeneration~=generation then return end
if applyLearnedSeeds()then return end
task.wait(0.5)
end
end)
end
observeContainer(LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack"))
observeContainer(LocalPlayer.Character)
trackConnection(LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(
character:Model
):()
observeContainer(character)
end))
trackConnection(
CollectionService:GetInstanceAddedSignal("ThrowingKnife"):Connect(
resolveKnifeProjectile
)
)
pcall(function():()
for _,projectile:Instance in ipairs(CollectionService:GetTagged("ThrowingKnife"))do
resolveKnifeProjectile(projectile)
end
end)
installGunConfirmation(generation)
print(trajectoryLogPrefix.." Passive calibration started in the background.")
end
local function clearHistory():()
runtime.gunAcceptance=newStats()
runtime.knifeSpeed=newStats()
runtime.knifeSpawnDelay=newStats()
runtime.motionBuckets={}
runtime.events={}
runtime.archivedEvents=0
runtime.archiveFiles={}
runtime.archiveSequence=0
runtime.freshGunLatency={}
estimatesCache=nil
estimatesAt=-math.huge
runtime.sessionGunAttempts=0
runtime.sessionGunConfirmed=0
runtime.sessionKnifeAttempts=0
runtime.sessionKnifeConfirmed=0
runtime.sessionKnifeObserved=0
runtime.rawKnifeActivations=0
runtime.unmatchedGunSignals=0
runtime.sessionStabAttempts=0
runtime.startedAt=os.clock()
runtime.startedAtUnix=os.time()
for _,track in pairs(runtime.tracks)do
if track.destroyConnection then track.destroyConnection:Disconnect()end
end
for _,queue in ipairs({runtime.pendingGuns,runtime.pendingKnives})do
for _,pending in ipairs(queue)do
if pending.healthConnection then pending.healthConnection:Disconnect()end
end
end
table.clear(runtime.tracks)
disconnectFeatureConnection("MM2TrajectorySampler")
runtime.sessionId=HttpService:GenerateGUID(false)
runtime.shotSequence=0
table.clear(runtime.pendingGuns)
table.clear(runtime.pendingKnives)
end
local function consentValue():boolean?
local states:any=type(configData)=="table"and configData.states or nil
if not states then
return nil
end
local value:any=states["MM2.Calibration.Consent"]
if value==true then return true end
if value==false then return false end
return nil
end
function controller:getConsent():boolean?
return consentValue()
end
function controller:setConsent(value:boolean?):boolean
local nextValue:boolean?=nil
if value==true then
nextValue=true
elseif value==false then
nextValue=false
end
if type(configData)=="table"and configData.states then
configData.states["MM2.Calibration.Consent"]=nextValue
end
if type(host.queueConfigSave)=="function"then
pcall(host.queueConfigSave)
end
if nextValue==true then
self:start()
if not runtime.active then return false end
runtime.dirty=true
local generation:number=runtime.observationGeneration
local queued:boolean=runtime.snapshotInProgress
local saved:boolean=saveSnapshot("consent")
if runtime.observationGeneration~=generation then return saved or queued end
if not saved and not queued then
runtime.dirty=false
self:destroy()
end
return saved or queued
end
runtime.saveScheduled=false
runtime.dirty=false
if runtime.active then self:destroy()end
return true
end
function controller:save(reason:string?):boolean
if not runtime.active then
return false
end
local saved:boolean=saveSnapshot(reason or"manual")
return saved
end
function controller:reset():()
if consentValue()~=true or not runtime.active or runtime.persistenceBlocked then return end
clearHistory()
runtime.revision+=1
runtime.dirty=true
local queued:boolean=runtime.snapshotInProgress
local saved:boolean=saveSnapshot("reset")
if saved or queued then
print(trajectoryLogPrefix.." History cleared.")
else
warn(trajectoryLogPrefix.." Cleared in memory; the new snapshot could not be saved.")
end
end
function controller:getEstimates():Estimates
return estimates()
end
function controller:noteTracerData(info:any):()
if not runtime.active or type(info)~="table"then return end
local function validVector(value:any):boolean
return typeof(value)=="Vector3"
and predictionMath.finite(value.X)and predictionMath.finite(value.Y)
and predictionMath.finite(value.Z)and value.Magnitude<1e7
end
if not validVector(info.origin)or not validVector(info.endpoint)then return end
local function pack(value:Vector3):{number}
return{value.X,value.Y,value.Z}
end
local shotId:string?=nil
local now:number=os.clock()
for index:number=#runtime.pendingGuns,1,-1 do
local pending:PendingGun=runtime.pendingGuns[index]
if now-pending.activatedAt<=2.5 and pending.origin
and(pending.origin-info.origin).Magnitude<=8 then
shotId=pending.shotId
break
end
end
local target:any=info.target
local targetPosition:Vector3?=validVector(info.targetPosition)and info.targetPosition or nil
local path:string?=nil
local hitTarget:boolean?=nil
if typeof(info.hitPart)=="Instance"then
pcall(function():()path=string.sub(info.hitPart:GetFullName(),1,512)end)
if target and target.Character then
hitTarget=info.hitPart:IsDescendantOf(target.Character)
end
end
appendEvent({kind="gun",phase="tracer-data",shotId=shotId,
serverTime=workspace:GetServerTimeNow(),pingMs=getPingMilliseconds(),
origin=pack(info.origin),endpoint=pack(info.endpoint),
shotRange=(info.endpoint-info.origin).Magnitude,
targetUserId=target and target.UserId or nil,
targetName=target and target.Name or nil,
observedTargetPosition=targetPosition and pack(targetPosition)or nil,
endpointToObservedTarget=targetPosition and(info.endpoint-targetPosition).Magnitude or nil,
targetPositionScope="client-observed-at-GunFired-receipt; not server impact-time position",
hitPartPath=path,hitTarget=hitTarget,evidence="GunFired endpoint/hitPart",
})
end
function controller:noteBlockedShot(blocker:any):()
if not runtime.active or type(blocker)~="table"then
return
end
local now:number=os.clock()
if now-runtime.lastBlockedAt<1 then
return
end
runtime.lastBlockedAt=now
appendEvent({
kind="gun",
phase="blocked",
serverTime=workspace:GetServerTimeNow(),
pingMs=getPingMilliseconds(),
reason=type(blocker.reason)=="string"and blocker.reason or"wall",
blockedName=type(blocker.name)=="string"and blocker.name or nil,
blockedTransparency=tonumber(blocker.transparency),
blockedCanCollide=blocker.canCollide==true,
blockedSkipped=blocker.skipped==true,
blockedDistance=tonumber(blocker.distance),
})
end
function controller:noteAuthoredShot(info:any):()
if not runtime.active or type(info)~="table"then
return
end
local kind:string=tostring(info.kind or"gun")
local shotId:string=nextShotId()
local now:number=os.clock()
local pingMs:number=getPingMilliseconds()
local target:Player?=
info.target and info.target.Character~=nil and info.target or nil
local targetHistory:{HistoryPoint}=captureTargetHistory(target,now)
if kind~="stab"then
appendEvent({kind=kind,shotId=shotId,phase="authored",
serverTime=workspace:GetServerTimeNow(),clock=now,
pingMs=pingMs,
settings=info.settings,source=info.source or"menu",
origin=typeof(info.originPos)=="Vector3"and vectorArray(info.originPos)or nil,
endpoint=typeof(info.aimPos)=="Vector3"and vectorArray(info.aimPos)or nil,
targetUserId=target and target.UserId or nil,
targetName=target and target.Name or nil,
targetHistory=#targetHistory>0 and targetHistory or nil})
end
local targetTrajectory:{TrajectoryPoint}={}
if target then
local initialPoint:TrajectoryPoint?=captureTrajectoryPoint(
target,
now,
pingMs
)
if initialPoint then
table.insert(targetTrajectory,initialPoint)
end
end
if kind=="stab"then
runtime.sessionStabAttempts+=1
local targetRoot:BasePart?=getTargetRoot(target)
local localRoot:BasePart?=
LocalPlayer.Character
and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
::BasePart?
local distance:number?=targetRoot
and localRoot
and(targetRoot.Position-localRoot.Position).Magnitude
appendEvent({
kind="stab",shotId=shotId,phase="attempt",
serverTime=workspace:GetServerTimeNow(),
pingMs=pingMs,
distance=distance and math.floor(distance*10+0.5)/10 or nil,
targetUserId=target and target.UserId or nil,
targetName=target and target.Name or nil,
targetState=targetTrajectory[1]
and targetTrajectory[1].humanoidState
or nil,
targetTrajectory=#targetTrajectory>0 and targetTrajectory or nil,
targetHistory=#targetHistory>0 and targetHistory or nil,
})
else
local isGun:boolean=kind~="knife"
local authoredTool:Tool?=nil
if typeof(info.tool)=="Instance"and info.tool:IsA("Tool")then
authoredTool=info.tool::Tool
end
if isGun or authoredTool~=nil then
if isGun then
local countedActivation:boolean=false
if info.source=="native-redirect"and authoredTool then
for index:number=#runtime.pendingGuns,1,-1 do
local pending:any=runtime.pendingGuns[index]
local age:number=now-(pending.activatedAt or now)
if pending.source=="native-activation"and pending.tool==authoredTool
and not pending.confirmedAt and age>=0 and age<=0.3 then
if pending.healthConnection then pending.healthConnection:Disconnect()end
table.remove(runtime.pendingGuns,index)
countedActivation=true
break
end
end
end
if not countedActivation then runtime.sessionGunAttempts+=1 end
else
runtime.sessionKnifeAttempts+=1
if info.source=="native-throw-event"then
for index:number=#runtime.pendingKnives,1,-1 do
local pending:any=runtime.pendingKnives[index]
local age:number=now-(pending.activatedAt or now)
if not pending.attemptCounted and pending.tool==authoredTool
and age>=0 and age<=0.3 then
if pending.healthConnection then pending.healthConnection:Disconnect()end
table.remove(runtime.pendingKnives,index)
break
end
end
end
end
beginPendingShot(isGun and"gun"or"knife",{
shotId=shotId,
attemptCounted=true,
source=info.source or"menu",
tool=authoredTool,
shotMode=type(info.settings)=="table"
and info.settings.mode
or(info.silent and"Silent"or nil),
silentSpawn=info.variant,
activatedAt=now,
origin=info.originPos,
requestedAim=info.aimPos,
requestedRoot=typeof(info.aimRoot)=="Vector3"
and info.aimRoot
or nil,
aimVelocity=typeof(info.aimVelocity)=="Vector3"
and info.aimVelocity
or nil,
aimLeadSeconds=predictionMath.finite(info.leadSeconds)
and info.leadSeconds
or nil,
pingMs=pingMs,
targetTrajectory=targetTrajectory,
targetHistory=targetHistory,
},target)
end
end
if type(state.emitGameBridgeEvent)=="function"then
emitShot("authoringShot",{
shotId=shotId,
eventSource="authored",
settings=info.settings,
kind=kind,
toolName=tostring(info.toolName or(kind=="knife"and"Knife"or"Gun")),
originPos=info.originPos,
aimPos=info.aimPos,
targetName=target and target.Name or nil,
silent=info.silent==true,
variant=info.variant,
offset=info.offset,
})
end
end
function controller:status():string
local current:Estimates=estimates()
local knifeText:string=current.knifeSpeed
and string.format("%.2f studs/s",current.knifeSpeed)
or"learning"
local bucketCount:number=0
for _key:string in pairs(runtime.motionBuckets)do
bucketCount+=1
end
local consent:boolean?=consentValue()
local consentText:string=consent==true
and((runtime.persistenceBlocked or type(environment.writefile)~="function")
and"saving unavailable; see console"or"giving calibration")
or(consent==false and"not giving calibration")
or"calibration not answered"
return string.format(
"MM2 session observations · guns %d · knives %d · motion buckets %d · knife %s · %s",
runtime.sessionGunConfirmed,
runtime.sessionKnifeConfirmed,
bucketCount,
knifeText,
consentText
)
end
function controller:destroy():()
if not runtime.active then
return
end
runtime.observationGeneration+=1
if consentValue()==true then
local projectiles:{Instance}={}
for projectile:Instance in pairs(runtime.tracks)do table.insert(projectiles,projectile)end
for _,projectile:Instance in ipairs(projectiles)do
finishKnifeTrack(projectile,"menu-unload")
end
end
if runtime.dirty then saveSnapshot("module-destroy")end
runtime.active=false
disconnectFeatureConnection("MM2TrajectorySampler")
disconnectFeatureConnection("MM2KnifeSampler")
if runtime.trajectorySampler then
runtime.trajectorySampler:Disconnect()
runtime.trajectorySampler=nil
end
for projectile:Instance,track:KnifeTrack in pairs(runtime.tracks)do
if track.destroyConnection then
track.destroyConnection:Disconnect()
end
if track.pending and track.pending.healthConnection then
track.pending.healthConnection:Disconnect()
end
runtime.tracks[projectile]=nil
end
for _,connection:RBXScriptConnection in ipairs(runtime.connections)do
connection:Disconnect()
end
table.clear(runtime.connections)
for _,queue in ipairs({runtime.pendingGuns,runtime.pendingKnives})do
for _,pending in ipairs(queue)do
if pending.healthConnection then pending.healthConnection:Disconnect()end
end
table.clear(queue)
end
table.clear(runtime.observedTools)
end
return controller
end
trajectoryCalibration=createTrajectoryCalibration()
state.mm2TrajectoryCalibration=trajectoryCalibration
trajectoryCalibration:prepare()
local calibrationPromptLive:boolean=true
local calibrationStates:any=type(configData)=="table"and configData.states or{}
local rememberedCalibration:boolean=calibrationStates["MM2.Calibration.DontShowAgain"]==true
and type(calibrationStates["MM2.Calibration.Consent"])=="boolean"
if rememberedCalibration then
if calibrationStates["MM2.Calibration.Consent"]==true then
if not trajectoryCalibration:setConsent(true)then
notify("Calibration could not save; check the console and executor file support.")
end
end
else
calibrationStates["MM2.Calibration.Consent"]=nil
task.delay(3,function():()
if not calibrationPromptLive
or(type(state.ownsRuntimeData)=="function"and not state.ownsRuntimeData())then
return
end
if type(state.confirmDialog)~="function"then return end
local function answer(value:boolean,remember:boolean):()
if not calibrationPromptLive
or(type(state.ownsRuntimeData)=="function"and not state.ownsRuntimeData())then
return
end
calibrationStates["MM2.Calibration.DontShowAgain"]=remember==true
local saved:boolean=trajectoryCalibration:setConsent(value)
if value then
if saved then
notify("Calibration ON: saving observed MM2 knife and shot data to Wurst/Telemetry.")
else
notify("Calibration could not save; check the console and executor file support.")
end
else
notify("Calibration OFF: no new data will be recorded; previous files are kept.")
end
end
state.confirmDialog({
title="Give your calibration?",
message="Would you like to save the MM2 knives and shots your client observes to your executor workspace for analysis? Previous files are kept if you choose No.",
yes="Yes",
no="No",
rememberLabel="Don't Show Again",
onYes=function(remember:boolean):()answer(true,remember)end,
onNo=function(remember:boolean):()answer(false,remember)end,
})
end)
end
pcall(function():()
if type((game::any).BindToClose)=="function"then
game:BindToClose(function():()
if type(trajectoryCalibration.save)=="function"then
pcall(trajectoryCalibration.save,trajectoryCalibration,"close")
end
end)
end
end)
local weaponServiceModule:any=nil
type PendingShot={
target:Player,
targetCharacter:Model?,
gun:Tool,
origin:Vector3,
aimPosition:Vector3?,
healthBefore:number,
targetedMurderer:boolean,
queuedAt:number,
ambiguous:boolean?
}
state.mm2ShotFeedback={
shootActive=false,
pending=nil::PendingShot?,
lastAccepted=nil::PendingShot?,
token=0,
defaultDuration=5,
gunFiredConnected=false,
theme={
Background=Color3.fromRGB(14,14,18),
Accent=Color3.fromRGB(205,82,42),
Outline=Color3.fromRGB(220,115,58),
Text=Color3.fromRGB(255,232,208),
},
resolve=function(_candidate:PendingShot,_directHit:boolean):()end,
showMiss=function(_gun:Tool,_candidate:PendingShot):()end,
}
state.mm2ShotFeedback.root=create("Frame",{
Parent=ScreenGui,
Name="MM2ShotCooldown",
AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,0.5),
BackgroundColor3=state.mm2ShotFeedback.theme.Background,
BackgroundTransparency=0.38,
BorderSizePixel=0,
ClipsDescendants=true,
Position=UDim2.fromScale(0.5,0.62),
Size=UDim2.fromOffset(320,36),
Visible=false,
ZIndex=240,
})::Frame
create("UICorner",{
Parent=state.mm2ShotFeedback.root,
CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,7),
})
create("UIStroke",{
Parent=state.mm2ShotFeedback.root,
Color=state.mm2ShotFeedback.theme.Outline,
Transparency=0.35,
Thickness=1,
})
state.mm2ShotFeedback.fill=create("Frame",{
Parent=state.mm2ShotFeedback.root,
Name="Fill",
BackgroundColor3=state.mm2ShotFeedback.theme.Accent,
BackgroundTransparency=0.46,
BorderSizePixel=0,
Size=UDim2.fromScale(0,1),
ZIndex=241,
})::Frame
create("UICorner",{
Parent=state.mm2ShotFeedback.fill,
CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,7),
})
state.mm2ShotFeedback.text=create("TextLabel",{
TextTruncate=Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
ClipsDescendants=true,
Parent=state.mm2ShotFeedback.root,
Name="Countdown",
BackgroundTransparency=1,
Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold,
Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1),
Text="",
TextColor3=state.mm2ShotFeedback.theme.Text,
TextSize=13,
ZIndex=242,
})::TextLabel
state.mm2ShotFeedback.hide=function():()
disconnectFeatureConnection("MM2ShootCooldown")
state.mm2ShotFeedback.token+=1
state.mm2ShotFeedback.root.Visible=false
state.mm2ShotFeedback.fill.Size=UDim2.fromScale(0,1)
state.mm2ShotFeedback.text.Text=""
end
state.mm2ShotFeedback.getDuration=function(gun:Tool?):number
if gun then
for _,attributeName:string in ipairs({
"Cooldown",
"ReloadTime",
"ShotCooldown",
})do
local value:number?=tonumber(gun:GetAttribute(attributeName))
if value and value>=0.5 and value<=15 then
return value
end
end
end
return state.mm2ShotFeedback.defaultDuration
end
state.mm2ShotFeedback.queue=function(
target:Player?,
gun:Tool?,
origin:CFrame?,
aim:CFrame?
):()
if not target or not gun or not origin then
return
end
local targetCharacter:Model?=target.Character
local targetHumanoid:Humanoid?=targetCharacter
and targetCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
::Humanoid?
local candidate:PendingShot={
target=target,
targetCharacter=targetCharacter,
gun=gun,
ambiguous=state.mm2ShotFeedback.pending~=nil,
origin=origin.Position,
aimPosition=aim and aim.Position,
healthBefore=targetHumanoid and targetHumanoid.Health or 0,
targetedMurderer=getPlayerRole(target)=="Murderer",
queuedAt=os.clock(),
}
state.mm2ShotFeedback.pending=candidate
task.delay(1.8,function():()
if state.mm2ShotFeedback.pending==candidate then
state.mm2ShotFeedback.pending=nil
end
end)
if not state.mm2ShotFeedback.gunFiredConnected then
task.delay(math.max(0.35,mm2Settings.predictionRtt+0.18),function():()
if state.mm2ShotFeedback.pending~=candidate
or state.mm2ShotFeedback.gunFiredConnected then
return
end
state.mm2ShotFeedback.pending=nil
state.mm2ShotFeedback.resolve(candidate,false)
end)
end
end
state.mm2ShotFeedback.showMiss=function(
gun:Tool,
candidate:PendingShot
):()
state.mm2ShotFeedback.hide()
local duration:number=state.mm2ShotFeedback.getDuration(gun)
local cooldownEndsAt:number=candidate.queuedAt+duration
local initialRemaining:number=cooldownEndsAt-os.clock()
if initialRemaining<=0 then
return
end
disconnectFeatureConnection("MM2ShootCooldown")
state.mm2ShotFeedback.token+=1
local token:number=state.mm2ShotFeedback.token
state.mm2ShotFeedback.root.Visible=true
local lastText:string=""
local lastScale:number=-1
local lastRefresh:number=-math.huge
local function updateCountdown():()
if token~=state.mm2ShotFeedback.token then
disconnectFeatureConnection("MM2ShootCooldown")
state.mm2ShotFeedback.root.Visible=false
return
end
local now:number=os.clock()
local remaining:number=math.max(0,cooldownEndsAt-now)
if remaining<=0 then
state.mm2ShotFeedback.hide()
return
end
if now-lastRefresh<0.08 then
return
end
lastRefresh=now
local progress:number=math.clamp(remaining/duration,0,1)
local scale:number=math.floor(progress*200+0.5)/200
if scale~=lastScale then
lastScale=scale
state.mm2ShotFeedback.fill.Size=UDim2.fromScale(scale,1)
end
local nextText:string=string.format("Miss cooldown - %.1fs",remaining)
if nextText~=lastText then
lastText=nextText
state.mm2ShotFeedback.text.Text=nextText
end
end
updateCountdown()
featureConnections.MM2ShootCooldown=
TaskManager:Connect(updateCountdown,nil,"Miss cooldown")
end
state.mm2ShotFeedback.toPosition=function(value:any):Vector3?
if typeof(value)=="Vector3"then
return value
end
if typeof(value)=="CFrame"then
return value.Position
end
if typeof(value)=="Instance"then
if value:IsA("Attachment")then
return value.WorldPosition
end
if value:IsA("BasePart")then
return value.Position
end
end
return nil
end
state.mm2ShotFeedback.resolve=function(
candidate:PendingShot,
directHit:boolean
):()
state.mm2ShotFeedback.hide()
state.mm2ShotFeedback.lastAccepted=candidate
local confirmationDelay:number=math.clamp(
math.max(0.18,mm2Settings.predictionRtt+0.12),
0.18,
0.55
)
task.delay(confirmationDelay,function():()
if state.mm2ShotFeedback.lastAccepted~=candidate then
return
end
state.mm2ShotFeedback.lastAccepted=nil
local targetCharacter:Model?=candidate.targetCharacter
local currentHumanoid:Humanoid?=targetCharacter
and targetCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
::Humanoid?
local hitConfirmed:boolean=directHit
or candidate.target.Parent==nil
or targetCharacter==nil
or targetCharacter.Parent==nil
or currentHumanoid==nil
or currentHumanoid.Health<=0
or currentHumanoid.Health<candidate.healthBefore
or(
candidate.targetedMurderer
and getPlayerRole(candidate.target)~="Murderer"
)
if hitConfirmed then
state.mm2ShotFeedback.hide()
end
if not hitConfirmed and mm2Settings.showMissCooldown then
state.mm2ShotFeedback.showMiss(candidate.gun,candidate)
end
end)
end
state.mm2ShotFeedback.handleGunFired=function(
firedGun:any,
eventOrigin:any,
endpoint:any,
hitPart:any
):()
if not state.mm2ShotFeedback.shootActive then
return
end
local candidate:PendingShot?=state.mm2ShotFeedback.pending
if not candidate then
return
end
if os.clock()-candidate.queuedAt>1.6 then
state.mm2ShotFeedback.pending=nil
return
end
local eventObject:Instance?=typeof(firedGun)=="Instance"
and firedGun
or nil
local eventTool:Tool?=nil
if eventObject then
if eventObject:IsA("Tool")then
eventTool=eventObject
else
eventTool=eventObject:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Tool")
end
end
local weaponMatches:boolean=eventTool==candidate.gun
or eventObject==candidate.gun
or(
eventObject~=nil
and eventObject:IsDescendantOf(candidate.gun)
)
local eventOriginPosition:Vector3?=
state.mm2ShotFeedback.toPosition(eventOrigin)
local originMatches:boolean=eventOriginPosition~=nil
and(eventOriginPosition-candidate.origin).Magnitude<=3.5
local endpointPosition:Vector3?=
state.mm2ShotFeedback.toPosition(endpoint)
local directionMatches:boolean=false
if eventOriginPosition and endpointPosition and candidate.aimPosition then
local eventDelta:Vector3=endpointPosition-eventOriginPosition
local expectedDelta:Vector3=
candidate.aimPosition-candidate.origin
if eventDelta.Magnitude>0.05 and expectedDelta.Magnitude>0.05 then
directionMatches=eventDelta.Unit:Dot(expectedDelta.Unit)>=0.965
end
end
if eventTool and not weaponMatches then return end
if not originMatches or not directionMatches or endpointPosition==nil then return end
state.mm2ShotFeedback.pending=nil
if candidate.ambiguous then return end
local targetCharacter:Model?=candidate.targetCharacter
local hitTarget:boolean=typeof(hitPart)=="Instance"
and targetCharacter~=nil
and hitPart:IsDescendantOf(targetCharacter)
state.mm2ShotFeedback.resolve(candidate,hitTarget)
end
type KnifeSettings={
aura:boolean,
auraRange:number
}
local function getWeaponServiceModule():any
if type(weaponServiceModule)=="table"then
return weaponServiceModule
end
local loaded:boolean,result:any=pcall(function():any
return state.requireModule(
game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
:WaitForChild("ClientServices")
:WaitForChild("WeaponService")
)
end)
if loaded and type(result)=="table"then
weaponServiceModule=result
return result
end
return nil
end
local function connectGunFiredSignal(weaponService:any):boolean
if featureConnections.MM2GunFired then
state.mm2ShotFeedback.gunFiredConnected=true
return true
end
local gunFired:any=weaponService.GunFired
local connected:boolean,connection:any=pcall(function():any
return gunFired.OnClientEvent:Connect(
state.mm2ShotFeedback.handleGunFired
)
end)
if connected and connection then
featureConnections.MM2GunFired=connection
state.mm2ShotFeedback.gunFiredConnected=true
return true
end
state.mm2ShotFeedback.gunFiredConnected=false
return false
end
local function mm2RoleProvider(player:Player):string?
if roundLifecycleActive==false then
return nil
end
return getPlayerRole(player)
end
local function mm2TargetProvider():any
if not hasActiveRoundRoles()then
return nil
end
local localRole:string?=getPlayerRole(LocalPlayer)
local target:Player?=nil
local source:string="MM2 role priority"
if localRole=="Sheriff"or localRole=="Hero"then
target=findMurderer()
source="Sheriff priority · Murderer"
elseif localRole=="Murderer"then
target=findSheriff()
source=target and"Murderer priority · Sheriff/Hero"or"Murderer priority"
if not target then
for _,player:Player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers())do
if player~=LocalPlayer
and isPlayerAlive(player)
and not isProtectedTarget(player)then
target=player
break
end
end
end
else
target=findMurderer()
source="Threat priority · Murderer"
end
if state.mm2ShotFeedback then
local pending:any=state.mm2ShotFeedback.pending
or state.mm2ShotFeedback.lastAccepted
local pendingRole:string?=pending and pending.target
and getPlayerRole(pending.target)
or nil
local preservesRolePriority:boolean=localRole~="Sheriff"
and localRole~="Hero"
or pendingRole=="Murderer"
local selectedRole:string?=target and getPlayerRole(target)or nil
if localRole=="Murderer"
and(selectedRole=="Sheriff"or selectedRole=="Hero")then
preservesRolePriority=pendingRole=="Sheriff"or pendingRole=="Hero"
end
if pending
and pending.target
and isPlayerAlive(pending.target)
and preservesRolePriority then
target=pending.target
source="Recent shot target"
end
end
if not target then
return nil
end
local role:string?=getPlayerRole(target)
return{
player=target,
role=role,
source=source,
accent=role=="Murderer"
and Theme.negative
or((role=="Sheriff"or role=="Hero")
and Theme.accent
or Theme.positive),
}
end
state.gameRoleProvider=mm2RoleProvider
state.gameTargetProvider=mm2TargetProvider
moduleCleanup=function():()
unregisterMM2Roles()
if state.gameRoleProvider==mm2RoleProvider then
state.gameRoleProvider=nil
end
if state.gameTargetProvider==mm2TargetProvider then
state.gameTargetProvider=nil
end
end
local function validateGunShot(target:Player,character:Model,targetCharacter:Model,
muzzle:Vector3,origin:Vector3,endpoint:Vector3,walls:boolean):(boolean,string?,any?,any?)
if LocalPlayer.Character~=character or not target or target==LocalPlayer
or target.Character~=targetCharacter or not isPlayerAlive(target)
or isProtectedTarget(target)or getPlayerRole(target)~="Murderer"then
return false,"Target changed or is not the murderer."
end
if roundLifecycleActive==false then return false,"The round is not active."end
local roundRole=getRoundRole(target)
if roundRole and roundRole~="Murderer"then return false,"Conflicting target role."end
if not roundRole and not getPlayerWeapon(target,"Knife")and not hasMurdererCollisionGroup(targetCharacter)then
return false,"No current murderer evidence."
end
local ownHumanoid=character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not ownHumanoid or not predictionMath.finite(ownHumanoid.Health)or ownHumanoid.Health<=0 then return false,"Your character is not alive."end
if not predictionMath.finite(muzzle.Magnitude)or not predictionMath.finite(origin.Magnitude)
or not predictionMath.finite(endpoint.Magnitude)or(endpoint-origin).Magnitude<0.05
or(endpoint-muzzle).Magnitude<0.05 then return false,"Invalid shot geometry."end
if walls then
local options={ignoreTarget=true}
local clear,blocker=isWeaponPathClear(muzzle,endpoint,targetCharacter,nil,options)
if not clear then
return false,"A wall or another body blocks the shot.",blocker
end
local solidClear,solidBlocker=isWeaponPathClear(muzzle,endpoint,targetCharacter,true,options)
if not solidClear then
return false,"An invisible wall blocks the shot.",solidBlocker
end
if(origin-muzzle).Magnitude>0.05 then
local originClear,originBlocker=isWeaponPathClear(origin,endpoint,targetCharacter,nil,options)
if not originClear then
return false,"A wall or another body blocks the shot.",originBlocker
end
local originSolid,originSolidBlocker=isWeaponPathClear(origin,endpoint,targetCharacter,true,options)
if not originSolid then
return false,"An invisible wall blocks the shot.",originSolidBlocker
end
end
end
local occluder:any=nil
if walls then
pcall(function():()
local first:any=describeRayHit(muzzle,endpoint,targetCharacter)
local reach:number=(endpoint-muzzle).Magnitude
if first and not first.onTarget
and predictionMath.finite(first.distance)
and first.distance<reach-0.5 then
occluder=first
end
end)
end
return true,nil,nil,occluder
end
local function disconnectGunFiredObserver():()
disconnectFeatureConnection("MM2GunFired")
state.mm2ShotFeedback.gunFiredConnected=false
end
registerMM2Roles()
state.mm2Core={
settings=mm2Settings,
mm2Settings=mm2Settings,
MM2Effects=MM2Effects,
GunEffects=GunEffects,
TrapEffects=TrapEffects,
CoinEffects=CoinEffects,
clearEffects=clearEffects,
createMM2Marker=createMM2Marker,
createMM2Cham=createMM2Cham,
mm2GameplayRemotes=mm2GameplayRemotes,
getRoundData=function():{[any]:any}
return mm2RoundData
end,
isRoundLifecycleActive=function():boolean?
return roundLifecycleActive
end,
onRoundRoles=onRoundRoles,
requestRoleRefresh=requestRoleRefresh,
onRoundPhase=onRoundPhase,
getRoundPhase=function():string
return roundPhase
end,
roundTimer=roundTimer,
findRoundTimerPart=findRoundTimerPart,
readRoundTimerPartSeconds=readRoundTimerPartSeconds,
getPlayerWeapon=getPlayerWeapon,
isPlayerAlive=isPlayerAlive,
findMM2Role=findMM2Role,
playerFromRoundKey=playerFromRoundKey,
getRoundRole=getRoundRole,
getPlayerRole=getPlayerRole,
getPlayerPerk=getPlayerPerk,
invalidateRoleCaches=invalidateRoleCaches,
roundRolesKnown=roundRolesKnown,
hasActiveRoundRoles=hasActiveRoundRoles,
findMurderer=findMurderer,
findSheriff=findSheriff,
isProtectedTarget=isProtectedTarget,
findMM2Map=findMM2Map,
findDroppedGun=findDroppedGun,
isWeaponPathClear=isWeaponPathClear,
describeRayHit=describeRayHit,
validateGunShot=validateGunShot,
predictionMath=predictionMath,
getEstimatedLatency=getEstimatedLatency,
getLatencyReadings=readLatencyReadings,
getGunOriginCFrame=getGunOriginCFrame,
trajectoryCalibration=trajectoryCalibration,
getWeaponServiceModule=getWeaponServiceModule,
connectGunFiredSignal=connectGunFiredSignal,
disconnectGunFiredObserver=disconnectGunFiredObserver,
}
cleanupMM2Runtime=function()
calibrationPromptLive=false
unregisterMM2Roles()
if state.gameRoleProvider==mm2RoleProvider then
state.gameRoleProvider=nil
end
if state.gameTargetProvider==mm2TargetProvider then
state.gameTargetProvider=nil
end
trajectoryCalibration:destroy()
disconnectGunFiredObserver()
state.mm2ShotFeedback.hide()
for player:Player,_ in pairs(roleWatchers)do
unwatchPlayer(player)
end
table.clear(roleIndex.roles)
for _,connectionName:string in ipairs({
"MM2PlayerDataChanged",
"MM2TimerTrackerStart",
"MM2TimerTrackerEnd",
"MM2RoundPhase",
"MM2RoundClock",
"MM2RoleWatchAdded",
"MM2RoleWatchRemoving",
"MM2RoleTagAddedWeapon_Knife",
"MM2RoleTagRemovedWeapon_Knife",
"MM2RoleTagAddedWeapon_Gun",
"MM2RoleTagRemovedWeapon_Gun",
})do
disconnectFeatureConnection(connectionName)
end
state.isProtectedTarget=function(player:Player?):boolean
return player==nil or player==LocalPlayer
end
table.clear(roundRolesListeners)
table.clear(roundPhaseListeners)
state.mm2Core=nil
end
end
function Module.init(runtime:any):any
assert(type(runtime)=="table","MM2 requires a Runtime table")
assert(type(runtime.Menu)=="table","MM2 requires Runtime.Menu")
assert(runtime.TaskManager~=nil,"MM2 requires Runtime.TaskManager")
if Module.Initialized then
return Module
end
Module.Menu=runtime.Menu
Module.Runtime=runtime
local success:boolean,buildError:any=pcall(buildMM2Features)
if not success then
moduleCleanup()
error(buildError,0)
end
local environment:any=getfenv()
if type(environment.cleanupMM2Runtime)=="function"then
moduleCleanup=environment.cleanupMM2Runtime
end
Module.Events=featureConnections
Module.Initialized=true
return Module
end
function Module.destroy():()
if not Module.Initialized then
return
end
Module.Initialized=false
moduleCleanup()
Module.Events={}
Module.Menu=nil
Module.Runtime=nil
end
return Module
-- FIN src/games/MM2/base.lua

teamResolver():any local hostState:any=host.state if hostState and hostState.gameBridge then return hostState.gameBridge.teamProvider end return nil end local function local Module={Name='GameRoles',Initialized=false}local active:any=nil function Module.init(host:any):any local bridge=host.services.gameBridge local service={bindings={}}function service:Register(provider:any):()->()assert(type(provider)=='table'and type(provider.Get)=='function','GameRoles needs Get(player)')assert(type(provider.Roles)=='table','GameRoles needs a Roles array')bridge.registerRoles(provider)return function()bridge.unregisterRoles(provider)end end function service:Bind(card:any,settings:any?):any settings=settings or{}local binding={provider=nil,rows={},roles={},dead=false}local function visibility(option,visible)if visible then option.Show=nil else option.Show={Option='__GameRolesAbsent',Values={true}}end if option.SetVisible then option:SetVisible(visible)end end local function update(provider)if binding.dead then return end binding.provider=type(provider)=='table'and provider or nil table.clear(binding.roles)if binding.provider then for _,role in ipairs(type(provider.Roles)=='table'and provider.Roles or{})do if type(role)=='string'and role~=''and#binding.roles<32 and not table.find(binding.roles,role)then table.insert(binding.roles,role)end end end if settings.palette~=false and#binding.roles>0 and not card.Options['Role colours']then binding.rows['Role colours']=card:CreateToggle({Name='Role colours',Default=true})end for _,role in ipairs(binding.roles)do local colourName=role..' colour'if settings.palette~=false and not binding.rows[colourName]then local colour=provider.Colors and provider.Colors[role]if type(provider.GetColor)=='function'then local ok,value=pcall(provider.GetColor,role)if ok and typeof(value)=='Color3'then colour=value end end binding.rows[colourName]=card:CreateColor({Name=colourName,Default=typeof(colour)=='Color3'and colour or Color3.fromRGB(236,236,240),Show={Option='Role colours'}})end if settings.filters and not binding.rows[role..' ESP']then binding.rows[role..' ESP']=card:CreateToggle({Name=role..' ESP',Default=true})end end for name,option in pairs(binding.rows)do local present=name=='Role colours'and#binding.roles>0 for _,role in ipairs(binding.roles)do if name==role..' colour'or name==role..' ESP'then present=true break end end visibility(option,present)if present and name~='Role colours'and string.sub(name,-7)==' colour'then option.Show={Option='Role colours'}end end card:RefreshVisibility()end function binding:Role(player)if not self.provider then return nil end local getter=bridge.playerRole or self.provider.Get local ok,role=pcall(getter,player)return ok and type(role)=='string'and table.find(self.roles,role)and role or nil end function binding:Resolve(player,fallback)local role=self:Role(player)local option=role and card.Options[role..' colour']local enabled=card.Options['Role colours']return option and enabled and enabled.Value and option.Value or fallback,role end function binding:Allows(player)local role=self:Role(player)local option=role and card.Options[role..' ESP']return not option or option.Value~=false end local disconnect=bridge.onEvent(function(kind,provider)if kind=='roles'then update(provider)end end)function binding:Destroy()if self.dead then return end self.dead=true if disconnect then disconnect()disconnect=nil end self.provider=nil local index=table.find(service.bindings,self)if index then table.remove(service.bindings,index)end end table.insert(service.bindings,binding)return binding end function service:Destroy()for _,binding in ipairs(table.clone(self.bindings))do binding:Destroy()end end host.services.gameRoles=service active=service Module.Initialized=true return service end function Module.destroy()if active then active:Destroy()active=nil end Module.Initialized=false end return Module

rectFromPoints(camera:Camera,viewport:Vector2,points:{Vector3},minimumAspect:number?):Rect?local minimumX:number,minimumY:number=math.huge,math.huge local maximumX:number,maximumY:number=-math.huge,-math.huge local inFront:number=0 for _,position:Vector3 in ipairs(points)do local point:Vector3=camera:WorldToViewportPoint(position)if point.Z>0 then inFront+=1 if point.X<minimumX then minimumX=point.X end if point.Y<minimumY then minimumY=point.Y end if point.X>maximumX then maximumX=point.X end if point.Y>maximumY then maximumY=point.Y end end end if inFront<2 then return nil end minimumX-=2 maximumX+=2 minimumY-=2 maximumY+=2 local width:number=maximumX-minimumX local height:number=maximumY-minimumY if minimumAspect and height>0 and bottom:number=math.min(maximumY,viewport.Y)if right-left<1 or bottom-top<1 then return nil end export type Module={Name='Targeting',PlaceId=0,Events={}::{[string]:any},Initialized=false}local activeLibrary:Library?=nil local function asBasePart(instance:Instance?):BasePart?if instance and instance:IsA('BasePart')then return instance::BasePart end return nil end function Module.init(context:any):Library local host:any=context.host or context local then local ok:boolean,location:any=pcall((userInput::UserInputService).GetMouseLocation,userInput)if ok and typeof(location)=='Vector2'then return location::Vector2 end end local viewport:Vector2=cameraObject.ViewportSize return Vector2.new(viewport.X/2,viewport.Y/2)end local function targetParts(entity:any,requested:string?):{BasePart}local character:Model=entity.Character local wanted:string=requested or'Closest'if wanted=='Head'then return{asBasePart(character:FindFirstChild('Head'))or entity.RootPart}end if wanted=='Torso'then return{asBasePart(character:FindFirstChild('UpperTorso'))or asBasePart(character:FindFirstChild('Torso'))or entity.RootPart}end if wanted~='Closest'then return{entity.RootPart}end local head:BasePart?=asBasePart(character:FindFirstChild('Head'))if head then return{head,entity.RootPart}end return{entity.RootPart}end local function protected(player:Player?):boolean if not player then return false end local policy:any=services and services.protectedTargets if policy and type(policy.isProtected)=='function'then return policy.isProtected(player)==true end return false end local function visibleFrom(cameraObject:Camera,entity:any,position:Vector3):boolean local origin:Vector3=cameraObject.CFrame.Position local parameters:RaycastParams=RaycastParams.new()parameters.FilterType=Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude local ignored:{Instance}={entity.Character,cameraObject}if localPlayer and localPlayer.Character then table.insert(ignored,localPlayer.Character)end parameters.FilterDescendantsInstances=ignored parameters.IgnoreWater=true local ok:boolean,result:any=pcall(currentWorkspace.Raycast,currentWorkspace,origin,position-origin,parameters)return ok and result==nil end local function candidateForPart(cameraObject:Camera,pointer:Vector2,entity:any,part:BasePart,prediction:number,query:Query):Target?local velocity:Vector3=part.AssemblyLinearVelocity or Vector3.zero local predicted:Vector3=part.Position+velocity*prediction local projected:Vector3,onScreen:boolean=cameraObject:WorldToViewportPoint(predicted)if(not onScreen or projected.Z<=0)and not query.AllowOffscreen then return nil end local screenPosition:Vector2=Vector2.new(projected.X,projected.Y)local cursorDistance:number=onScreen and(screenPosition-pointer).Magnitude or math.huge if query.FOV~=nil and cursorDistance>(query.FOV::number)then return nil end if query.VisibilityCheck==true and not visibleFrom(cameraObject,entity,predicted)then return nil end return{player=entity.Player,entity=entity,character=entity.Character,part=part,position=predicted,screenPosition=screenPosition,distance=entity.Distance,cursorDistance=cursorDistance}end function library:List(entities:any,query:Query?):{Target}local options:Query=query or{}local cameraObject:Camera?=camera()if not cameraObject or not entities then return{}end if type(entities.Refresh)=='function'then entities:Refresh()end local pointer:Vector2=cursor(cameraObject::Camera)local found:{Target}={}local maxDistance:number=options.MaxDistance or math.huge local prediction:number=math.max(options.Prediction or 0,0)local function consider(entity:any):()if entity.Distance>maxDistance then return end if options.TeamCheck~=false then local hostile:boolean?=nil if entity.Player and type(entities.IsEnemy)=='function'then hostile=entities:IsEnemy(entity.Player)end if hostile==false or hostile==nil and entity.IsFriendly then return end end if protected(entity.Player)then return end local bestForEntity:Target?=nil for _,part:BasePart in ipairs(targetParts(entity,options.TargetPart))do local candidate:Target?=candidateForPart(cameraObject::Camera,pointer,entity,part,prediction,options)if candidate and(not bestForEntity or candidate.cursorDistance<(bestForEntity::Target).cursorDistance)then bestForEntity=candidate end end if bestForEntity then table.insert(found,bestForEntity::Target)end end for _,entity:any in ipairs(entities.List or{})do consider(entity)end if options.IncludeNPCs then for _,entity:any in ipairs(entities.NPCList or{})do consider(entity)end end local priority:string=options.Priority or'Cursor'table.sort(found,function(first:Target,second:Target):boolean if priority=='Distance'then if first.distance==second.distance then return first.cursorDistance<second.cursorDistance end return first.distance<second.distance end if first.cursorDistance==second.cursorDistance then return first.distance<second.distance end return first.cursorDistance<second.cursorDistance end)return found end function library:Select(entities:any,query:Query?):Target?return self:List(entities,query)[1]end function library:Destroy():()if activeLibrary==library then activeLibrary=nil end end activeLibrary=(library::any)::Library Module.Initialized=true return(library::any)::Library end function Module.destroy():()local library:Library?=activeLibrary if library then library:Destroy()end activeLibrary=nil Module.Initialized=false end return Module
-- FIN src/libraries/Targeting.lua

-- INICIO src/libraries/Weapons.lua
export type Candidate={
id:string,
label:string,
kind:string,
melee:boolean,
instance:Instance
}
export type GameSource={
scan:()->{{string}},
press:(label:string,target:any?)->boolean
}
export type WeaponLibrary={
Scan:(self:WeaponLibrary,query:any?)->{Candidate},
Labels:(self:WeaponLibrary,query:any?)->{string},
Find:(self:WeaponLibrary,label:string)->Candidate?,
IsMelee:(self:WeaponLibrary,name:string)->boolean,
Activate:(self:WeaponLibrary,candidate:Candidate,target:any?)->boolean,
Best:(self:WeaponLibrary,query:any?)->Candidate?,
Invalidate:(self:WeaponLibrary)->(),
SetAllowEquip:(self:WeaponLibrary,allowed:boolean)->(),
Swing:(self:WeaponLibrary)->boolean,
RegisterGameSource:(self:WeaponLibrary,source:GameSource?)->(),
PressButton:(self:WeaponLibrary,button:GuiButton)->boolean,
Destroy:(self:WeaponLibrary)->()
}
local Module={
Name="Weapons",
PlaceId=0,
Events={}::{[string]:any},
Initialized=false,
}
local WEAPON_WORDS:{string}={
"sword",
"katana",
"dagger",
"blade",
"knife",
"axe",
"mace",
"spear",
"scythe",
"sabre",
"saber",
"machete",
"hammer",
"club",
"bat",
"staff",
"pickaxe",
"fist",
"punch",
"attack",
"swing",
"hit",
"melee",
"weapon",
"combat",
}
local MELEE_WORDS:{string}={
"sword",
"katana",
"dagger",
"blade",
"knife",
"axe",
"mace",
"spear",
"scythe",
"sickle",
"sabre",
"saber",
"rapier",
"cutlass",
"machete",
"glaive",
"halberd",
"hammer",
"club",
"baton",
"bat",
"pickaxe",
"fist",
"knuckle",
}
local RANGED_WORDS:{string}={
"bow",
"crossbow",
"arrow",
"gun",
"rifle",
"pistol",
"revolver",
"shotgun",
"sniper",
"launcher",
"rocket",
"grenade",
"snowball",
"projectile",
"throw",
"wand",
"staff",
"gauntlet",
"fireball",
}
local RIG_WORDS:{string}={
"hand",
"arm",
"leg",
"foot",
"torso",
"head",
"upper",
"lower",
"root",
"limb",
"joint",
"motor",
"weld",
"attachment",
"rig",
"bone",
"hitbox",
"collider",
"humanoid",
"camera",

"idle",
"walk",
"run",
"sprint",
"sneak",
"crouch",
"jump",
"fall",
"land",
"swim",
"pose",
"track",
"sequence",
}
local NOT_WEAPON_WORDS:{string}={
"emote",
"animation",
"anim",
"dance",
"cosmetic",
"cape",
"skin",
